# 2.1.326 ◆◆◆◆

[Back to the changelog](../README.md#changelog)

- New terminal renderer
  - 🔴 Added a terminal renderer for inline and fullscreen output, enabled by `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or the `tengu_idempotent_willow` flag (off by default) in a terminal with screen reader mode off. It measures how wide the terminal draws characters such as emoji and accented letters where it can, or uses widths for known terminals when it can't measure.
- @-mentioned files
  - 🔴 Added a retry in the main conversation when a request is too long after an @-mentioned file was attached in full despite its size. Claude Code withdraws that turn's over-limit whole-file attachments and sends the request again, as it already did for PDFs.
- MCP connections and reconnects
  - 🔴 Expanded automatic MCP reconnection to eligible WebSocket and Claude.ai connector servers without starting a new sign-in, and removed the old `tengu_abundant_shore` gate (off by default). Tool search waits for a reconnect only when `tengu_ticklish_fern` is on (off by default).
- Bash permission checks
  - 🔴 Denied a Bash command flagged as a dangerous removal when one of your deny rules also covers it, instead of only asking for approval.
  - 🟢 Changed Bash commands too long for the permission check to read in full: a patterned `Bash`, `Read` or `Edit` deny rule now makes the check refuse the command with an explanation. Without one of those rules, a command that hasn't already been refused needs approval.
  - 🟢 Stopped treating a Bash command as read-only when it uses a value assigned earlier in the same command or runs a loop whose passes differ, so it goes through the normal permission checks, when the `bot_protected_tengu_stateful_milner` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Stopped a write deny rule on a `cp` command's source file from blocking the copy when the command uses only plain paths and the options `-p`, `-f`, `-i`, `-n` or `-v`; such sources are now checked as reads.
- Git commands Claude Code runs for itself
  - 🔴 Switched off the filter drivers named in a repository's own git config, as worktree creation already did, for the `git status` and other git commands Claude Code runs for its own checks, so a repository's config can't make those checks run its filter programs; Claude Code skips the command when the folder isn't a usable repository, sits outside its work tree, or has a filter it can't switch off. On by default through the `tengu_sifted_pebble` flag.
- Safe mode
  - 🔴 Ran hooks from plugins that managed settings enable in safe mode (`--safe-mode`) and in `--bare` mode; before, plugin hooks were off in both modes, and `--bare` ran only hooks the server supplied.
- Artifacts
  - 🔴 Changed local Artifact live watching to default on when the service sends no setting. `tengu_slate_lantern` and `tengu_slate_lantern_ember` now default on, and the new `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_LIVE_WATCH` variable overrides the first. It applies to sessions where Artifacts are available.
  - 🔴 Changed Artifact comments to default on when the service sends no setting: the `tengu_teal_corbel` and `tengu_onyx_sluice` flags now default on, so the Artifact tool can read and handle comments unless `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENTS` turns them off.
  - 🔴 Changed automatic replies to Artifact comments to default on when the service sends no setting (`tengu_sorrel_trellis` now defaults on, and `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_COMMENTS_AUTOREACT` still overrides it), and made them also require the Artifact tool to be available.
  - 🟢 Changed the Artifact publish result to say that an owner-only Artifact can also be opened by people with folder access when the folder is shared or its audience is unknown.
- Agent view and background sessions
  - 🔴 Let `claude respawn` restart a background session that was stopped before its first response from the copy of the conversation it was moved from, kept in the session's folder, instead of refusing for lack of a transcript; when that copy can't be read right now, the refusal adds that trying again may work.
  - 🟢 Fixed stopping or removing a background session also ending a different background session's process whose session ID merely starts with the same short ID, such as a session that resumed the first one's conversation.
  - 🟢 Ended a session you moved to the background with ← as soon as you quit agent view if you hadn't typed anything into it, as long as it's still unnamed and has no task, worktree, queued prompt or Remote Control link; such sessions used to wait out the 5-minute idle grace.
- `subagentStatusLine` setting
  - 🔴 Brought back the `subagentStatusLine` setting that 2.1.323 removed: a custom status line for each subagent row in the agent panel works again, also when a plugin's settings provide it, with a notice (prefixed with `Managed settings:` or the plugin's name when one of those set it) that it's deprecated in favor of mods and will stop working in a future release; plugin validation flags it too.
- Background commands and tasks
  - 🔴 Made `TaskStop` also stop processes left running by a stopped agent's earlier commands or by agents it started, and report how many it stopped. This runs when `tengu_snug_crab` is on (off by default).
  - 🟢 Added the owning subagent's name to `/tasks` rows for background commands and monitors and to shell details.
- Subagents and forks
  - 🔴 Added cleanup of processes left running by an agent's Bash commands when that agent is stopped: Claude Code sends them SIGTERM and, 1.5 seconds later, SIGKILL, behind the `tengu_snug_crab` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🟢 Showed each agent's outcome in the Agent tool's list of agents launched together, such as "Failed", "Denied", "Not run", "Interrupted" or "No result recorded", where every finished agent used to read "Done".
  - 🟢 Showed finished agents in the agent panel as “waiting on …” while their background commands, monitors or subagents are still running, with the start time of the earliest work they're waiting on when it's known.
- Plan mode and plan approval
  - 🔴 Kept the main conversation's plan file and plan mode when approving a subagent's plan.
  - 🟢 Fixed local `/plan <description>` to send the description to Claude even when plan mode is already on.
- Resuming sessions
  - 🔴 Fixed session lists losing a session's custom title, tag, summary or pull-request link when its transcript file shrank, or grew past 64 KB, after being listed; Claude Code now checks the file's current size and reads its end again.
  - 🔴 Fixed `--resume-session-at` failing with "No message found with message.uuid of …" when the named message was dropped while loading the session; it now resumes at the last kept message before it.
- Model choice and `/model`
  - 🔴 Kept the model a session actually uses when Claude Code relaunches itself, for example into fullscreen or agent view, if the session was on the default model and the default model chosen for your account differs from Claude Code's own; it used to pass `default`. First-party API only.
  - 🟢 Cleared your saved model pick on the server when you set the model back to the default with a model list served by Anthropic, putting it back locally if the server write fails; before, choosing the default left the server's saved pick in place.
  - 🟢 Changed `/model default` and eligible local model names to switch without opening a separate panel when no hook, confirmation, or session warning needs one.
- Usage and cost accounting
  - 🔴 Estimated output tokens from streamed text, tool input and thinking when a response cuts off before reporting their count. Usage and cost can include those tokens instead of counting only what the provider reported; When the API returns an error, Claude Code keeps the reported counts; it can still estimate after a disconnect, timeout or abort.
- Settings files
  - 🔴 Kept applying the last good copy of a settings file that becomes unreadable mid-session (a `Settings file could not be read` error), limited to what restricts: added directories, allowed domains and paths, sandbox exceptions, enabled plugins and other loosening values are dropped, behind the `bot_protected_tengu_foamy_pebble` flag, which is on by default.
- Terminal rendering
  - 🔴 Removed the opt-in scroll-region renderer that kept the prompt in fixed rows at the bottom of the terminal while the transcript scrolled into the terminal's own scrollback; `CLAUDE_CODE_DECSTBM` and the `tengu_marlin_porch` flag (off by default) that turned it on no longer do anything.
  - 🟢 Fixed leftover terminal input not being drained at exit once the screen had been torn down, which could leave mouse escape codes typed into the shell.
  - 🟢 Fixed the Ctrl+Z message and the shell prompt printing over Claude Code's prompt box outside fullscreen: the cursor now moves below the interface before Claude Code suspends.
- Hidden and invisible characters
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_SHOW_HIDDEN_CHARACTERS`, which shows control characters and other invisible characters in Claude Code's terminal output as visible markers such as `␀` or a `<U+…>` code; it's off unless set, or unless the `tengu_bubbly_gadget` flag turns it on.
- Themes
  - 🔴 Fixed custom theme loading trying to read `.json` entries in the themes folder that aren't regular files, such as a named pipe, which could leave it waiting; they're now skipped.
  - 🟢 Changed the default theme to follow the terminal (`auto`) instead of dark until onboarding is first completed, when no theme is set.
- Prompt footer
  - 🔴 Kept `Ctrl+C` from clearing a nonempty terminal draft while a footer item is selected.
- 🔴 Sped up shell snapshots for bash 5.1 and newer outside macOS by capturing all shell functions in one pass, with `DEBUG` traps switched off, when the `tengu_gentle_pumice` flag is on, as it is by default; older bash, and function names starting with `-` or `+`, fall back to the per-function loop.
- Sign-in checks for remote agents and cross-machine messages
  - 🔴 Stopped remote agents and remote workflow runs from falling back to an unused claude.ai login when the current session signs in another way, such as an API key or workload identity. The launch is also refused if Claude Code can't use the sign-in the session started with.
  - 🟢 Stopped messages to Claude sessions on other machines from falling back to a claude.ai login the sending session isn't using. The send fails with an `auth:` error unless a named profile supplies the sign-in or a live Remote Control bridge is signed into the same organization. Messages on this machine still work.
- Agent teams
  - 🔴 Applied an agent definition's `effort` to in-process teammates when `CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS` or `--agent-teams` enables Agent Teams and the `tengu_amber_flint` flag is on, as it is by default.
- Plugin loading and `plugin.json`
  - 🔴 Fixed `--agent`, or a resumed session's agent, not being found when it comes from an enabled plugin that hadn't loaded yet, and a plugin's `agent` setting being missed at startup: Claude Code now loads those plugins and rereads agent definitions first.
  - 🟢 Loaded a plugin whose `plugin.json` has a malformed `$schema`, `displayName`, `description`, `author`, `homepage`, `repository`, `license` or `keywords` value, ignoring that field with a warning that `/plugin` lists ("… in plugin.json is malformed and ignored. Earlier versions of Claude Code refused this plugin"), when the `tengu_jaunty_hare` flag is on, as it is by default.
- MCP tool schemas and calls
  - 🔴 Changed MCP schema recovery to retry without `$schema` before falling back to an empty input schema, and name that retry in the warning. The retry needs `tengu_playful_ullman` (off by default) and the existing `tengu_calm_crescent` recovery flag (on by default).
  - 🟢 Fixed MCP prompts and tools being matched to the wrong server when two servers have nearly identical names, which could make one server's prompts disappear; Claude Code now matches on the server's own name when the tool or prompt carries it.
  - 🟢 Added random characters to saved MCP result filenames so concurrent replies from one tool don't share a filename just because they arrived in the same millisecond.
- Connector file references and uploads
  - 🔴 Expanded local file inputs for MCP tools that mark arguments with `x-mcp-file`. With `tengu_steady_quail` on (off by default), these tools can take lists of paths, files nested inside other inputs, and a `local_file_path` object where the schema doesn't take a plain path.
- Screen reader mode
  - 🔴 Stopped stray typing from choosing an option in screen-reader mode's typed-number prompt for confirmation and action menus such as `Remove server?`, plugin disable or uninstall, and permission-rule editing: a letter clears the entry, keys in the same quick burst are ignored, and the next Enter answers `Nothing was chosen. Enter a number between 1 and N.`
  - 🟢 Fixed screen-reader mode's redraws when Claude Code's output ends above the bottom of the terminal, such as after a tall list closes: they now count the blank rows below the last line written when moving the cursor, so old text isn't left behind; Windows Terminal is unchanged.
- 🔴 Skipped individual checks for missing project instruction files and `.claude/rules` paths when Claude Code can check their folder together; `tengu_modular_reddy` is on by default, and `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CLAUDE_MDS` still turns instruction loading off.
- Auto mode
  - 🟡 (2.1.329) Added a note to auto mode denials of destructive Bash and PowerShell commands that were judged without the repository's git status: Claude is told git status comes with its next three requests, which may clear the block, and that it may send the command once more if git status shows nothing it would lose.
  - 🟢 Counted more Bash and PowerShell allow rules as dangerous, the kind auto mode drops: rules whose wildcard follows only shell wrappers or interpreters (such as `env`, `sudo`, `nohup` or `timeout 30`, including chains of them and `NAME=value` prefixes), a `-c` style option, an absolute, home-folder or `..` path, or part of a wrapper or interpreter's name, as in `Bash(pyth*)`.
- Slash command descriptions and `/help`
  - 🟢 Added `/help <command>` for available slash commands: it prints usage, the description and any aliases, with a link to the commands docs for built-in commands.
  - 🟢 Changed `/<command> --help` and `/<command> -h` for enabled built-in commands at an idle prompt to show help instead of running the command. `/config`, `/output-style` and `/effort` handle their own help, so `/help` for one of them passes it `--help`.
- 🟢 Kept a session's conversation in its transcript when saving the session's API request replay records fails, such as when memory runs short: those records are dropped with a logged error, and tombstone removal is skipped after such a failure.
- Worktrees
  - 🟢 Changed `ExitWorktree` to refuse removing a worktree while a background task or subagent may still be working in it, naming the task, unless `discard_changes` is set; its description also says the branch stays when a hook made the worktree or git refuses to delete it.
- `/resume` picker
  - 🟢 Fixed `/resume` search missing older sessions: the picker now checks older pages in groups of 50 and shows when it's still searching.
  - 🟢 Fixed `/resume` treating a typed number as a menu choice instead of starting a search.
- Output-token limits
  - 🟢 Added a lower `max_tokens` retry for eligible HTTP 400, 402, 413, 422 and 429 responses. Claude Code tries a lower limit named in the error if it's at least 4,096, reuses one learned earlier, or tries 32,000 when the model's limit is unknown and `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` isn't set to a positive value. If the retry starts a reply, it keeps the limit for that model until `/clear`, compaction, resume or a provider change.
- Folder trust and added folders
  - 🟢 Added a trust prompt for an untrusted folder added with `--add-dir` or `/add-dir` in a local terminal session; declining at startup tells you to run Claude Code again without that `--add-dir`. The `bot_protected_tengu_slate_orchard` flag is on by default, and the prompt is skipped when `CLAUDE_CODE_SANDBOXED` is set.
  - 🟢 Stopped `claude --bg` from starting a new background chat with an untrusted `--add-dir` folder when `bot_protected_tengu_slate_orchard` is on (the default) and `CLAUDE_CODE_SANDBOXED` isn't set. It names the folder and, if known, its repository, explains how to trust it, and exits with status 1.
  - 🟢 Fixed `/add-dir` saying a folder was saved to local settings when the settings write failed; it now says the change couldn't be saved, may stop applying after a restart, and which file to check.
  - 🟢 Changed the workspace trust prompt to list project configuration files, label the ones that run commands or set environment variables, and name `.mcp.json` when it adds servers.
- Bash sandbox
  - 🟢 Added a check after sandboxed commands that sets aside new links, as well as ref files that point outside `refs/`, in the git folders Claude Code could inspect before the command. Claude Code warns you when it sets items aside and tells Claude to leave them there. The `bot_protected_tengu_robust_island` flag is on by default.
  - 🟢 Held the sandbox at its earlier strictness when `EnterWorktree` or `ExitWorktree` moves the session: it stays on and required if it was, keeps strict mode, and stops auto-allowing Bash unless the new folder's settings turn the sandbox on, until `/cd` moves the session or `/sandbox` changes its settings; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_partitioned_river` flag, on by default.
  - 🟢 Added sandbox write protection for `~/.ssh` and `~/.claude/remote` on macOS, Linux and WSL. Broader grants no longer open them, and only user or managed settings that name `~/.ssh` can open that folder. It runs when `tengu_placid_lantern` is on (the default) and subprocess environment scrub mode is off.
  - 🟢 Changed sandboxed Bash on Linux and WSL to remove the empty write-denied placeholders after the sandbox has ended. Claude Code ends a built-in sandbox that's still running after its command exits and leaves the placeholders in place if it can't watch the sandbox end. It waits without killing the sandbox when you've set a custom `bwrapPath`.
- Permission checks that fail with an error
  - 🟢 Stopped bypass-permissions mode from approving a tool call whose own permission check threw an error, which allow rules already couldn't; Claude Code asks instead, and the prompt says the check failed and needs approval.
- Protection of Claude Code's sign-in files
  - 🟢 Refused writes by Claude's file tools to Claude Code's sign-in files, the token file `.credentials.json`, the device-key file `.device-keys.json` and the `.storage-write.lock` file it locks while saving tokens, including writes through a hard link to one of them or a Windows 8.3 short name.
- Permission prompts
  - 🟢 Added a `Waiting for permission…` line in the terminal when a permission prompt is parked and isn't shown elsewhere on the screen.
  - 🟢 Stopped sending text from a hidden accept or reject feedback field in tool-permission dialogs.
- Bundled `/claude-api` skill
  - 🟢 Changed the bundled `/claude-api` skill to use Managed Agents by default for agents, including assistants and bots that choose their own tool calls. It explains the beta, runtime charges and saved session history, and uses the Messages API instead when you want to host the loop, your project already uses it, or platform or compliance requirements rule out Managed Agents.
- Compaction
  - 🟢 Changed compaction to give the summarizer the earlier Claude reply that opens a kept turn when it fits and applies, on the first non-reactive attempt. When compaction adds messages to summarize, it also tells the summarizer the request came from the system rather than from you, without needing the old off-by-default flags.
  - 🟢 Stopped a partial compaction, which summarizes from or up to a chosen message, when it's cancelled partway, keeping the conversation and the record of files Claude had read as they were instead of finishing.
  - 🟢 Replaced `/autocompact`'s step-by-step picker with a slider and clickable stops in fullscreen; a valid `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW` override still makes the setting read-only.
  - 🟢 Refreshed the git status Claude gets at compaction and dropped that snapshot when the next turn comes more than 5 minutes later or in another folder; behind the new `tengu_tidy_acorn` flag, which is on by default.
- Rewind
  - 🟢 Changed the rewind menu to say "Code changes unknown" instead of "No code changes" for a turn where a shell command may have changed files that file history couldn't list.
  - 🟢 Refused to restore a file during `/rewind` when the sandbox is on and the file sits behind a symbolic link at or under a path the sandbox protects, counting it among the skipped paths, when the `tengu_polished_hammock` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Fixed `/rewind` after two successive `/branch` calls to include files whose backup copy from the first branch was still running.
  - 🟢 Enabled Escape to cancel a `/rewind` summary in either direction in local sessions where compaction is available.
- Unavailable slash commands
  - 🟢 Explained why some built-in slash commands aren't available in local interactive sessions: your provider doesn't offer them, an environment setting such as `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` turns them off, or they need a Claude subscription. The message points to the relevant docs or sign-in option when it can.
- `/effort` and effort levels
  - 🟢 Made `/effort <level>` skip the slider when the choice doesn't need confirmation, so it doesn't steal focus from the prompt; switches that could invalidate a warm prompt cache still open the slider.
  - 🟢 Made the `/effort` slider's levels and Ultracode toggle clickable in fullscreen mode.
- Usage limits and automatic continuation
  - 🟢 Cancelled a pending usage-limit auto-resume when the account or organization changes. If Claude Code can't confirm the login when the continuation would fire, it waits up to one minute, then cancels. This applies when `autoContinueAtUsageLimit` allows continuation and `tengu_encapsulated_crab` and `tengu_marble_heron` are on, as they are by default.
  - 🟢 Kept the usage-limit menu from opening over an active typing burst and let it open later without erasing the draft or clearing the IDE selection.
- `/config` settings
  - 🟢 Changed `/config`'s Spinner tips and Reduce motion toggles to save to your user settings, so they apply in every project, instead of this project's local settings; a local entry is kept only when project settings would override the choice or user settings can't be written.
  - 🟢 Turned `/scroll-speed` into a slider with labeled stops, a lines-per-notch readout, and Home and End shortcuts; the available stops depend on the terminal's scroll handling.
- Settings file errors and limits
  - 🟢 Showed where a settings file stops being valid JSON, adding `(line N, column M)` of the first syntax error to its settings error.
  - 🟢 Added a pinned warning when Claude Code starts skipping a settings file because of a syntax or validation error during a local session. It names the file, points to `claude doctor` and clears once the file loads again.
- Prompt input and completions
  - 🟢 Fixed prompt recall after clearing a draft with Escape twice to bring pasted text and images back with it.
  - 🟢 Fixed fullscreen prompts to stay expanded when typing takes them past 10,000 characters.
  - 🟢 Fixed Shift+Enter or Option+Enter pressed while a paste was still being read being held back and sent as Enter; they now add a new line.
  - 🟢 Quoted or escaped file paths completed with Tab in `!` shell mode when the shell is bash, so names with spaces, quotes, `$` or brackets go in safely, and let Tab complete paths after a command there.
  - 🟢 Fixed terminal prompt Undo after deleting a line or word by giving the deletion its own undo step.
  - 🟢 Fixed fullscreen prompt history so pressing Ctrl+R again moves the selection to the next older match.
- Pasted images
  - 🟢 Fixed a slow clipboard image paste to insert the image before text you keep typing at the paste point, instead of putting it after that new text.
- Fullscreen and flicker-free rendering
  - 🟢 Improved fullscreen scroll position as rows above the viewport are measured, and adjusted the position within a row when a terminal resize wraps it differently.
  - 🟢 Added a `Waiting for your answer` label to the fullscreen jump pill when a question or permission prompt is waiting below.
- Clickable links
  - 🟢 Changed local terminal replies to make web addresses clickable without requiring a known terminal. By default this still skips an empty `TERM` on Linux, `TERM=dumb`, GNU screen and terminals that show unrecognized escape sequences. Attached sessions follow the attaching client's capabilities; `FORCE_HYPERLINK=0` turns local links off.
- Character width measurement
  - 🟢 Fixed text being drawn out of place after characters that terminals draw at a different width than Claude Code expects, such as some emoji: a changed line holding one is now redrawn whole, and the terminal's line wrapping is turned off around such a character at the right edge.
- Keybindings
  - 🟢 Fixed a rebound `chat:submit` shortcut doing nothing when the slash-command or `@` file suggestions are open.
  - 🟢 Read macOS Option-key characters as Alt shortcuts whenever the terminal is iTerm2 or Apple Terminal, including when Claude Code runs on another system over SSH.
- Esc and Ctrl+C
  - 🟢 Fixed Escape over an unsent draft at the idle prompt to leave a pending `/loop` wakeup alone.
- Questions Claude asks you
  - 🟢 Made the terminal question dialog scroll within the available rows so a long question doesn't push the choices past the screen.
- Markdown rendering
  - 🟢 Fixed wide tables and code blocks breaking apart in plan-mode boxes and in the Agent tool's Prompt and Response sections, by laying out their markdown for the width left inside the border or indent instead of the full terminal width.
  - 🟢 Changed how wide Markdown tables fit the terminal: only the widest columns are narrowed, instead of every column shrinking in proportion, and row-by-row tables leave room for the margin and each field's label.
  - 🟢 Fixed Markdown bold and combined bold-italic formatting that could show its stars in Claude's replies when it touches Chinese, Japanese or Korean text or punctuation.
- Shell command output
  - 🟢 Changed a `!` command typed at the prompt to show its output and timer after its first second, instead of waiting at least two seconds.
- Transcript display
  - 🟢 Showed “No result recorded” for a tool call with no result after a newer reply or user prompt appears, or after its subagent ends. Calls still in progress keep their running status.
- `/copy` and `/export`
  - 🟢 Made `/copy` and the clipboard option of `/export` say whether the text went to the clipboard, the tmux buffer or the terminal's clipboard, or wasn't copied, and point to the saved file or `/export` to a file when pasting may fail.
- `/btw`
  - 🟢 Made long `/btw` answers fit the available space and respond to Page Up, Page Down, Home and End.
- Goals
  - 🟢 Fixed a `/goal` that was cleared or replaced while its check waited on running background work coming back as a Stop hook check when that turn ended.
- 🟢 Fixed a hang in the bundled glob matcher on some malformed glob patterns, which settings, plugin marketplace entries and Grep file filters go through.
- 🟢 Changed the memory folder scan on macOS and Linux to walk links to other folders itself, visiting each real folder once so a link loop can't repeat it, and skipping links when a folder above can't be read.
- Failed turns and API errors
  - 🟢 Recognized HTTP 400 replies saying the request size exceeds a megabyte limit, plain or wrapped in a JSON `FAILED_PRECONDITION` error such as Vertex AI sends, as request-too-large errors, so Claude Code's recovery for oversized requests handles them instead of a generic API error.
  - 🟢 Suggested a next step in the empty prompt after a turn ends in an error: "Resend your message to try again" for temporary failures, "Once fixed, resend your message" for setup problems, a model that needs switching or a full context, and "Run /rewind to go back" when the message itself has to change.
- `claude -p` output
  - 🟢 Added a dim line on terminal stderr for `claude -p` API retries once the wait reaches or is expected to reach five seconds. It's shown once per attempt with the error and wait time, for text output or when JSON is redirected; `stream-json` doesn't get the line.
  - 🟢 Printed headless execution errors to the terminal on stderr when stdout is redirected and stderr is a terminal.
- Command-line options
  - 🟢 Added a hint when a prompt was taken as the last value of a list option such as `--mcp-config`, `--allowedTools` or `--add-dir`: startup says the text was read as that option's value and to put the prompt before the option or after `--`, and `Error: Invalid MCP configuration` adds the same hint.
  - 🟢 Fixed `claude --help` piped to a pager, as in `claude --help | less`, taking keyboard input before the pager could read it.
- `/doctor` and `claude doctor`
  - 🟢 Changed `/doctor` to report what it finds as it goes: it now uses fewer tool calls, scans transcripts once in a single script, and gives early findings in plain words after each set of results, before the full report.
- Unused `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` notices
  - 🟢 Added an “Unused key” row to `/status` and `claude auth status` when a local Claude sign-in leaves `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` unused and you can choose the key in `/config`.
- Sign-in screens and messages
  - 🟢 Improved the sign-in code paste prompt: instead of "Invalid code", it now says when you pasted a localhost address, a web or sign-in link, or a short verification code instead of the code, or a code missing its `#` part, and the `/login` screen lets you paste again instead of stopping on an error.
- IDE connection and `/ide`
  - 🟢 Fixed the local terminal's IDE connection so it stops grabbing the IDE back when another Claude Code session takes over and shows a warning pointing to `/ide` to switch back.
- Teleport
  - 🟢 Fixed `/teleport` session lists using a fixed-height panel that could hide a choice in a short terminal.
- Attachments in cloud and Remote Control sessions
  - 🟢 Fixed eligible resent attachments in cloud and Remote Control sessions so they don't overwrite files edited since the last download. Claude can also be told that the expected path holds a different file on the attachment paths that use `tengu_tidy_shelf`, which now defaults to `tell`.
- Stop from Remote Control and the web
  - 🟢 Changed Stop from Remote Control on a terminal session to stop background tasks too, unless the request asks to keep them running. The transcript lists which tasks stopped, and Claude is told to leave them stopped unless you ask to restart them.
  - 🟢 Fixed a cloud worker replaying an interrupted turn after the user pressed Stop during restart: the worker now saves that decision for the next worker unless `tengu_settled_wren_killswitch` is on (off by default).
- Remote Control
  - 🟢 Changed Remote Control permission requests shown on claude.ai and the mobile app to describe a non-shell tool call with the tool's own one-line summary when it has one, and to strip hidden characters from question headers; the `tengu_bridge_requires_action_details` flag is on by default.
  - 🟢 Reported terminal-only permission waits to Remote Control as waiting for an answer, including teammate prompts and prompts that couldn't reach a remote approval surface.
- Code Mode
  - 🟢 Let Code Mode (`CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_CODE_MODE`) turn on when agent teams are on; agent teams used to keep it off.
- `/plugin` screens and plugin notices
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` Discover and Browse to size their lists to the terminal and support Home, End, paging, wheel scrolling and clicks, with action hints that follow the selected row.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` Installed to support Home, End, paging and clicks, count hidden rows, and follow the selected item when an action reorders the list.
  - 🟢 Fixed `/plugin` Discover, Browse and Installed searches to accept text that arrives in multi-character key events, including from IMEs, dictation and fast typing.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` uninstall for a plugin enabled in team settings to show separate choices to disable it for yourself, uninstall it for everyone, or cancel, replacing the `y` and `u` shortcuts.
- Skills
  - 🟢 Fixed skill and command folders created after a session started going unwatched: the change watcher now looks for new folders with files in them as the session goes on and starts watching them.
  - 🟢 Changed `/skills` so pressing Ctrl+C or Ctrl+D twice saves the changes and closes the panel instead of quitting Claude Code.
  - 🟢 Changed interactive `/update-config` to list the `settings.json` keys with short descriptions and give Claude a `schema/<key>.json` file to look up each key. Without a place to read those files, the skill still includes the full schema in its prompt.
- Hooks
  - 🟢 Kept an earlier PreToolUse hook's `updatedInput` when a later hook allows or asks about the same call without rewriting it. For calls whose input is already recorded, the carried-forward rewrite is skipped when `tengu_foamy_pelican` is off, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Fixed a hook that answers `"decision": "block"` with a `hookSpecificOutput` naming a different event, other than a PermissionRequest hook: the block now applies and that output is ignored with a warning, instead of the hook failing with an incorrect event name error.
  - 🟢 Changed how Stop hook results appear: a blocked stop shows "Stop hook stopped Claude" with the hook's reason under it, a failing Stop hook shows "Stop hook failed" or "Stop hook reported a problem" with its message, and extra context shows as "Stop hook says: …" instead of "Stop hook feedback: …".
  - 🟢 Showed up to 10,000 characters of hook output in verbose and transcript views and used the exception line in compact views when a traceback's first line only identifies its location.
- `/mcp` and MCP dialogs
  - 🟢 Let Esc, or Ctrl+C twice, close the `/mcp` screen while a server connects or reconnects; the connection carries on in the background, and Claude Code says so with `/mcp to check`.
  - 🟢 Fixed enabling a disabled MCP server in `/mcp` to show progress and temporarily hide the action menu, so pressing Enter again can’t turn the server back off.
- Saving permission rules
  - 🟢 Added a warning when a permission rule or folder you chose to keep couldn't be saved to its settings file: it names the file, says the change may stop applying when settings change or Claude Code restarts, and asks you to check that the file is valid JSON and writable.
- `/permissions` dialog
  - 🟢 Made `/permissions` search accept text from input methods, dictation, and key events carrying more than one character.
  - 🟢 Stopped stray letters from changing the answer in `/permissions` when confirming a rule deletion, a workspace directory removal, or a replacement of the built-in auto mode environment.
- PowerShell tool
  - 🟢 Changed the PowerShell tool to refuse a function definition that uses a built-in alias for a destructive command, and explain why the function needs a different name. `bot_protected_tengu_dynamic_pike` is on by default wherever the tool is enabled; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_POWERSHELL_ALIAS_SHADOW_CHECK` turns the check off.
- Device, process and special file paths
  - 🟢 Made Claude Code's file search refuse a pipe or device, a path that leads to a device or process file, or a path whose opened target isn't what it names now (such as a link into another process's view of the files or to a deleted file), asking for the file's or folder's own path without links.
- `/privacy-settings` and training consent
  - 🟢 Changed the local account-settings form of `/privacy-settings` to recheck which account is signed in before saving and before reporting the result, and to stop if the account changed or can't be confirmed; this applies when `tengu_playful_puzzle` and `tengu_generic_flask` are both off, as they are by default.
- `claude purge`
  - 🟢 Fixed interactive `claude purge` to show the project picker and delete confirmation on stderr when stdout is redirected. Purge still needs terminal input and at least one terminal output stream for its prompts.
- 🟢 Fixed terminal Web Search going quiet while it retries: the tool now shows retry progress while it waits on another search request.
- 🟢 Let background task details, the model picker, and keyboard help use nearly the full terminal height in fullscreen instead of cutting them off at half the screen.
- 🟢 Fixed syntax-highlighted file previews using the wrong default text color when Claude Code's theme and the terminal background differ: text without a special syntax color now uses the terminal's default foreground where the terminal supplies the background.
- 🟢 Fixed syntax-highlighted diffs using the wrong default text color when Claude Code's theme and the terminal background differ: unstyled text on the terminal's own background now uses the terminal's default foreground.
- 🟢 Fixed the prompt pinned at the top of the fullscreen conversation showing an older question when the scroll position moves past the start of the last rendered row.

<details>
<summary>1,220 more visible changes</summary>

- PowerShell tool
  - 🔴 Checked PowerShell deny and ask rules against each command a second time with empty arguments, `--%` stop-parsing tokens and here-strings set aside, so those rules hold for people who rely on them.
  - 🔴 Told the model in the PowerShell tool's description to give helper functions a new Verb-Noun name, because a built-in alias such as `rd`, `rm` or `del` runs instead of a function with the same name.
  - 🔴 Stopped PowerShell permission prompts from offering an always-allow rule for `ConvertFrom-Csv`, `Import-Csv` and `ipcsv` commands.
  - 🔴 Changed the PowerShell process that Claude Code runs to parse a command for its permission check to use only PowerShell's own modules and path (`$env:PSModulePath=$PSHOME+'/Modules';$env:PATH=$PSHOME`), so modules or programs on the user's path can't change the check; behind the new `bot_protected_tengu_mellow_reef` flag, which is on by default.
  - 🟢 Added a PowerShell tool check for commands that define a function with the same name as an existing alias, which PowerShell would run instead, behind the new `bot_protected_tengu_dynamic_pike` flag, which is on by default; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_POWERSHELL_ALIAS_SHADOW_CHECK` turns it off.
  - 🟢 Asked before running PowerShell commands that Claude Code wouldn't parse because they mention `using module`, `using assembly`, `#requires` or `Import-DscResource`, with no always-allow choice, and refused them outright when they use Unicode escapes or line continuations, mention `Remove-Item` or `cmd`, or a content deny rule is set for PowerShell, Read, Edit or Write; when the `tengu_unopened_parcel` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Refused PowerShell commands with a null character in a path, saying file names can't contain one and that a value that isn't a path can go in a named parameter such as `-Value` or `-Pattern`.
  - 🟢 Stopped the PowerShell tool from letting PowerShell parse commands that mention `using module`, `using assembly`, `#requires` or `Import-DscResource`, even split with backticks or quotes, since reading them can load code; Claude Code then says it couldn't fully check your permission rules. Behind the new `tengu_unopened_parcel` flag, which is on by default.
- Bash sandbox
  - 🔴 Told Claude, once per agent, when Claude Code has renamed entries in a `.git` folder so that git outside the sandbox doesn't follow them, explaining the new names and that an ignore list, hook or branch among them may no longer apply. (behind `tengu_violin_rosin`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Kept Git marker names such as `commondir` and `.lock` files on the sandbox's after-command cleanup list when the sandbox config is rebuilt while a sandboxed command is still running on Linux or WSL, so a file found there afterwards is moved aside instead of deleted; behind the `tengu_steady_wren` flag, on by default.
  - 🔴 Showed a note under "Switched to worktree" when the sandbox stays on for the session even though the worktree's settings don't turn it on.
  - 🔴 Changed sandbox read-deny and protected credential paths so that a glob that can't be fully parsed, in the whole pattern or in any one folder level of it, covers everything under the folder before its first wildcard; before, that fallback applied only when compiling the whole pattern failed.
  - 🟢 Added a search of Git folders after a sandboxed command that wrote outside its own scratch space, looking for new links and for ref files that point outside `refs/`, which the cleanup moves aside or removes; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_robust_island` flag, on by default.
  - 🟢 Changed the sandbox on Linux and WSL to create some missing added working folders before each sandboxed command, remove the empty ones it made when Claude Code exits, and warn when one can't be made, behind the `bot_protected_tengu_gleaming_bonbon` flag, which is on by default.
  - 🟢 Created missing added working folders, such as ones from `--add-dir`, before sandboxed commands run on Linux and WSL, so the sandbox can block writes to their `.claude` files; a folder that can't be made stays unwritable to sandboxed commands and a warning is logged; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_gleaming_bonbon` flag, on by default.
  - 🟢 Added sandbox guidance on Linux and WSL, when sandbox network rules are set, explaining that each sandboxed command gets its own private network, so a server it starts can't be reached by later commands or your browser, and telling Claude to start and check a test server in one command.
  - 🟢 Kept the sandbox on through `EnterWorktree` and `ExitWorktree` moves into a folder whose settings don't turn it on, and added a note to the tool result saying so and, where it applies, that `/sandbox` turns it off.
  - 🟢 Ended the hold that keeps the sandbox at its earlier settings during a working-directory move once a `cd` command's move finishes, so the new directory's settings apply; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_partitioned_river` flag, which is on by default.
  - 🟢 Waited up to half a second at exit for sandboxed shell commands to finish their after-command cleanup.
  - 🟢 Added `~/.claude/child-logs` to the folders sandboxed commands can't write.
  - 🟢 Denied sandboxed commands writes to the paths that links in Claude Code's `dev-mods` folder lead to, so a command can't change the code of a mod being developed, on macOS, Linux and WSL behind the `bot_protected_tengu_enchanted_gem` flag, which is on by default.
  - 🟢 Re-read links under `~/.claude/dev-mods` before each sandboxed command on macOS, Linux and WSL and denied writes to where new links lead, instead of only when the sandbox config is built; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_enchanted_gem` flag, on by default.
  - 🟢 Stopped sandbox write grants for `/dev/stdout` and `/dev/stderr` from also opening the terminal or file they point to.
- Sandbox-approved commands when the sandbox is gone
  - 🔴 Stopped the Monitor tool from running a command whose permission check counted on the sandbox when no sandbox is available by the time it runs; it reports that the command did not run and that running it again repeats the permission check.
  - 🔴 Stopped a PowerShell command that was allowed on the condition it runs in the sandbox from running when no sandbox is available by then; it reports `The command did not run. This run had to be in the sandbox, and no sandbox is available now.`
  - 🟢 Kept a Bash command in the sandbox when its permission check counted it as sandboxed, even if a hook or SDK host rewrote it while approving, unless the approval adds `dangerouslyDisableSandbox: true`; when no sandbox is available by run time, the command doesn't run and Claude is told that running it again repeats the permission check.
- `/sandbox` command
  - 🔴 Reworked the Linux `/sandbox` Config and Dependencies tabs into labeled summaries, so allowed and denied paths, hosts and dependency status are easier to scan.
  - 🔴 Made sandbox status skip its dependency checks when the basic settings and platform checks already rule out sandboxing, including unsupported WSL versions.
  - 🟢 Stopped typed letters from moving the selection in the `/sandbox` overrides picker, which now starts on the current mode instead of labeling it `(current)`.
  - 🟢 Stopped letters typed in the Linux `/sandbox` Mode picker from moving the selection.
- Device, process and special file paths
  - 🔴 Treated macOS `..namedfork` resource-fork paths like device paths and Claude Code's own credentials in the checks that refuse files for upload or transfer, including Chrome-extension file uploads.
  - 🔴 Refused macOS `..namedfork` paths when uploading file inputs or reading file references.
  - 🔴 Changed Projects to use the shared checks for Claude Code's own files and special device paths before uploading a file already attached to the conversation.
  - 🔴 Refused device or process files when reopening a file for a Claude attachment send on Linux or WSL, checking the opened file as well as its path.
  - 🔴 Made the send-file tool refuse paths it can't resolve or read under the session's permissions, and use the checked file target when opening an allowed file; `tengu_send_file` is off by default.
  - 🔴 Stopped treating a hard-linked file in Claude Code's own download folder as a saved download inside the session for file uploads and attachments, so a hard link placed there can't pass off a file from elsewhere.
  - 🔴 Refused Files API downloads and deletes for an empty, `.` or `..` file id, which would otherwise have reached a different API path, with "Invalid file ID" or "Delete file refused: the id names no file".
  - 🟢 Refused Grep and Glob searches of a path that is or leads to a `/proc` or `/dev` link, and dropped such links from search results, with "Cannot search '…': it is or leads to a /proc or /dev link that this tool does not search through. Give the folder's own path."
  - 🟢 Made the Read tool and CLAUDE.md imports refuse `/dev/hwrng`, `/dev/kmsg`, `/dev/core`, `/dev/mem`, `/dev/kmem` and `/dev/port`, and paths that use `..` to climb out of `/proc` or `/dev` (other than `/dev/shm`); paths through `/proc` or `/dev` links now get their own message asking for the file's own path.
  - 🟢 Made the Grep tool refuse to search device or process files, including paths that pass through `/proc` or `/dev` links, drop matches from such files in folder searches, and pass `--no-follow` to ripgrep so it doesn't follow symbolic links.
  - 🟢 Refused to attach or send device or process files, and paths through `/proc` or `/dev` links, in the file-sending tools, asking for the file's own path; outside Windows a path that can't be checked is refused too, and a path that now resolves to something other than what was approved isn't sent.
  - 🟢 Made the send-file tool refuse device and process files when `tengu_send_file` is enabled; it's off by default.
- Git commands Claude Code runs for itself
  - 🔴 Extended the check of a repository's own git config to the `git status` runs auto mode makes when it sends git status to its server check: Claude Code switches off any filter commands the config names and skips the status when it can't confirm the folder is inside the work tree, behind the `tengu_sifted_pebble` flag, which is on by default.
  - 🟢 Stopped the git commands that Claude Code runs against a pinned repository, such as when creating a worktree, from searching relative `PATH` entries: only absolute directories are kept, and `PATH` becomes `/dev/null` if none remain; behind the `tengu_proud_perlis` flag, which is on by default.
- Saving permission rules
  - 🔴 Added a warning when your organization's `allowManagedPermissionRulesOnly` setting stops a permission rule you chose from being saved, saying to ask an admin to add it to managed settings.
  - 🔴 Stopped saving new permission rules to settings files when the organization allows only its own managed permission rules, with a notice to ask an admin to add the rule to managed settings.
  - 🟢 Limited what a permission answer sent over the network can save, such as one from Remote Control, a channel or a self-hosted runner's host, to the allow rules and folders its prompt offered; any others in the answer are left out.
- Permission prompts
  - 🔴 Redacted field names and nested values before generating non-shell tool summaries in the regular permission prompt; remote Cowork has a separate prompt path behind `tengu_hazel_sluice`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added the destination Claude account to interactive file-transfer confirmation when the account can be identified.
  - 🔴 Gave tool-permission menus a decline choice to highlight when you type in a menu that doesn't accept text.
  - 🔴 Made long folder-listing entries stay on one line in the file permission dialog.
  - 🟢 Showed tool calls waiting on a worker's permission request as `Waiting for permission…` and distinguished them from calls with no recorded result.
- `/permissions` dialog
  - 🔴 Added a confirmation before toggling built-in auto mode rules in `/permissions`, explaining that the next request will use the new choice and your own rules still apply.
  - 🔴 Removed the numbered actions and separate Cancel row from the auto mode rule details menus in `/permissions`, where Escape goes back.
  - 🔴 Changed `/permissions` to show built-in auto mode environment entries as off when a managed `autoMode.environment` list replaces them and `--settings` requests `$defaults`, outside self-hosted sessions and when `bot_protected_tengu_dapper_origami` is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Added a warning when `/permissions` can apply a new rule in the session but can't save it to settings; the warning names the target settings file when available.
  - 🟢 Updated the `/permissions` fields for normal permission rules and auto mode rules to use a labeled input, with auto mode validation errors shown on the field.
  - 🟢 Added a clear empty result when a `/permissions` search finds no rules, including searches of the auto mode rules.
- Auto mode
  - 🔴 Replaced repeated punctuation in auto-mode block notices with the shared activity separator, keeping the `/permissions` review hint where it's available.
  - 🔴 Skipped auto mode's review of a subagent's withheld checkpoint handback when the server-side classifier had already cleared every tool call the subagent made, behind the `tengu_crispy_thunder` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Kept the server-side auto-mode check on in sessions whose tools may run on a separate tools host when it returns no verdict for a call; before, auto mode switched to the local classifier for the rest of the session. (behind `tengu_humble_wand`, on by default)
  - 🔴 Stopped resending a request without the dangerous-tool-use beta header and `safeguards` field, or moving it to a fallback model, when the API refuses them with HTTP 400 in a session whose tools may run on a separate tools host; the error stands, and the next request sends both again.
  - 🔴 Stopped sending the dangerous-tool-use beta header and `safeguards` field until restart in HIPAA-configured organizations after the API refuses them with HTTP 400, when the server-side auto-mode check isn't active; before, only the refused request dropped them. (behind `tengu_mellow_quill` and `tengu_quirky_tide`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Named the selected permission mode when organization settings switch it, and distinguished a policy default from a settings load failure.
  - 🔴 Reworded the Auto mode setup scan's progress text and clarified when the proposal review opens.
  - 🔴 Updated the auto-mode setup wizard's existing-entries picker to mark Cancel as the decline option and stopped its review list from wrapping focus.
  - 🔴 Added explanations when auto mode's server-side check can't judge a call because the server refused its request, the prompt is too long for it, or the input changed after it was sent, telling Claude the call wasn't run and that this isn't a judgment on the call.
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude auto-mode reset` and `claude auto-mode critique` messages, for example "This resets auto mode to its defaults…" and "No feedback came back. Try again."
  - 🟢 Removed the startup check that kept auto mode from becoming the starting permission mode for organizations under HIPAA or whose policy hadn't loaded yet; where auto mode is the default (the `tengu_harbor_willow` flag is on by default) or a proactivity level picks it, the session now starts in auto mode, and the "Auto mode isn't the default for your organization" and "Auto mode didn't start: your organization's policy hasn't loaded" notices are gone, while an Accept edits pick still waits for the policy.
  - 🟢 Changed auto mode to keep the server-side classifier as the judge when its rollout flag `tengu_smooth_chipmunk` reads off only from the on-disk flag cache, and always when the launching host says tools may run on its machine.
- Folder trust and added folders
  - 🔴 Added a compact layout to the `/cd` and added-folder trust prompts for windows under 30 rows, or too short to show them in full.
  - 🔴 Guarded the added-folder trust prompt against type-ahead: keys typed while it's open don't answer it, Enter only adds the folder after you press ↓ to Yes, and text typed at startup stays in your prompt.
  - 🔴 Reworded the empty-path error for adding a working directory to `Give a directory path.`
  - 🟢 Added a trust prompt for folders added with `--add-dir` or `/add-dir` that aren't trusted yet, warning that their skills, commands, agents and repository git settings can run commands; the `bot_protected_tengu_slate_orchard` flag turns it on by default, and `CLAUDE_CODE_SANDBOXED` skips it.
  - 🟢 Made local `/add-dir` ask for folder trust before adding an untrusted folder supplied by path, and decline the addition when a detached background session can't show that prompt.
  - 🟢 Added a message when you decline the folder trust prompt at launch: `Claude Code didn't start because this folder isn't trusted.`, followed by how to go on.
  - 🟢 Stopped Up from wrapping onto Yes in the folder trust prompts for a folder already trusted through a parent whose settings declare permission rules or extra directories.
  - 🟢 Added a line to the folder trust prompts at launch and in `/cd` naming the project settings files that trusting the folder loads, such as `.claude/settings.json`.
  - 🟢 Added a line to the folder trust dialog naming the project settings files that trusting the folder loads, such as `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/settings.local.json`.
  - 🟢 Changed `/cd`'s trust prompt in fullscreen mode to refuse Yes while the prompt is cut off, saying to make the window taller first; the added-folder trust prompt does the same.
- 🔴 Reworded `/cd`'s errors and warnings for a non-directory path, a failed move and MCP server choices that couldn't be saved.
- Sessions running off your computer
  - 🔴 Refused tool calls that a mod made, changed or started when they'd reach an MCP server or tool that acts on your computer or browser, in sessions that don't run on your computer: cloud sessions, SSH remote sessions and sessions with `CLAUDE_CODE_OFF_PERSONS_MACHINE` set.
  - 🔴 In sessions running off the person's machine, such as remote or SSH sessions, refused calls to tools that act on that person's computer or browser once a plugin mod has changed them, and kept a mod-changed hook approval from granting permissions.
  - 🔴 Extended the refusal of `mcp_tool` hooks that call an MCP server acting on your computer or browser from cloud sessions to SSH remote sessions and sessions with `CLAUDE_CODE_OFF_PERSONS_MACHINE` set; the new refusal names the tool and suggests removing the hook or pointing it at another server.
  - 🔴 Left out allow rules for MCP servers that act on your computer or browser, and wildcard allow rules, when a mod's hook answer in one of those sessions asks to save permission rules.
- Tools this computer runs for a remote session
  - 🔴 Expanded the host credential checks on Bash commands run through Remote Control outside Windows to recognize more forms of nested commands and protected credential access.
  - 🔴 Tightened approval for tools this computer runs for a remote session: command-like text in an MCP tool's input that can't be fully judged now asks first, any `env -S` counts as risky, and `sandbox-exec` options are understood when finding the command it runs; the `tengu_witty_harbor` flag is on by default.
  - 🔴 Checked whether unquoted Bash wildcards could reach Claude's settings or other protected files before remote tool approval, with `bot_protected_tengu_fuzzy_coral` and the served settings check `tengu_violin_purfling` on by default.
- 🔴 Let `plugin.register` handlers in mods see everything a plugin provides (modules, hooks, MCP and LSP servers, monitors, skills, commands, agents, output styles, themes, workflows and `bin` files) and narrow that list instead of only refusing the whole plugin; removing its modules still counts as refusing it, and plugins reload when a verdict changes what they may provide.
- Safe mode
  - 🔴 Updated the safe-mode startup notice to say that organization-managed plugin hooks can still run when managed hooks are enabled in safe mode.
  - 🔴 Clarified the `/hooks` safe-mode notice that already mentions managed policy hooks to say that managed plugins' hooks still apply too.
  - 🔴 Changed safe mode (`--safe-mode` or `CLAUDE_CODE_SAFE_MODE`) to keep loading hooks and hooks modules from plugins the organization manages, meaning plugins enabled in managed policy settings or installed from its managed marketplace, alongside built-in plugins; other installed plugins' hooks stay off.
  - 🟢 Clarified in `/plugin` Installed that safe mode leaves organization-managed plugin hooks running while disabling the plugins you installed.
- Plugin loading and `plugin.json`
  - 🔴 Changed plugin LSP server loading to read `.lsp.json` and the config files a manifest's `lspServers` lists only when they're regular files, logging a plugin load error that says `not a regular file, or too large` otherwise.
  - 🔴 Made startup wait for hooks modules to judge plugins that are still held back before it reports an `--agent` as not found or applies a plugin's `agent` setting, and then reload agents so one from a plugin that's let through is found.
  - 🟢 Added a plugin warning naming malformed fields in a plugin's `plugin.json` that are now ignored, noting that earlier versions of Claude Code refused the plugin, with advice to disable it from `/plugin` if it wasn't expected to run or to run `claude plugin validate <path>` if you maintain it.
- Plugin marketplaces and installs
  - 🔴 Added a headless notice when marketplace approval rules set aside a repository's plugin marketplace, with the CLI command for adding it explicitly.
  - 🟢 Reworded marketplace load errors to "Couldn't load marketplace '…'" and "Couldn't load any marketplace: …".
  - 🟢 Warned when Claude Code skips plugin marketplaces declared in a worktree's settings, naming the first one and how many more, and saying to add them with /plugin (or `claude plugin marketplace add <source>` in non-interactive runs) if you trust them.
- `/plugin` screens and plugin notices
  - 🔴 Changed `/plugin` Errors to give deprecated subagent status lines their own advisory row even when the plugin already has another warning.
  - 🔴 Simplified the local plugin auto-update warning that points to `/plugin` when reloading a plugin produced errors.
  - 🔴 Lowercased the `/plugin` pointer in the local warning for multiple plugins that require another Claude Code version.
  - 🔴 Shortened the notification that a recommended plugin couldn't be installed.
  - 🔴 Changed `/plugin` Installed to show an ellipsis rather than an empty circle while an MCP server is connecting.
  - 🔴 Removed the warning color from the “never used” label on skill rows in `/plugin` Installed.
  - 🔴 Changed claude.ai marketplace addition in `/plugin` to a visible Yes/No selection that starts on Cancel rather than taking a typed `y` as approval.
  - 🔴 Clarified the plugin setup warning when a folder lists a marketplace the session doesn't trust for plugins.
  - 🔴 Listed a plugin whose hooks module didn't load, crashed the hooks worker, or was switched off or refused in its `/plugin` details, as "Its hooks module did not load: …" or "Its hooks module is not running: …" with the reason, and, unless it was switched off, a hint to update or fix the plugin and run `/reload-plugins`.
  - 🔴 Added warnings to `/plugin` Installed rows for enabled plugins whose hooks mod didn’t load, crashed, or is off.
  - 🔴 Changed the marketplace update notice in `/plugin` to appear as information when it’s an offer, with marketplace load errors still shown as warnings.
  - 🔴 Shortened the LSP plugin install prompt and its `Not now` option.
  - 🔴 Labeled plugins from a claude.ai marketplace that can't be installed yet as `not installable on claude.ai yet` in `/plugin` lists.
  - 🔴 Reduced the space below the context-cost section in plugin details.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` Errors to give ignored manifest fields their own advisory row even when the plugin already has another warning.
  - 🟢 Showed errors in `/plugin` as a status line with the first sentence as a headline and the rest folded behind `+N more` when it's long, instead of one block of red text.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin`’s Add marketplace screen to use a labeled source field and a table of GitHub, SSH, URL and folder examples.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin`’s Add marketplace screen to shorten long errors and let Ctrl+O show the full error.
  - 🟢 Changed the `/plugin` install notice when a plugin installs but its configuration couldn’t be saved.
  - 🟢 Changed the MCP and LSP component headings to sentence case in `/plugin` details.
  - 🟢 Reworded `/plugin` batch install summaries to say which plugins weren’t installed and shorten the activation instructions for those that were.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` Discover’s empty and unavailable states to group the reason with the next step, such as adding a marketplace or checking the network connection.
  - 🟢 Put marketplace source, available and installed plugins in a label-and-value summary in `/plugin` marketplace details.
  - 🟢 Reworded `/plugin` errors for unread manifests, marketplace or component loads, failed MCPB downloads or extraction, and MCP startup failures.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` detail stats to call out the context used by skills in tokens per turn.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/plugin` error when component details couldn’t load.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/plugin` update error for a local plugin on a remote session to point to its source path for editing.
  - 🟢 Changed the `/plugin` update check error to say it couldn’t check for updates.
  - 🟢 Changed the `/plugin` configuration error to say it couldn’t load the plugin’s MCPB file.
  - 🟢 Reworded `/plugin`’s configuration read and save errors, including the warning when it saved one settings file but couldn’t save another.
  - 🟢 Reworded `/plugin` errors that explain a toggle failed or couldn’t be undone.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/plugin` Errors fallback to say it couldn’t install the marketplace when there’s no more specific error.
  - 🟢 Fixed the missing cursor after clearing search in `/plugin` Installed by putting the selection back on the first result.
  - 🟢 Added a confirmation before the Errors tab in `/plugin` opens the uninstall flow for an affected plugin.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` Browse to explain when there are no marketplaces yet, when everything in a marketplace is already installed, or when organization policy hides the available plugins.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin`’s Marketplaces tab to fit its list to the available rows and show the update or removal shortcuts that apply to the selected marketplace.
  - 🟢 Changed the Errors tab in `/plugin` to fit the list to the available rows and show the selected error's details below it when screen-reader mode is off, with a transcript-toggle shortcut to expand long details.
  - 🟢 Sized `/plugin` lists to the window: they fill the space left in fullscreen mode, never shrink below 3 rows, and show up to 14 rows otherwise.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` Discover and Browse to show a spinner while loading and frame marketplace or plugin load failures with a close or go-back hint.
  - 🟢 Reworded `/plugin` troubleshooting hints for SSH or HTTPS credentials and unreadable or damaged MCP bundles.
  - 🟢 Added a loading spinner and Escape hint to the skill usage panel in `/plugin`.
  - 🟢 Changed the skill usage error in `/plugin` to put the reason and `--debug` hint on their own line.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin`’s skill usage warnings to say loaded skills were never used, including local, plugin, claude.ai and MCP skills.
  - 🟢 Changed the skills table in `/plugin` to labeled columns (Skill, Source, Context, 7d tokens, Uses, Last used), dropping Source and 7d tokens in narrow windows, with an empty state that points to `.claude/skills`.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin`’s skill usage table to wrap long names, hiding Source and 7-day token counts below 72 columns and Last used below 48 outside screen reader mode.
  - 🟢 Changed marketplace removal in `/plugin` to a visible Yes/No selection that starts on Cancel and lists the installed plugins it’ll remove.
  - 🟢 Changed `/plugin` uninstall for a plugin with saved data to offer delete, keep, or cancel in a list that starts on Cancel, replacing `y` and `n` for the data choice.
  - 🟢 Changed plugin-copy switching in `/plugin` to ask with visible Yes/No options that start on Cancel.
  - 🟢 Replaced the `Y` and `N` shortcuts for removing marketplace settings entries from the Errors tab in `/plugin` with a confirmation menu that starts on the option to keep them.
  - 🟢 Changed the interactive marketplace list to say it couldn’t load marketplaces and include the cause.
  - 🟢 Added loading spinners to the interactive marketplace list and installed plugin list.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/plugin` activity hint to point from skill use on this machine to `/usage` for costs.
  - 🟢 Made the plugin details screen in `/plugin` scroll its description with Page Up and Page Down in fullscreen mode when it doesn't fit, keeping the install choices on screen.
  - 🟢 Changed the error from `claude plugin create` when it can’t write the files to say it couldn’t create the plugin files.
- `claude plugin validate`
  - 🔴 Added plugin validation warnings when a plugin's `plugin.json` or a hooks path can't be read because it's a link, isn't a regular file, can't be opened or is too large, since hooks modules named there load without being listed.
  - 🔴 Added a `Provides:` section to `claude plugin validate` for a plugin directory, listing what the plugin provides, and `provides`, `wasm` and `modules` fields to its `--json` output.
  - 🔴 Added a `Provides` list to `claude plugin validate` for plugin folders with no validation errors, showing the components Claude could read or explaining why it couldn’t list them.
  - 🔴 Extended `claude plugin validate` to check hooks for local plugin sources when you validate their marketplace folder or pass a marketplace manifest that lives outside `.claude-plugin`.
  - 🔴 Extended `claude plugin validate` to look for hooks in more local layouts, including plugins inside skill directories when validating a folder with no manifest and plugins whose manifest is outside `.claude-plugin`.
  - 🔴 Listed each WebAssembly file of a plugin's hooks modules in plugin validation with its size, SHA-256, imports and exports, and reported a file that doesn't compile.
  - 🔴 Added hooks-file checks to plugin validation that fail when two names differ only by letter case or Unicode form, or when a hooks path is spelled in a way Windows can open as another file, such as with a backslash, a colon, a trailing dot or space, or a short name like `NAME~1`.
  - 🟢 Changed the interactive plugin validator to use bullets for findings and say “Validating…” while it runs.
  - 🟢 Restyled plugin directory check reports, using separate symbols and wording for submission blockers, warnings and checks that found nothing to report.
- Plugins in cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Marked “Not now” as the decline option when previewing which plugins a remote session would receive.
  - 🔴 Kept `agent` and `http` hooks, and hooks with an `if` condition, in plugins synced from claude.ai by changing the default of the `tengu_woven_ladder` flag; they were dropped unless the flag listed them.
  - 🟢 Clarified the plugin settings notice for cloud sessions: new sessions follow `/cloud-plugins`, and a running session receives changes the next time this machine connects or on your first Yes in `/cloud-plugins`.
- Plugin mods and permissions
  - 🔴 Refused tool calls a plugin makes through `$.agent.call` in auto mode when no classifier can judge them in the session, since nobody can be asked to approve them there; the refusal says it isn't a judgment that the call is unsafe.
  - 🔴 Stopped a plugin's `tool.check` hook from allowing a tool that only the auto-mode classifier may approve; Claude Code ignores that allow and keeps its own decision.
- Plugin mod API
  - 🔴 Added `$.agent.call` for mods: it runs one tool as a given agent, or as the main conversation, with that agent's tools and permission checks, and refuses with a reason when the agent is being stopped, runs in the cloud, is a teammate or an observer, can't be read by this session, or has no tool by that name.
  - 🔴 Added `$.wasm.compile` for mods: it compiles WebAssembly from a file the module names as a string literal in the call, or from bytes (up to 64 MiB) when the module's scan shows it builds them at run time; a sealed plugin's file must match what its load recorded, and WebAssembly doesn't run on the hosted server or in a test file's own hooks.
  - 🔴 Stopped successful display-only slash commands from a mod being built in the session from triggering an extra Claude reply about the command's output.
  - 🔴 Made agent messages from plugin hooks carry the script's existing source labels when `tengu_brindle_otter` is enabled; it's off by default.
- 🔴 Fixed the prompt to lay out the permission mode label separately while a mod's `PromptHint` render hook is still drawing.
- `subagentStatusLine` setting
  - 🔴 Brought back the `subagentStatusLine` setting, which 2.1.325 ignored, now marked deprecated: its command gets the agent panel's rows as JSON on stdin, and each JSON line it prints with an `id` and `content` replaces that row's text, refreshed every 5 seconds while rows are shown.
  - 🔴 Brought back the deprecated `subagentStatusLine` setting, which had no effect in 2.1.325: its command again draws each subagent's row in the agent panel from row context passed as JSON on stdin, a plugin's own `settings.json` can supply one, and it doesn't run before the workspace is trusted or in safe mode.
  - 🔴 Warned in `/plugin` when a plugin's `settings.json` sets `subagentStatusLine`, which is deprecated in favor of mods and will stop working in a future release.
  - 🔴 Ran a plugin-supplied `subagentStatusLine` command again, with `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` and `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}` filled in for the command that plugin's own `settings.json` supplies; Claude Code creates the plugin's data folder first and skips the command with an error if it can't.
- Hooks
  - 🔴 Changed the notice shown when a Stop hook fails from `Stop hook error occurred · ctrl+o to see` to `stop hook reported a problem · ctrl+o to see`.
  - 🔴 Changed the `/hooks` menu's source headings to sentence case and described session hooks as "this session only" and built-in hooks as "part of Claude Code".
  - 🔴 Returned an error from an `mcp_tool` hook whose MCP server was removed from the session, instead of calling that server.
  - 🟢 Put hook failures, timeouts, stops, and deferrals into separate terminal headlines and reason lines.
  - 🟢 Explained that a `PreToolUse` hook exit code of 127 usually means a required program wasn't found, and pointed to `/hooks` to check its command.
- Skills
  - 🔴 Changed the synced `my-writing-style` skill to load as plain text when the `tengu_quiet_bird` flag is on, as it is by default: Claude Code no longer runs inline shell commands from it or fills in variables such as `${CLAUDE_SKILL_DIR}`, and it strips invisible characters, turns unusual line breaks into spaces and escapes `<` in its text and arguments.
  - 🔴 Shortened the Skill tool's success message to “Loaded skill.”
  - 🔴 Made the skill review form use the shared Unicode normalizer for skill names, so long strings of combining marks don't stall its name check.
  - 🔴 Fixed the example rule in the bundled fewer-permission-prompts skill from `Bash(foo*)` to `Bash(foo *)`.
  - 🔴 Stopped offering to save sent files as skills when they can't pass the skill review checks, whenever skill proposals are available; the extra `tengu_sorted_thimble` switch is gone.
  - 🟢 Changed the `update-config` skill in interactive sessions to list every settings key with the start of its description and save each key's full schema as `schema/<key>.json` for Claude to read when needed, instead of putting the whole settings schema in the prompt.
  - 🟢 Kept distinct `/skills` rows and their cursor position when two same-named skills have the same source type but live in different files.
  - 🟢 Stopped the Skill tool from showing a progress placeholder after a call is rejected or fails before progress arrives.
  - 🟢 Fixed skill suggestions to distinguish definitions from the same source when they point to different skill files.
- Bundled `/claude-api` skill
  - 🔴 Changed the `/claude-api` skill's API sign-in steps to skip the test request when Claude was told not to use the network, and to warn the user before signing in that Claude Code can switch to the new Console sign-in without asking if its own `/login` ends, sending the conversation so far to that organization, which is billed, so they should run `/login` when warned.
  - 🔴 Added a code-ownership check to the `/claude-api` skill's prompt audit for code-editing agents on Claude Fable or Mythos, which compares what the prompt says about who owns the code with what the user wants and suggests adding, removing or flagging the ownership sentence; the audit also says to roll out `system` or `tools` edits in new conversations where such an edit could make a stored conversation return a 400 when it resumes.
  - 🔴 Corrected the `/claude-api` skill's prompt-caching guidance: a mid-conversation change of top-level effort can invalidate all or part of the cache, a thread caches nothing until the whole conversation with `system` and `tools` reaches the minimum length, and a per-message effort change goes after everything already sent and stays in place.
  - 🔴 Updated the `/claude-api` skill's credential guidance: Claude reads the `Credentials` block of `ant auth status` for an `(active)` row instead of its `Active profile:` line, treats `ANTHROPIC_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_ID` as optional for workload identity federation, notes that `ant` skips a missing `ANTHROPIC_PROFILE` without saying so, and uses Microsoft Entra ID tokens for Claude Mythos models on Foundry.
  - 🔴 Updated the `/claude-api` skill's error guidance: a 429 from a usage tier's monthly spend cap (`enforced_spend_limit_reached`) has no `retry-after` and fails on retry until access resumes, rate limits count input and output tokens per minute, input over the context window is a 400 (`prompt is too long`), SDKs don't retry when the `x-should-retry` header is `false`, and Go's default 10-minute timeout covers only non-streaming Messages requests.
  - 🔴 Updated the `/claude-api` skill's Managed Agents reference: an archived memory store can't be attached to new sessions or written, and sessions already attached keep reading it but their writes don't reach it; scheduled-deployment error types now end in `_error` (such as `session_rate_limited_error`), an archived subagent or another unrecoverable session-creation error pauses the deployment, and archived vault credentials don't count toward the 20 per vault.
  - 🔴 Changed the `/claude-api` skill's TypeScript streaming examples for the tool runner and the manual loop to re-issue a turn when the connection drops mid-stream, as they already did for unparseable tool input, and its TypeScript Files API example to print every text block of the reply instead of only the first.
  - 🔴 Updated the `/claude-api` skill's model notes: programmatic tool calling works on Haiku 5.5, Fable and Mythos but not Haiku 4.5, per-message effort through Bedrock's InvokeModel API covers Fable and Mythos without a "where offered" caveat, and on Foundry the Fable models, like Mythos, take it on Hosted on Anthropic deployments only.
  - 🔴 Updated the `/claude-api` skill's Claude Fable migration checklist to also remove prompt lines asking the model to write out its reasoning and to review approval rules that don't name the actions they cover, its cache-miss causes table to send changes to a file other people can edit in a `tool_result` block, and its live lookup of the Fable prompting guide to also pull advice on instructions written for earlier models.
- Claude Test skill
  - 🔴 Changed Claude Test's sign-in step to retry deleting its temporary folders and, when one stays, warn and list it with a note that it can hold a signed-in browser session or test credentials, which Claude passes on to the person; Claude Test needs the `tengu_mellow_hollerith` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Fixed Claude Test treating the earlier of two runs started in the same second as the latest one when it reports on past runs; Claude Test needs the `tengu_mellow_hollerith` flag, which is off by default.
- MCP connections and reconnects
  - 🔴 Fixed on-demand MCP reconnects in the interactive terminal so a failed retry doesn't replace a connection that's already working, and an incomplete tool list doesn't clear a server's sign-in status.
  - 🔴 Changed interactive MCP reconnects to check that the saved server configuration still matches and that the retry is still allowed before starting an on-demand connection.
  - 🔴 Fixed the interactive MCP retry loop so a failed on-demand dial doesn't also schedule a general reconnect, and a retry respects the current reason a server can't connect.
  - 🔴 Retried more dropped connections when fetching your claude.ai connector list: `EPIPE`, `ConnectionClosed` and `UND_ERR_SOCKET` errors now count as temporary, so they get the quick retries (as `ConnectionRefused` now does too) and the later background retries other connection drops already got.
  - 🔴 Changed how long tool search waits before trying a failed MCP server again: 10 seconds after the first failure, doubling up to 5 minutes, then 10 minutes once it has failed 10 times.
  - 🔴 Told the model why tool search couldn't bring back an MCP server it tried to reconnect: its existing sign-in was refused, the header or headersHelper credential in its configuration was rejected, it answered 429, it asks for a sign-in, it couldn't connect (with how long until the next try), or it's still connecting.
  - 🔴 Changed the note Claude gets about MCP servers that failed to connect to say a tool search naming a server retries it with waits between tries, instead of at most once every set number of seconds.
