  - 🔴 Told Claude that calling an MCP server's tool by name, or a tool search naming the server, connects to it again, for servers that reconnect when reached and for servers needing sign-in whose earlier sign-in may still be good, so it tries that before reaching the server another way or telling the person.
  - 🔴 Changed resumed Claude Code sessions started from a claude.ai chat to count tools from MCP servers that haven't registered yet as reconnecting, instead of listing them to the model as unavailable; 2.1.325 did this only behind the `tengu_level_harbor` flag, off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the interactive terminal to listen for tool-list refreshes from claude.ai connectors with the supported MCP file-upload extension, even when the connector doesn't advertise `tools.listChanged`.
  - 🟢 Changed MCP proxy call errors to say when Claude Code couldn't tell whether the server received the call, so the user can check the outcome before trying again.
  - 🟢 Stopped MCP re-authentication from retrying a tool call if the SDK host removed its server during the attempt.
  - 🟢 Refused calls to an MCP server after it's removed from the session, such as by an SDK host, with `MCP server "…" was not called: it was removed from this session. Continue without it.`
- MCP tool schemas and calls
  - 🔴 Shortened the warning that a large MCP response can quickly fill the context.
  - 🟢 Behind the `tengu_cozy_volcano` flag, off by default, changed how Claude Code flattens an MCP tool's input schema with top-level `anyOf` or `oneOf`: a property the branches describe differently becomes an `anyOf` of its versions, and a property counts as required when every branch requires it; when that result would be dropped or rejected, the old flattening is used.
- `/mcp` and MCP dialogs
  - 🔴 Stopped `/mcp` from printing a second completion message after you've dismissed a reconnect dialog and left it running in the background.
  - 🔴 Fixed the notices about MCP servers disabled or re-enabled in another session to say "server was" or "servers were" to match the count, instead of "server(s) were".
  - 🔴 Reworded the MCP task detail screen to say how often it checks the server for updates.
  - 🟢 Updated MCP server headings to sentence case in the terminal dialogs.
  - 🟢 Used the MCP prompt's own cancel wording when the terminal asks you to press the cancel key again, so a question you can answer later doesn't appear to be cancelled.
  - 🟢 Let Escape or two presses of Ctrl+C close `/mcp reconnect` while one server is connecting, so the connection can finish in the background.
  - 🟢 Let Escape or two presses of Ctrl+C close `/mcp reconnect all` while the servers continue connecting in the background.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/mcp` tool-detail error to say it couldn’t load the description.
  - 🟢 Changed MCP reconnect feedback to say when the server couldn’t reconnect or its tools couldn’t be loaded, including connections where error details are withheld.
  - 🟢 Added an Issue row to local MCP server details when a stdio server failed and has an error to show.
  - 🟢 Changed MCP action errors to say the action couldn’t be completed and name the server.
- 🔴 Asked before destructive tool calls to the Artifacts MCP server (Claude Pages and Docs) in default and accept-edits modes when the person didn't open the turn, such as a subagent's call or a turn a notification started, with no always-allow choice, when the `tengu_sprightly_orbit` flag is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Gave background file sends a five-second grace period before they can be moved aside to process a new message, replacing the `tengu_splendid_puddle` setting for that wait.
- Organization connector rules for MCP servers
  - 🔴 Checked plugin MCP servers' already-open connections against the organization's claude.ai connector settings when policy requires the connector block on plugin servers: a server that's blocked, or that can't be checked, has its connection closed and its messages and questions held, with an error such as `MCP server "…": nothing was sent, because your organization's connector settings do not allow this server or have yet to be checked`.
  - 🔴 Stopped MCP HTTP and SSE requests to addresses that aren't `http://` or `https://`, or to a bare `host:port` address other than the server's own, with a message to check the server's address or ask its owner to fix the addresses it gives.
  - 🔴 Reworded the refusal of an MCP request whose server can't be checked against your organization's connector settings to "This server could not be checked…" instead of "This server's address could not be checked…".
- Connector file references and uploads
  - 🔴 Refused a connector file upload when an Edit or Write of that file earlier in the same response didn't apply, telling Claude to settle that change first; the `tengu_walnut_tiller` flag turns this on by default.
  - 🔴 Added a `local_file_path` object form to connector file uploads: where a connector tool takes a file inside an argument that also accepts other values, Claude can write `{"local_file_path": "<path>"}` there and Claude Code uploads that file, where it used to tell Claude the argument couldn't take a file; behind the `tengu_steady_quail` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Refused connector tool calls that skip the tool's file handling but carry a `local_file_path` object, with a message that no file was sent and the tool wasn't called; behind the `tengu_steady_quail` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Recognized more connector file schemas for uploads, including file objects that list alternative required fields with `anyOf` and a file schema wrapped in a one-entry `allOf`; behind the `tengu_steady_quail` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed pressing Esc during an MCP connector call that sends files, in the main session, to wait up to 2 seconds for the connector's own error; without one, Claude is told the call was stopped while running and the files may have reached the connector, so it should tell you before sending them again.
  - 🔴 Distinguished a connector file that wasn't sent whole from one that wasn't sent at all when the upload shows some bytes sent.
  - 🔴 Showed a stopped-transfer line in the compact connector error view when the first file was stopped during transfer or while waiting for confirmation.
  - 🔴 Named the connector in follow-up advice when a file send can't be confirmed or is stopped during confirmation.
  - 🟢 Allowed Artifact publishing to use a background job's own temporary files when the organization's Artifact policy allows it and `tengu_walnut_tiller` is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Let Artifacts file uploads from a background session take files from that job's own `tmp` folder, which the upload check used to refuse as Claude's own files.
- Connector file downloads
  - 🔴 Added a terminal notice for connector file outcomes outside cloud-session downloads, including when a stopped download might've left a file behind, behind `tengu_piped_planet`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the result Claude gets when you stop an MCP connector call that has already offered files to download, from the plain refusal text to a note that the call was stopped while running and may have taken effect.
  - 🔴 Stopped putting the names of files that connectors saved into the file reminders Claude gets (changed on disk, opened or selected in the IDE, read before a summary): the reminder names the connector-downloads folder and asks Claude to list it, or leaves the path out when the file may be a connector's download.
- Tool search and deferred tools
  - 🔴 Told Claude when its lists of skills, MCP server instructions or deferred tools were shortened to save room, and how to load a tool when the list shows a server's tools as one row.
  - 🔴 Told Claude, when it asks the tool search for a built-in tool that this session replaces with tools from the host's server, which tools replace it and which of those still load through the tool search.
  - 🔴 Added a remote character budget for the deferred-tool list and MCP server instructions re-attached after compacting the main conversation, behind the `tengu_slim_lantern` flag, which is 0 (off) by default: over budget, a server's tools collapse to `server__* (count)` and each server's instructions end in ` [cut short]`.
  - 🔴 Turned on citations for the deferred-tool placeholder sent to the API when the request asks for search citations, behind the `tengu_virtual_leaf` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🟢 Fixed tool search picking a different tool whose name differs only in letter case when a query names a loaded or deferred tool exactly; the name with matching letter case now wins, with a case-insensitive match as the fallback.
- Starting cloud sessions and agents
  - 🔴 Added live cloud-startup details for newly created sessions, including a waiting question and the settings that will apply once the session opens.
  - 🔴 Let startup prompts wait for a cloud session to open before launching, so setup questions don't compete with the cloud-startup panel.
  - 🔴 Held cloud permission-mode changes while the startup panel is still opening the session.
  - 🔴 Opened the session screen right away when you create a cloud session from the terminal, behind the `tengu_elegant_forest` flag, which is off by default: you can type while it's set up, a typed message or slash command is sent once the session opens, settings you change meanwhile are applied then, and a message that couldn't be sent is printed when you leave.
  - 🔴 Added, behind the `tengu_elegant_forest` flag (off by default), opening the interface right away when `claude --cloud` starts a new cloud session, showing a checking state while the session is created and fetching environments and a name ahead.
  - 🔴 Reworded the cloud session cancellation notice to say that the session may still exist if the creation request was already sent.
  - 🔴 Let typing an exit command while a cloud session is starting cancel the session's creation.
  - 🔴 Let Escape cancel a new cloud session while its startup panel is visible, and let the exit shortcut cancel it when the prompt box is empty.
  - 🔴 Hid skipped steps and normal worker boot from the terminal's cloud-session startup progress.
  - 🔴 Reworked cloud-startup step rows to align details under the step name and show a spinner while the step is running if animation is enabled.
  - 🔴 Showed the starting steps in the terminal for cloud sessions opened with seeded setup steps, and hid the panel once the setup steps are done.
  - 🔴 Removed the worker-start step from headless cloud provisioning notices while it's running, so the SDK doesn't repeat that step as an ordinary progress message.
  - 🔴 Shortened cloud-startup errors when provisioning fails or a queued message wasn't sent before the session disconnected.
  - 🔴 Warned "Remote session couldn't switch to <mode> mode" when the permission mode picked while a cloud session was starting isn't the mode the session opened in.
  - 🔴 Added a `cloud` label to the footer while a new cloud session starts launching, before it has a link.
  - 🔴 Added the permission mode to the footer while a new cloud session is starting.
  - 🔴 Let the terminal keep a permission mode chosen before a cloud session opens without a first prompt.
  - 🔴 Added a notice when trying to change permission mode while a cloud session is already opening with its first prompt.
  - 🔴 Added a slow cloud-startup status after 30 seconds without a worker frame, restarting the countdown when you send a message while the worker is starting.
  - 🔴 Reworded the cloud-launch prompt to say when it turns off Remote Control for the current session.
  - 🔴 Showed the pending draft in the terminal's cloud-launch question and required you to return to the choices before Enter can answer it.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud session launch checklist to show "Waiting for a machine", then "Got a machine" or "Couldn't start on a machine", to hide the "Starting Claude Code" step unless it fails with "Couldn't start Claude in the cloud", and to say "directory" instead of "folder".
  - 🔴 Let slash commands that run in this terminal, entered or run from a keyboard shortcut while a cloud session is starting, wait and run once the session opens, with a cancellable "/name will run once the session opens" notice.
  - 🔴 Showed "/name not run · retry once the session opens" when a keyboard shortcut runs a slash command that can't run in this terminal while a cloud session is starting.
  - 🔴 Skipped a slash command that a keyboard shortcut ran while a cloud session was starting, showing "/name not run · send or clear your text first", when the session opens with a starting prompt and the prompt box has text.
  - 🔴 Kept your typing going to your message while a question is shown during cloud session startup, with warnings when Enter is pressed before answering ("Not answered yet"), when a key or a large paste can't be added, and when part of the question is scrolled out of view.
  - 🔴 Added a waiting notice when a new cloud session needs an answer to start; in fullscreen, questions that fit can open at the bottom instead of in a modal.
  - 🔴 Changed cloud-session startup to show that the interrupt shortcut quits immediately if cancellation is already underway.
  - 🟢 Reworded the cloud sign-in screen to "No cloud session started. Cloud sessions need a claude.ai sign-in, and this computer has none.", and renamed the "Validating session" step to "Checking session" when a cloud session is brought into the terminal.
- Cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Clarified the warning when a cloud session's Claude.ai sign-in no longer works; the notice still offers `/login` when login is available.
  - 🔴 Clarified that an archived cloud session won't take more messages until you unarchive it on Claude.ai.
  - 🔴 Kept the cloud startup panel in sync with queued sends, including when the worker start wait expires and messages begin sending without waiting.
  - 🔴 Kept the cloud busy indicator in sync when the worker starts or finishes compaction in the background. (behind `tengu_stateless_treasure`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Cleared the cloud busy indicator when the last pending send is taken back or dropped, if no cloud turn is active.
  - 🔴 Made Escape choose Back in cloud session details.
  - 🔴 Showed a resume hint for idle cloud sessions that can resume by typing or sending a message, with a warning that uncommitted files may not carry over.
  - 🔴 Reworded the notice shown when you stop waiting on a command that runs in a remote session to `Stopped waiting for the session — the command may still finish there`.
  - 🔴 Changed cloud sessions started from a claude.ai chat to show the oldest pending permission request first, the main conversation's before a subagent's, instead of the newest; 2.1.325 did this only behind the `tengu_humble_bumblebee` flag, off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud background task error to say `Couldn't stop the task` before the reason.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud background task dialog's help text to point to the finished task's output in the transcript.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud stop confirmation to say directly whether stopping will also discard the plan.
  - 🔴 Reworded the note shown after toggling focus view in a remote session this connection can't update, which now says the change was `applied here only`.
  - 🔴 Reworded cloud session errors to say a requested ref `can't be used` instead of `cannot be honored`, and dropped `(BYOC)` from the note about Autofix and self-hosted default environments.
  - 🔴 Decoded escaped ampersands and angle brackets in replayed remote messages that have an encoded markup trailer.
  - 🔴 Made remote-session failure messages show reconnect progress as an attempt out of the total and state when an outgoing message wasn't sent.
  - 🔴 Used `directory` instead of `folder` in the cloud prompt for sending files from your computer.
  - 🔴 Reworded the cloud-session warning for unavailable commands to make clear that `claude --teleport` continues the session on your computer.
  - 🟢 Reworded the cloud session picker's empty and error screens, and showed the chosen session's title below the loading message when resuming it.
- Taking back messages sent to cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Waited until an open dialog closes before putting a taken-back cloud message in the prompt box, and sent it to history if you sent another message in the meantime.
  - 🔴 Changed classic prompt history search to show a wait message if you open it while a cloud message is still being taken back.
  - 🔴 Blocked opening the external prompt editor while the cloud session is still taking back a queued message and showed a message asking you to wait.
  - 🔴 Checked the message ID in a cloud take-back response when one is present, and ignored a confirmation for a different message.
  - 🔴 Clarified cloud take-back messages when a reply arrives late or another client took back the message.
- Attachments in cloud and Remote Control sessions
  - 🔴 Changed cloud attachment handling to report when a different file already occupies the expected path; eligible sessions now default to the `tell` setting of `tengu_tidy_shelf`.
  - 🔴 Sent eligible image attachments to Claude using the stored JPEG before the original finishes downloading when `tengu_whimsical_trinket` is on (off by default) and Claude can tell early that the JPEG is suitable.
  - 🔴 Let the early JPEG path use image size and dimensions from the JPEG response when `tengu_eager_fiddle` is on (off by default), so it can make the decision before reading the original image's first bytes.
  - 🔴 Changed cloud attachment copies to respect the destination model's supported image dimensions in eligible sessions, without the `tengu_twin_heron` flag.
- Files Claude sends from cloud and Remote Control sessions
  - 🔴 Changed failed uploads of files Claude sends from cloud and Remote Control sessions to get their second try at any size the server accepts; before, only files up to 30 MiB, set by the removed `tengu_shimmying_willow` flag, were posted again.
  - 🔴 Gave files over 30 MiB that Claude sends from cloud and Remote Control sessions a second upload try after a network error, a dropped connection or a server error; by default 2.1.325 never re-sent files that large, and now only an upload size limit the server reported in the last 10 minutes skips the retry.
  - 🟢 Added Cloudflare errors 520–524 to the errors that can trigger another Claude attachment upload attempt.
- Sign-in checks for remote agents and cross-machine messages
  - 🔴 Refused to start a cloud agent from a tool or workflow when the session's sign-in has changed since it began, saying it can't use the sign-in it began on, or when the session signs in some other way than a claude.ai login.
- Remote Control
  - 🔴 Added redacted tool summaries to Remote Control approval details for non-shell tools when `tengu_bridge_requires_action_details` is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Changed Remote Control's dialog to render the current error as formatted text.
  - 🔴 Changed the `/remote-control` action list to leave Home and End alone when every action fits on screen.
  - 🔴 Reworded the workspace-changes notice when an older remote Claude Code only supports per-turn changes.
  - 🟢 Reworded the Remote Control worktree option's help text to say it needs a Git repository.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude remote-control --verbose` to write spawned sessions' debug logs to `~/.claude/child-logs/`, warning that session debug logs are off when that folder isn't owned by you, is writable by others or can't be used, and skipping a session whose environment points at another home folder.
  - 🟢 Changed Remote Control sessions to report idle as well as running when they reconnect and when the worker registers, counting an open turn or work still running as running. (behind `tengu_bridge_owner_pinned_end` and `tengu_bridge_requires_action_details`, on by default)
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude daemon remote-control` messages to say "Remote Control server", and changed the error for a name shared by several servers to "more than one Remote Control server is named '…'. Use its directory instead."
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude remote-control` startup messages and errors, for example "Unknown permission mode '…'. Use one of: …", "Session ID "…" has characters that aren't allowed." and "… is already running in this directory. Use that one, or stop it first."
  - 🟢 Reworked the connected `/remote-control` menu so the QR code can sit beside the action list on a wide, short terminal, and the session blurb yields space on shorter displays; the compact layout applies when screen-reader mode is off.
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude remote-control --help` to describe `--permission-mode`, `--settings` and `--[no-]chrome` as applying to new sessions instead of spawned sessions.
  - 🟢 Told claude.ai that a Remote Control session is waiting on the terminal when a question there can only be answered in the terminal and stays open for a second.
- Stop from Remote Control and the web
  - 🔴 Added an option for Remote Control clients to interrupt a session while sparing its background tasks (`spare_background_tasks`): a headless session then keeps them running instead of stopping them, and a terminal session keeps both its background tasks and its `/loop` loops.
- Late remote approvals
  - 🔴 Let a newer remote prompt leave a matching tool approval open when `tengu_steady_canyon` and `tengu_candid_juniper` are on (both off by default).
  - 🔴 Expired late remote approvals after 10 minutes when `tengu_steady_canyon` is on (off by default).
  - 🔴 Changed what Claude sees after a late approval to explain that the repeated tool call represents an earlier action and that the result belongs to that action.
- Teleport
  - 🔴 Changed cloud session details to say `Couldn't teleport` before the reason when the local handoff fails.
  - 🟢 Reworded Teleport's prompt to sign in with a claude.ai account.
  - 🟢 Switched the `/teleport` progress screen to the shared terminal checklist, with the session ID below the title.
  - 🟢 Changed the `/teleport` success notice to say “Session resumed.”
  - 🟢 Clarified `/teleport`'s refusal when a message or command is still waiting to run.
  - 🟢 Pointed `/teleport` users to `claude.ai/code` when the move may have gone through despite an error, or when the session is already in the cloud.
  - 🟢 Clarified `/teleport`'s errors for missing Git remotes and cloud environments, including when the default environment is self-hosted.
  - 🟢 Updated `/teleport`'s progress, resume and cancellation hints to distinguish sending the current session to the cloud from resuming a cloud session locally.
  - 🟢 Reworded `--teleport` errors: uncommitted changes now read `You have uncommitted changes. Commit or stash them before using --teleport.`, and failures say `Couldn't check out branch` and `Couldn't check the session's repository`.
  - 🟢 Reworded `--teleport` and transcript loading errors: `Run claude --teleport … from a checkout of …`, `Couldn't check the session`, `Couldn't load transcript from file:`, `N of M files didn't download.` and `Couldn't download files:`. (behind `tengu_elegant_forest`, off by default)
  - 🟢 Updated Teleport's dirty-worktree prompt and its fallback messages for failures listing or stashing changed files.
- `/send-to-project`
  - 🔴 Added direct `/send-to-project` messages from an interactive terminal driving a cloud session: when policy allows remote sessions and `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel` is on (it's off by default), type the command yourself, choose a project, and approve the preview to post as you.
  - 🔴 Added default messages when you leave the note blank in cloud `/send-to-project`, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default: an existing project is asked what it'd pick up from the session, and a new project is asked to propose a setup for review.
  - 🔴 Let you create a Claude Project from cloud `/send-to-project` with a name suggested from the session title and confirmation before creation, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made cloud `/send-to-project` refuse to post when it reads back a different project from the one you selected, including in the check after you approve the preview, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made cloud `/send-to-project` distinguish a failed send from uncertain delivery and tell you to check the project before retrying an uncertain send, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made cloud `/send-to-project` give you the new project's link when creation worked but delivery wasn't confirmed, so you can retry without creating another project, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Gave cloud `/send-to-project` notes a 1,024-byte limit with a message asking you to shorten a longer note, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made cloud `/send-to-project` tell you to check Claude Projects before retrying when a create request could've succeeded without returning a usable reply, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made cloud `/send-to-project` tell you when it couldn't read a character in the note and ask you to edit the note before sending it, behind `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Enabled project-name completion when sending sessions to Projects over the local interactive route under `tengu_amber_tributary_dotterel`, which ships off by default.
- Claude Projects sessions
  - 🔴 Made Projects use the current conversation when deciding whether to shorten extra-long project instructions in the returned project info.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select a destination in the Continue in Projects project picker.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select on the Continue in Projects message confirmation.
  - 🔴 Marked “No” as the decline option when Continue in Projects asks to create a project from the current session.
  - 🔴 Made Projects refuse reads through a name made up to distinguish duplicate uploads when the session's hooks and permission rules can't check the stored name.
  - 🔴 Changed the Projects write error to distinguish temporary network failures using the same check as the rest of Claude Code.
  - 🔴 Started and stopped status reminders in remote project sessions as the service adds or removes them in the worker state.
  - 🔴 Changed the Claude Project continuation skill's context prompt to load only where the skill itself is available.
  - 🔴 Changed Continue in Projects' message composer to use a multiline field with its validation error shown beside the field.
  - 🔴 Gave files uploaded to an attached project a unique name when they share one with a doc or another file, adding part of the file's ID as in `notes (1a2b3c4d).md`, behind the `tengu_floating_bumblebee` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed claude.ai project docs whose names are longer than 255 bytes to be written as files whose names are cut to 255 bytes with a short hash before the extension, so they fit the usual file-name limit.
  - 🔴 Added the current document text to some Projects refusals to overwrite an unseen document, with `tengu_lovely_rain` and `tengu_candid_lantern` on and `tengu_lucky_nebula` also on for remote bridge calls; all three are off by default.
  - 🔴 Made Projects ask for confirmation before a follow-up write when a recent refused overwrite showed the current document text and the usual confirmation rules apply.
  - 🔴 Made Projects reject an upload with a `local_path` longer than 4,096 characters and show which limit it hit.
- Cowork memory
  - 🔴 Told Claude when memory files it was shown earlier in the conversation have been removed, so it stops relying on them.
  - 🔴 Changed Cowork memory delivery to show an already-seen snapshot again when eligible cloud-prefetched files mentioned by it have changed, with a notice about what changed.
- Artifacts
  - 🔴 Changed Artifact type commands in cloud sessions that reach Artifacts through the session's Artifacts connector, when the built-in Artifact tools aren't available, to tell Claude to use the Artifact tools it's offered instead of the built-in one.
  - 🔴 Broadened the instruction given to Claude after an Artifact is deleted: it should stop using the old URL anywhere, not just in the Artifact tool.
  - 🔴 Disabled XSLT in the headless Chromium browser used for local Artifact previews, including the XSLT origin trial, when Artifact preview is available.
  - 🔴 Changed new slide deck Artifacts to share one stylesheet, `project/deck.css`, published with the deck index and the cover slide and recorded in the index (`"v": 5`); a deck that already has slides keeps its version.
  - 🔴 Changed new Slides decks that Claude builds through the `AppifactRepl` tool to start with a shared `project/deck.css`, listed in the deck index, for the rules and `--variables` every slide shares, while a deck that already has slides keeps its inline styles; the tool needs a remote Cowork session with the `tengu_buzzing_lightning` flag on, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Gave `/artifacts` a labeled rename field, reserved space for it in the dialog, and stopped the artifact list from wrapping focus at its ends.
  - 🔴 Changed `/artifacts`' empty list to suggest asking Claude to make an artifact, and marked “Keep” as the decline option when confirming an artifact action.
  - 🔴 Turned off the `anthropic-skills:docs` account skill when your organization's policy doesn't allow Artifacts.
  - 🔴 Changed Artifact auto-refresh setup to take its trigger URL and secret from the server's structured `com.anthropic/watch-url` response instead of recovering them from display text.
  - 🔴 Changed Artifact auto-refresh setup to show a cleaned server explanation only when the trigger service sends a structured error code.
  - 🔴 Redacted detected tokens from file names and Artifact titles in publish, update, delete, and preview summaries; local previews need `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PREVIEW` or the browser preview gate.
  - 🔴 Made Artifact deletion fall back to its normal retry wait if a rate-limit response carries a malformed or unusually long `Retry-After` header.
  - 🔴 Changed Artifact presence, which opens presence connections for the artifacts a session works on, to default on when the service sends no setting (`tengu_brass_plover` now defaults on, and `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_PRESENCE` still overrides it).
  - 🔴 Changed the hosted cloud document tool so a creation already allowed by the user or a hook won't start the separate plan-mode review flow.
  - 🔴 Changed the artifact-watching warning to say when watching is switched off for this session.
  - 🔴 Changed the Artifact design guidance that Claude gets on Opus 5.5 to style plain technology, business, money and data pages after the first of the three design cards it's dealt (its typefaces, ground and colors) instead of defaulting to Space Grotesk with Inter, to avoid Bricolage Grotesque, and to give two cards new fonts; this guidance needs the `tengu_tingly_petal` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🟢 Updated Artifact publish guidance for owner-only links inside folders that may have a wider audience, so Claude explains what folder access allows and warns when the intended audience may still need access.
- 🔴 Stopped counting an Artifact as published by you when a hook approved the Artifacts tool call that published it from this computer, so later `publish_version` and `edit_version` calls for it ask for permission instead of being allowed on that basis.
- Claude in Chrome
  - 🔴 Simplified the Claude in Chrome setup text to say the terminal updates as each step completes.
  - 🔴 Changed Claude in Chrome's check of a tab's current address before an action to wait up to 10 seconds instead of 5, and to always start a second lookup after 2.5 seconds; the `tengu_velvet_haven` remote setting that tuned both is gone.
  - 🔴 Made Claude in Chrome always refuse a `browser_batch` that acts on more than one tab, or an action whose tab can't be identified; 2.1.325 refused them only when the `tengu_buzzing_thompson` flag was on, which it wasn't by default.
  - 🔴 Denied Claude in Chrome actions whose permission ask couldn't confirm which site the tab was on, with "Claude in Chrome didn't send this action because it couldn't confirm which site the tab was on."; behind the `tengu_hazel_sluice` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added a way for Claude in Chrome in remote sessions in auto mode to skip re-reading a tab's address before judging an action on a site Claude just read; behind the `tengu_sturdy_kettle` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Withheld a Claude in Chrome result when its tab read was skipped and the tab had left that site, and refused such a call unless the classifier allowed the site; both only happen when the `tengu_sturdy_kettle` flag is on, as it isn't by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the Claude in Chrome tab link label to `View tab`.
  - 🔴 Shortened the onboarding explanation of what Claude in Chrome can do through its extension.
  - 🔴 Changed Claude in Chrome's single-tab batch approval to recognize batches that open a site already allowed for the session in cloud workers, behind `tengu_squishy_prism` and `tengu_sparkling_gosling` (both off by default).
  - 🔴 Changed Claude in Chrome on Desktop to continue in the built-in browser when Chrome isn't connected and only built-in browser tools are available, behind `tengu_cheerful_orbit` (off by default); tasks that asked for Chrome or need one of its tabs can still ask to connect it.
  - 🔴 Changed Claude in Chrome's disconnected-browser setup card to use the fallback that fits the client's browser tools, behind `tengu_cheerful_orbit` or `tengu_memoized_owl` as applicable (both off by default).
  - 🔴 Polished `/chrome`'s connection status and browser-lookup messages, with a spinner while it looks for connected browsers.
  - 🔴 Made Escape go back to the `/chrome` menu from its browser-switching screen instead of closing the dialog; the back-only view also supports typing to select.
  - 🔴 Changed headless resume to restore a saved Claude in Chrome hint about another session taking over, when the saved record and current Chrome connection still match.
  - 🔴 Kept a cloud worker from picking a superseded Chrome device after a restart when an extension is paired and no newer browser hint has arrived, behind `tengu_vivid_key`, which is off by default.
  - 🟢 Reported a Claude in Chrome connection that organization policy blocks with the message "Claude in Chrome is disabled by your organization's policy." and counted it as policy-blocked in MCP status.
  - 🟢 Added a note to tool search results naming configured MCP servers that provide Claude in Chrome when the organization's policy turns Claude in Chrome off, saying their tools are unavailable and retrying won't help.
  - 🟢 Told Claude when MCP servers that provide Claude in Chrome are disabled by the organization's policy: their tools are unavailable, retrying won't help, it shouldn't control the browser another way unless asked, and it shouldn't say that a setting or an administrator can turn it on.
  - 🟢 Raised the Claude in Chrome `browser_batch` tool's timeout from 90 to 150 seconds, the value the removed `tengu_serene_gizmo` flag used to turn on.
- Subagents and forks
  - 🔴 Changed project-instructions tracking to copy the parent's already-read files only to agents forked by a tool call, rather than to every fresh fork.
  - 🔴 Hid zero tool-use and token counts in grouped Agent progress rows.
  - 🔴 Changed completed Agent results to omit zero tool-use and token counts and show elapsed time when the call took at least a second.
  - 🔴 Fixed earlier tool entries in a running subagent's or teammate's details to display as past activity rather than as calls still running.
  - 🔴 Changed resuming a forked subagent to continue with the system prompt, model, tools and permissions of the agent that forked it, rebuilding that agent from its saved record when it isn't running, instead of borrowing the resuming agent's; the resume is refused if rebuilding that agent fails, and keeps the old behavior when that agent left no record.
  - 🔴 Added a refusal when resuming a subagent whose saved record names run limits this build doesn't know, or that needed a permission check this process no longer holds, telling Claude to start a new agent; known run limits are applied again on resume.
  - 🔴 Clarified the message Claude receives when an agent couldn't be resumed: another message will try to resume it again, whether or not its saved report could be shown.
  - 🔴 Shortened the terminal notice when background agents are stopped.
  - 🔴 Renamed `Local agents` to `Background agents` in the task list and changed the fallback label in agent details to match.
  - 🔴 Stopped refusing to resume a stopped agent at its own interrupted call when a cloud session restarts with background agents turned off; that path now checks only the permission rules for the agent's type, and its refusal no longer mentions background agents.
  - 🔴 Changed `/tasks` to mark completed subagents that still have work to wait for as `waiting`.
  - 🔴 Moved the paragraph telling subagents that messages from the agent that launched them direct their work, but never count as the user's approval, out of the Notes list to stand on its own before it in the subagent system prompt.
  - 🔴 Showed `Not fetched` in an expanded agent result when its web fetches returned no successful response.
  - 🔴 Added room for the starting prompt when showing it in an expanded agent transcript.
  - 🔴 Fixed background subagent resumes to use the delivered task notification when there is one and check the subagent's current permissions separately from the background-agent setting.
  - 🟢 Stopped counting a subagent's `SubagentHandback` call in the tool-use total shown when it finishes and in its completion telemetry.
  - 🟢 Left `SubagentHandback` calls and their results out of the subagent progress summary, so the handback doesn't look like ordinary tool work.
  - 🟢 Counted how many Agent calls finished or launched in a batch, showing the number out of the total when some didn't succeed.
  - 🟢 Told the main session when another agent resumes an agent it started that was waiting on its own background work: Claude gets a note that the agent now reports to the other agent, so it shouldn't treat the work as cancelled, relaunch it, or wait for its notification. This applies in interactive sessions while the `tengu_clever_pillow` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Stopped showing an in-progress placeholder for a finished agent with no progress messages, and marked its outstanding calls as having no result recorded.
  - 🟢 Changed subagent rows to use the same selection marker and highlight as other lists.
  - 🟢 Changed subagent rows to label failed and stopped work explicitly instead of showing it as idle, running or done.
- Messages to subagents and resuming them
  - 🔴 Changed the queued-message warning to say it couldn't send the message to the subagent.
  - 🔴 Added a warning when sending a message to a subagent without its images because they couldn't be saved.
  - 🔴 Reworded subagent resume failures and made clear when a message wasn't sent because the subagent is no longer running.
  - 🟢 Fixed images pasted into a message to a viewed subagent so the subagent can read the image instead of seeing only its placeholder.
  - 🟢 Changed the footer to offer `↓ to resume subagent` while a stopped subagent is available to resume. (behind `tengu_copper_thistle`, off by default)
- Agent teams
  - 🔴 Changed the agent team task list summary to read like "3/5 tasks done · 1 in progress", with "open" and "done" in place of "pending" and "completed", and hidden tasks listed as "N tasks (…)".
  - 🔴 Made the background-work wait tool use the session's normal teammate inbox reader, including team messages stored by the host.
  - 🔴 Reworded the Agent Teams notice when a terminal pane can't open and the teammate runs in the current session instead.
  - 🔴 Reworded iTerm2 teammate pane setup to say what installed, what couldn't be installed or verified, and what choosing tmux or skipping will do.
  - 🔴 Skipped empty indented blocks in agent team task details when teams are enabled and `tengu_amber_flint` is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Made resumed in-process teammates use their custom agent definition's effort setting when that definition can be loaded.
  - 🔴 Hid empty nested message details in the Agent Teams transcript.
  - 🔴 Simplified the Agent Teams notices for plan decisions, shutdown rejection, and a teammate stopping or shutting down.
- Agent view and background sessions
  - 🔴 Changed moving through the agents view's list during onboarding to skip group header rows.
  - 🔴 Changed the agents view's session count line to leave out counts of zero and show `nothing running` when no session is awaiting input, working or completed.
  - 🔴 Added a paragraph to `claude daemon --help` saying idle background sessions usually stop after about an hour, and one you moved to the background with ← before typing anything after about 5 minutes or as soon as you exit agent view; the help also now says the on-demand background service exits only once no client is connected and no background session is running.
  - 🔴 Changed the reason shown when a prompt sent to a running background session can't be confirmed from `its supervisor did not answer` to `its background service didn't answer`.
  - 🔴 Changed the background-session notices to say when there isn't a saved session to resume or a message to move.
  - 🟢 Ended the background copy of a session when you quit the background sessions view you moved it to with the left arrow, if that copy is still empty, giving the background service up to 1.5 seconds to answer; on by default through the `tengu_bg_leftarrow_inprocess` flag.
  - 🟢 Changed quitting from a startup dialog, such as the folder trust prompt, in a session run by the background service to detach your terminal from that session instead of shutting it down.
  - 🟢 Stopped background sessions that aren't `--bare` from starting from a `--bare` session that uses an unapproved `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, with a message to run the work in that terminal instead; a session started with `claude --bare --bg` gets the key only if the background service has it or isn't running yet.
  - 🟢 Changed the agents view to mark the selected row with the same pointer, color and `selected, …` screen-reader label as other lists, count hidden rows above and below with the shared indicator, and say `No sessions match "<filter>"` when a filter finds nothing.
  - 🟢 Changed opening a past conversation from the agents view's `/resume` when the background service doesn't hold this terminal's API key or token: it now stops with `Run claude --resume <id> — the background service lacks this terminal's key or token` instead of opening it there.
  - 🟢 Reworded the restart, resume and removal prompts in agent view's session list into short sentences, such as "Press Enter again to restart this session. It isn't responding. It picks up where it left off."
  - 🟢 Fixed the background service signalling a recorded session's process group, when it adopts sessions after a restart, because a check on that session's process failed for a reason other than the process being gone, such as its number now belonging to another user's process. (behind `tengu_bg_gateway_mark`, off by default)
  - 🟢 Reworded background-session messages from `claude agents`, `claude attach`, `claude logs`, `claude stop`, `claude rm`, `claude respawn` and `claude daemon` to say "session", "background service" and "restart" instead of "job", "worker", "supervisor", "daemon" and "respawn", with hints such as "Press Enter to restart it" set off by a period instead of a dash; the Linux desktop notification for an expired login now says `Log in to Claude Code again`.
  - 🟢 Added Home, End, Page Up and Page Down to the agents view's resume picker and setting picker.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude logs` to scroll the terminal's existing contents up and start from the top-left corner before replaying a session's full-screen output, so the replay no longer draws over what was already on screen.
  - 🟢 Reworded many agents view (`claude agents`) messages, mostly replacing dashes with full stops or colons and "worker", "job" or "conversation" with "session", such as `Reply queued until this session restarts` and `Not signed in. Press l to sign in, or run claude /login.`
  - 🟢 Changed quitting the agents view with Ctrl+C twice right after `←` moved an empty conversation to the background to end that session too, instead of leaving it running, unless you reopened it; the hint now says ` and ends it` or ` and it keeps running`.
  - 🟢 Renamed the "Claude daemon" screen of `claude daemon hub` and `/daemon` to "Background service" and reworded it: errors start with "Couldn't …", empty lists say "No … yet", the status heading reads "Status", a stopped service shows "service stopped", the task form says "Run a prompt on a schedule", and Esc replaces the Back menu items.
- Worktrees
  - 🔴 Named a plugin whose `WorktreeCreate` hook a `plugin.register` hook kept out, with the reason, in the "not a git repository" errors from `--worktree`, agent worktrees and `/batch`.
  - 🔴 Reworded worktree exit notices about worktrees that couldn't be accessed or removed and tmux sessions that ended or might still be running.
  - 🔴 Stopped removing a repository's empty `.git/worktrees` folder when Claude Code drops a worktree; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_drifting_zephyr` flag, on by default.
  - 🟢 Changed the prompt shown before removing a worktree to count untracked folders as new folders, as in "2 uncommitted files and 1 new folder", instead of counting each untracked folder as one uncommitted file.
  - 🟢 Added a `Setting up worktree …` message after one second of worktree creation on a local interactive terminal. (behind `tengu_amber_flint`, on by default)
- Git and pull request tracking
  - 🔴 Capped the pull-request status poller's GitHub backoff at one hour when GitHub's `Retry-After` asks for longer.
  - 🟢 Fixed git activity tracking taking the pushed branch from fetch lines such as `main -> origin/main` when a command's output also fetched; it now reads the branch from the push's `To <remote>` block first.
  - 🟢 Fixed the branch Claude Code records for a `git push` when the command fetched or pulled first: it now comes from the refs listed under the push's `To …` line, skipping lines that update a remote-tracking branch.
  - 🟢 Fixed a failed `gh pr merge`, `close` or similar command counting as done when gh's output reports the failure with `X Pull request …`, so the pull request is no longer marked resolved for the session or linked to it.
  - 🟢 Stopped treating a `gh pr merge` or `gh pr close` that `gh` reports as failed, with an `X Pull request` line, as merging or closing the pull request in the session's PR tracking.
  - 🟢 Fixed the PR footer badge to refresh after switching branches, or clear if polling has stopped, when `prStatusFooterEnabled` is on (the default).
- Background commands and tasks
  - 🔴 Changed the background-work wait tool's label to show whether it's still waiting or has finished.
  - 🔴 Changed the background-task summary to call local agents background agents and remote dynamic workflows cloud dynamic workflows, and the detach message to read `Detached. N … still running`.
  - 🔴 Changed the local background task list to stop wrapping when you move past the last or first row.
  - 🔴 Reworded the queued background-update note to say `Background update` or give a count, without calling them task updates.
  - 🔴 Reset the background-update preview when a different update takes its place, so an offscreen preview can't keep displaying the old update.
  - 🟢 Labeled background shells and monitors in the background tasks list with the subagent that started them, as `@name` for a named agent or "<type> subagent" otherwise.
- Workflows
  - 🔴 Put the workflow name, overwrite warning, and save errors in one labelled field in the workflow save dialog.
  - 🔴 Shortened the workflow save confirmation while keeping both ways to run the saved workflow.
  - 🔴 Simplified workflow agent labels, including `cloud` for a cloud run and `from an earlier run` for a result from a prior run.
  - 🔴 Changed the orphaned-workflow notice to tell you to use the same arguments when resuming and explain where reuse of earlier agent results stops.
  - 🔴 Changed the `Workflow` launch result to remind Claude to pass the same `args` when resuming a run that had arguments.
  - 🔴 Redacted detected tokens from the script path in `Workflow` tool summaries.
  - 🔴 Made the loading message for dynamic workflow history brighter.
  - 🔴 Changed the dynamic workflow history list to stop wrapping when you reach its first or last row.
  - 🔴 Added a prompt-cache marker at the end of the shared opening block that sibling workflow agents declare with `sharedHeadLength`, so each agent can read that block from the cache instead of paying to write its own copy; when a request can't carry the marker, Claude Code logs a warning and sends it anyway.
  - 🔴 Added `tengu_sorrel_avocet` as a switch to hide the built-in deep-research workflow; the switch is off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the workflow rows to color the selected name and its bullet instead of putting a separate arrow by the name.
  - 🟢 Reported how a resumed dynamic workflow used its journal, in the workflow log at the first call that misses and in the run's results: how many saved agent results were reused, which call ran fresh first, and why.
  - 🟢 Changed the Workflow tool's resume and re-run hints to say that `agent()` calls replay from cache only up to the first one that failed or no longer matches, and that a call a usage limit refused usually runs again without ending reuse; a resumed run's result now starts with how many saved results were reused and where reuse stopped.
- Goals
  - 🔴 Held back approving a proposed goal that doesn't fit on screen until you scroll to its first lines, or asked you to make the terminal taller when it can't fit.
  - 🔴 Renamed the session goal tool from `ProposeGoal` to `SetGoal`, keeping the old name as an alias, and told Claude that the user is always asked before an active goal is replaced, that `GetGoal` shows the goal in force because a waiting request can't be seen, and that a met goal clears itself.
  - 🔴 Renamed Claude's `ProposeGoal` tool to `SetGoal` and kept the old name as an alias, in interactive local sessions that offer `/goal` with `tengu_propose_goal` enabled, off by default.
  - 🔴 Added `PauseGoal` and `GetGoal` tools for `/goal`: Claude can pause the session goal's stop check until the user's next message when only the user can remove a blocker, showing its reason (at most 500 characters) in the terminal, and can read the goal and the evaluator's last reason it isn't met, for example after a summary; they're behind the `tengu_propose_goal` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made `SetGoal` check its condition against 500 terminal columns after formatting, so wide characters count for the space they take in the approval dialog, when `tengu_propose_goal` is on, off by default.
  - 🔴 Made `SetGoal` always ask before setting a new goal if one is active or a `/goal` command is already queued, even with `ask_user` off, when `tengu_propose_goal` is enabled, off by default.
  - 🔴 Made `SetGoal` apply a goal immediately when `ask_user` is off and no approval is required, so the next stop is checked against that goal without waiting for a kickoff message, when `tengu_propose_goal` is enabled, off by default.
  - 🔴 Made an approved `SetGoal` proposal take effect before it sends Claude the “Goal set” message, when `tengu_propose_goal` is enabled, off by default.
  - 🔴 Made `SetGoal` show why an approved proposal couldn't start if plan mode turned on, proposals were disabled, or the goal check refused it, when `tengu_propose_goal` was enabled, off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the goal proposal shown while a goal is already active to "Claude proposes replacing the goal", with a "Replace the goal" button and the active goal under "Current goal:", and made the repeat-key hint say it dismisses "this proposal".
  - 🔴 Reworded the status for an unmet goal to `Goal couldn't be achieved`. (behind `tengu_amber_flint`, on by default)
  - 🔴 Added a steady `/goal paused` indicator in place of the pulsing timer when a goal is paused.
  - 🔴 Changed `/goal` with no argument to show when a goal is paused, why it paused, and how to continue or clear it.
  - 🔴 Changed `/goal` to show when the goal is paused, give the reason, and explain that your next message can continue it.
  - 🟢 Fixed clearing the active goal when its Stop hook was no longer registered, which left the goal in place; it's now cleared.
- Plan mode and plan approval
  - 🔴 Reworded the plan-mode notices to "Claude is exploring and planning an approach." and "Declined to enter plan mode", replacing "Claude is now exploring and designing an implementation approach." and "User declined to enter plan mode".
  - 🔴 Fixed `ExitPlanMode` approval prompts to load the calling agent's plan, falling back to the main session's plan when it was backgrounded.
  - 🔴 Reworded plan approval messages and added space for the plan itself in the terminal's approval and rejection rows.
  - 🔴 Reworded the cloud plan launch error to show where Claude Code saved the plan when it couldn't start the session.
  - 🔴 Applied the terminal's unintended-input guard to the Tab shortcut for approving a plan.
  - 🔴 Fixed a resumed session losing the fact that Claude entered plan mode from auto mode, when `tengu_tranquil_fern` is on as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Changed `/plan` to carry your plan mode choice into a cloud session that's still opening, when the terminal is starting that session and it isn't ready to take a mode update yet.
  - 🔴 Changed `/plan` to use the checks for user-selected plan mode even if Claude put the session in plan mode first.
  - 🟢 Used a yes-or-no approval card for plans submitted by subagents, while keeping the main session's plan controls when it runs in the background.
  - 🟢 Changed terminal approval of a foreground subagent's plan to a Yes/No choice that won't accept stray typing as approval.
  - 🟢 Changed the plan approval panel to use less vertical space in short fullscreen terminals.
- Code Mode
  - 🔴 Stopped Code Mode from running computer-use MCP calls that its tool rules now refuse, including calls made through saved tool definitions.
  - 🔴 Refused computer use calls to the hosted desktop's MCP server while the session is in REPL mode, with the same explanation Claude in Chrome calls already got there.
  - 🔴 Fixed tool calls from the REPL so plugin feedback can reach Claude even when a plugin refuses, replaces, or stops a call.
  - 🔴 Made the REPL check permissions one call at a time for tools that can't run concurrently when matching plugin hooks can change those checks.
  - 🔴 Kept tool results' source labels when a plugin hook replaces the result of a REPL or add-on tool call.
  - 🔴 Added limits to scripts run in Code Mode: a script nested more than 500 levels deep, or one whose nested default values, destructuring, loop heads or regular-expression named groups would be too costly to parse, is refused with a message saying how to restructure it.
  - 🔴 Made the JavaScript REPL check parse cost before compiling and explain how to split up scripts that are still too expensive after it simplifies eligible expressions.
  - 🔴 Made the REPL try to reconnect an eligible MCP tool named in a script before running it when the tool is missing from the current tool list.
  - 🔴 Changed Code Mode opt-in and opt-out to restart with the running Claude Code binary.
  - 🔴 Added a warning to the Code Mode opt-in prompt that Remote Control, Claude in Chrome and computer use are off in that mode.
  - 🔴 Made inner REPL calls run matching `tool.run` hooks around the actual tool execution when plugin hooks are enabled for that call.
  - 🔴 Made model-written REPL scripts reject attempts to overwrite an existing tool, helper, or JavaScript built-in through `globalThis`, just as they already rejected direct assignments to those names.
  - 🔴 Made the JavaScript REPL reject scripts with more than 500 levels of syntax and suggest building deeply nested values with a loop or `JSON.parse`.
  - 🔴 Marked a REPL refusal to accept a plugin's replacement for an account-memory server response as a permission denial.
  - 🟢 Fixed Code Mode's shell permission check to distinguish a dangerous-removal refusal from a command fragment it couldn't read reliably.
  - 🟢 Allowed eligible interactive local sessions with Agent Teams enabled to start in Code Mode or turn it on.
  - 🟢 Named the plugins whose JavaScript hooks keep Code Mode from turning on, with how to disable them, and said so when Claude Code couldn't check installed plugins.
  - 🟢 Reworded Code Mode's unavailable reasons: when managed settings or `~/.claude.json` also set the variable, it names `--settings` as well as the `env` block of `~/.claude/settings.json`; it says it needs the Anthropic API directly rather than Bedrock, a gateway or another `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`; and it says updating or restarting may help when it isn't open to the account.
  - 🟢 Changed Code Mode's `$` shell helper to insert a `{raw: ...}` value unquoted only when it's a one-key object literal written in the script and unchanged since; any other object with a `raw` field, such as one from parsed JSON, is refused with an error and nothing runs, while the `bot_protected_tengu_logical_meadow` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Changed Code Mode's `$` shell helper to refuse an object with a `raw` field unless it's a one-key `{raw: ...}` literal the script wrote and left unchanged, so outside data such as parsed JSON can't pass unquoted shell text; nothing runs, and the error says to check the value or use `${x.raw}` for one quoted argument. Code Mode is an experimental opt-in.
- Ultraplan
  - 🔴 Reworded the ultraplan `Implement here` option to say it adds the plan to the current conversation, when `tengu_ultraplan_config` enables ultraplan and cloud sessions are available; the build ships with no ultraplan config.
  - 🔴 Showed the terminal's loading indicator while opening ultraplan when `tengu_ultraplan_config` enables it and cloud sessions are available; the build ships with no ultraplan config.
  - 🔴 Reworded ultraplan text: its launch dialog now calls it plan mode you can edit in your browser and says Run instead of Execute, and its notes say `already running`, `already starting`, `plan saved to` and `couldn't save`.
- `/autofix-pr`
  - 🔴 Changed `/autofix-pr`'s cancellation screen to point to `/tasks` if you dismiss it before the cloud session has confirmed it stopped.
  - 🔴 Clarified `/autofix-pr`'s startup messages, including what to do when `gh` is missing or an autofix session is already watching the PR.
  - 🟢 Made `/autofix-pr` try to stop a cloud session and unsubscribe it from the PR if startup is cancelled or fails after the session starts, and show a stop link if it couldn't stop the session.
- `/ultrareview`
  - 🔴 Reworded the `/ultrareview` confirmation to say that several cloud agents find and verify bugs, and that the review runs in a cloud session.
  - 🔴 Changed `/ultrareview` to say it couldn’t start the cloud session and suggest checking that the repository is on GitHub.
- Scheduled tasks
  - 🔴 Added clean-up for removed scheduled tasks: about 10 minutes after you remove one, background housekeeping blanks its lines in the background service's `daemon.log` and `daemon.log.1`, and each line of a multi-line task log entry now carries the task's id so it can be found.
  - 🔴 Reworded the scheduled task editor's prompt hint to call each scheduled execution a run.
- Monitor tool
  - 🔴 Aligned wrapped monitor-event summaries with their text rather than the leading indicator.
  - 🟢 Told Claude when a Monitor that delivered no events had a command that wrote to stderr, naming the file to read, since stderr isn't delivered as events.
- Fullscreen and flicker-free rendering
  - 🔴 Reworded the flicker-free rendering notice's copy line to "Selected text is copied (/config to turn off)".
  - 🔴 Restored the previous fullscreen scroll view when an overlapping view closes, so scroll controls still have a view to act on.
  - 🔴 Changed fullscreen layout so the composer spans the conversation and its sidebar, while the dock can still sit to their right.
  - 🔴 Shortened the fullscreen suggestion's explanation of reduced flashing and mouse support.
  - 🔴 Stopped counting turn-duration lines as new messages in the fullscreen unread divider.
  - 🔴 Kept the fullscreen unread divider after the reply already being written when you scroll up, instead of counting that reply as unseen.
  - 🔴 Kept a collapsed read or search group in the fullscreen transcript in place as more blocks from the same reply arrive, when fullscreen virtual scrolling is enabled.
  - 🔴 Made opening a detailed transcript in an editor cancellable while Claude Code is rendering it from fullscreen transcript view with virtual scrolling enabled.
  - 🔴 Changed the error shown when a detailed fullscreen transcript can't be rendered for the editor.
  - 🔴 Shortened the fullscreen auto-copy hint and the scroll-wheel warnings for terminals that send arrow keys or use JetBrains IDE 2025.2.
  - 🔴 Skipped the fullscreen suggestion if another onboarding prompt already opened in this session, unless `CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_FULLSCREEN_UPSELL` forces it.
  - 🔴 Scrolled a fullscreen dialog to the bottom when its input is disabled or an input error appears, so the dialog can show its latest feedback.
  - 🔴 Stopped counting internal plugin notes, prompt-file notices, and withdrawn-memory notices as ordinary output rows in the fullscreen transcript.
  - 🟢 Gave the fullscreen composer at least five rows when the terminal has room, even when the panel above the prompt is tall.
  - 🟢 Kept the fullscreen jump pill available when there is more output below, even after you scroll past the unread divider; the new-message count still applies only above the divider.
  - 🟢 Kept the whole clickable prompt header at the top of the fullscreen transcript out of text selections; before, only its left margin was left out.
  - 🟢 Reworked fullscreen transcript shortcut help into a table that wraps to fit the terminal.
- New terminal renderer
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` to turn on a new terminal rendering engine, which the `tengu_idempotent_willow` flag also controls and which is off by default; screen reader mode keeps the current one.
  - 🔴 Changed Apple Terminal's fullscreen clear detection to use a terminal-specific cursor probe in the new renderer, off by default and selected by `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or `tengu_idempotent_willow`.
  - 🟢 Measured emoji joined with zero-width joiners, skin-tone modifiers and tag sequences by the cells the terminal actually draws when the new renderer runs on a TTY, behind `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or the `tengu_idempotent_willow` flag, which is off by default.
- `/tui` and the renderer choice
  - 🔴 Skipped the fullscreen feedback dialog when choosing the classic renderer if the `tui` setting already says to use classic.
  - 🔴 Simplified the notice when `/tui` can't save a renderer setting and when a fullscreen restart is refused because new work started.
  - 🟢 Changed renderer checks to keep using the `tui` setting as it was when the session first drew, rather than re-reading it, for the rest of the session.
  - 🟢 Corrected the fullscreen switch refusal to say the current renderer stays active and the classic preference applies on the next start when a restart would lose session restrictions.
  - 🟢 Clarified that a `/tui` switch refused because background work started still saves the requested renderer for the next start.
  - 🟢 Stopped showing the classic-renderer confirmation after a renderer switch when Claude Code actually started in fullscreen.
  - 🟢 Added a warning when the renderer you picked with `/tui` doesn't take effect after the restart, saying it's still the classic or fullscreen renderer and why: `CLAUDE_CODE_NO_FLICKER` or `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ALTERNATE_SCREEN`, the `tui` setting in a named settings file or managed settings, fullscreen turned off after failed starts, screen-reader mode, tmux -CC, or SSH to Windows.
- Terminal rendering
  - 🔴 Used more of the available terminal width for wrapped session links.
  - 🔴 Removed the scroll-region display mode that `CLAUDE_CODE_DECSTBM=1`, or the `tengu_marlin_porch` flag (off by default), turned on in supported terminals.
  - 🔴 Shortened the terminal's suspend message, which still points to `fg` to return and Ctrl+_ to undo typing.
  - 🟢 Fixed the shell prompt being drawn over Claude Code's last screen when it exits while a text field's cursor sits inside that screen, outside fullscreen: Claude Code now moves the cursor below its output before exiting.
- Character width measurement
  - 🔴 Fixed a lone regional indicator taking one column instead of two in a terminal diagram.
  - 🟢 Fixed terminal column measurements for some combining marks and emoji in the plugin eval results table, settings, stats screenshots, and plugin lists.
  - 🟢 Used corrected Unicode widths when fitting labels in tool summaries, connector transfer rows, and attachment notices.
  - 🟢 Changed how the terminal UI measures text so Indic vowel signs that take their own column (Devanagari, Bengali, Gurmukhi, Gujarati, Odia, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada and Malayalam), regional-indicator letters and a few emoji such as ☝, ✌ and ✍ count as wider, which keeps wrapping and truncation lined up for that text.
  - 🟢 Changed the terminal renderer to clear and rewrite a whole row, cell by cell, when it holds characters whose width varies between terminals, such as emoji with a presentation selector, keycaps, flags, joined emoji, emoji newer than Emoji 12, and letters with spacing vowel signs as in Hindi or Tamil; 2.1.325 only padded some emoji with a space.
  - 🟢 Changed the built-in Mermaid diagram plugin to count more emoji (such as ☝, ✌, ✍, 🖐 and 🤻) and a few CJK and Khmer characters as two columns wide, so boxes drawn around such labels line up.
  - 🟢 Fixed text wrapping and cell layout in the terminal UI for characters such as single regional-indicator letters, ☝, ✌, ✍ and the two- and three-em dashes, which are now measured at their real width, with extra cells padded for characters wider than two cells.
  - 🟢 Fixed spacing in terminal diagrams that contain Indic vowel signs by counting the signs that take up space instead of treating them all as zero-width.
- Hidden and invisible characters
  - 🔴 Cleaned up invisible characters and prompt marks in terminal messages from plugins without depending on the earlier `tengu_virtual_pancake` display check.
  - 🔴 Redrew the terminal after cleaning invisible characters from a submitted draft.
  - 🔴 Marked hidden characters in rendered Markdown text, tables and streaming replies when `CLAUDE_CODE_SHOW_HIDDEN_CHARACTERS` or the `tengu_bubbly_gadget` flag, which is off by default, turns that on.
  - 🔴 Showed hidden characters in terminal question previews according to `CLAUDE_CODE_SHOW_HIDDEN_CHARACTERS`, falling back to `tengu_bubbly_gadget`, off by default.
- Clickable links
  - 🔴 Made eligible shell-output URLs clickable from background sessions on terminals that can display links.
  - 🟢 Recognized the Rex terminal (`TERM_PROGRAM=rex` or `LC_TERMINAL=rex`) as supporting clickable links.
- Prompt input and completions
  - 🔴 Fixed a click in a search field placing the cursor inside an emoji or a character with combining marks.
  - 🔴 Reworded the warning when a message exceeds the number of slash commands that can run together and the rest is passed as arguments.
  - 🔴 Changed the example prompts suggested in the empty prompt, such as `edit <file> to…`, to end with an ellipsis character instead of three dots.
  - 🔴 Changed the draft stash indicator to say `draft stashed` in fullscreen mode.
  - 🔴 Changed the restored-draft notification to lowercase.
  - 🟢 Kept the full expanded paste in the fullscreen prompt when opening a paste placeholder takes the draft past 10,000 characters.
  - 🟢 Changed the prompt's suggestion list to line up descriptions in a column after the names, and to show how many more suggestions sit above and below when they don't all fit.
  - 🟢 Added room for a count of lines hidden above the prompt at the left of its border when there's enough terminal width.
  - 🟢 Added counts of hidden lines above and below the visible portion of a long prompt in fullscreen mode.
  - 🟢 Fixed Up, Down and End putting the prompt cursor at the start of the next screen row, instead of the end of the current one, where a long line wraps.
  - 🟢 Changed the fullscreen prompt history preview to shrink with the picker and disappear when fewer than two lines of preview can fit.
  - 🟢 Dropped suggested prompts that are only an XML-style tag, such as `<placeholder>` or `<signal type="empty" />`, optionally in backticks, as it already did for bracketed ones.
  - 🟢 Fixed mouse placement of the prompt cursor at wrapped whitespace so continued typing lands on the line you clicked.
- Pasted images
  - 🔴 Saved images pasted from the clipboard into a new owner-only temporary folder for each paste, removed right after reading, instead of a fixed `claude_cli_latest_screenshot.png` in the shared temporary folder.
  - 🔴 Shortened the terminal's empty clipboard hint so the image-paste shortcut is easier to spot.
  - 🟢 Fixed the prompt to bring a deleted image back when its placeholder is restored.
- Queued messages
  - 🔴 Made queued-prompt previews say `1 block` or `N blocks` when content is omitted instead of using `block(s)`.
  - 🔴 Shortened the queued-message placeholder when `tengu_jiggly_mochi` is on, off by default, and `CLAUDE_CODE_KB_COHESION_FIXES` is off: it now says Enter sends messages `now` instead of `immediately`.
  - 🟢 Capped the space queued messages take under the prompt at about a third of the terminal height, at least 3 rows each, clipping tall ones with a count of hidden lines.
  - 🟢 Put the send-now shortcut beside the `Queued` label under the last queued message when both fit on one line, with a separate hint on narrow terminals.
- External prompt editor
  - 🔴 Changed the external prompt editor failure message to say Claude Code couldn't use the editor.
  - 🔴 Changed the terminal's external editor screen to say `Save and close the editor to continue…`.
  - 🟢 Moved the cursor to the end of a terminal question's notes when opening them or returning from an external editor.
- Key hints
  - 🔴 Preferred an arrow key over other unmodified special keys when choosing which of an action's keybindings to show in a hint.
  - 🟢 Changed dialogs' default footer to the key-hint bar, labeled with each dialog's own Enter and Esc actions, and let dialogs add single-key shortcuts that work while no text field has focus.
  - 🟢 Added a shared key-hint footer for prompt fields and confirmation dialogs that uses the available width and names the active Enter and Escape actions.
  - 🟢 Added keyboard hints to focused settings controls in the terminal, including Space to toggle, arrow keys to adjust, and Enter to select, edit, or save.
  - 🟢 Changed the notifications settings footer to show the keys for the focused control and Escape to close.
  - 🟢 Updated the setup wizards to put their keyboard hints inside the dialog and show the Enter action for the current step.
  - 🟢 Added the previous-tab and next-tab keys, and a ↓ "navigate" hint, to the key hints of tabbed screens while the tab row has focus.
  - 🟢 Reworked the automatic key hint line in menus and dialogs: hints follow one order (move, switch, toggle, letters, Enter, other keys, Esc), paired keys such as previous and next share one hint, and a line too wide for the window drops its move, switch and select hints instead of ending in "+N more".
  - 🟢 Reworded the newline hint to `shift+enter for newline` and `backslash (\) then enter for newline`, replacing the ⏎ symbol.
  - 🟢 Added key hints for focused text fields: Enter to submit, and in search fields Down to move into the list and Esc to clear the search, or to go back when it's empty.
  - 🟢 Shortened the key hint for switching tabs from `switch tab` to `switch`.
  - 🟢 Added ↑/↓ and Enter hints, plus any per-row key actions, to the key hints of selection lists.
  - 🟢 Made the background shortcut hint in the fullscreen transcript show that view's binding instead of the normal prompt's key.
  - 🟢 Changed the `?` shortcut list to leave room between columns and label the shortcuts for cycling modes and opening the full transcript.
- Keybindings
  - 🔴 Reworded `keybindings.json` errors to "Couldn't read keybindings.json: …" instead of "Failed to parse keybindings.json: …", and the command-binding warning to say a command name can only contain letters, numbers, colons, hyphens and underscores.
  - 🟢 Added a section to the keybindings help skill in Claude Desktop sessions saying the app doesn't read `~/.claude/keybindings.json`, so Claude should edit it only when you mean Claude Code in a terminal, and pointing to Help > Keyboard Shortcuts and Settings instead.
- Selection menus and pickers
  - 🔴 Allowed typing ahead in the terminal's plugin confirmation and autocompact offer instead of making the picker ignore those keys.
  - 🔴 Allowed typing ahead in the terminal's computer-use app permission prompt instead of making the picker ignore those keys.
  - 🔴 Allowed typing ahead in the checklist for removing redundant auto mode rules instead of making the picker ignore those keys.
  - 🔴 Moved the cloud artifacts picker's open and close shortcuts into the standard dialog guide.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud resume repository picker to stop at the ends of the list and use the shared loading display while it checks a repository.
  - 🔴 Added a guard that ignores key presses and clicks starting before, or within 150 ms of, a menu first appearing, so a key meant for the previous screen doesn't pick an option, and shows `Not answered yet` after ignoring one; it's behind the `tengu_cheeky_newt` flag, which is off by default, and `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EARLY_KEY_GUARD` turns it off or on regardless.
  - 🔴 Stopped dialog buttons from acting on a space typed as part of text you were typing when the dialog appeared, or on a click that only brings the window forward or lands within 300 ms of the button appearing.
  - 🔴 Stopped menus inside slash-command dialogs from taking Page Up and Page Down when all their options already fit on screen.
  - 🔴 Changed menus that hide option numbers and have a current choice to mark it with a green tick at the start of its row, padding the other rows to line up, instead of coloring that row green with a tick after it.
  - 🟢 Stopped letters such as `j` and `k` from moving the selection in the macOS computer-use permissions menu.
  - 🟢 Changed long single- and multi-select menus to show clickable `↑ N more` and `↓ N more` rows instead of an arrow on the first or last visible option; clicking one jumps to that end of the list.
  - 🟢 Added Home and End to multi-select menus inside slash-command dialogs, jumping to the first and last option.
  - 🟢 Changed selection lists that don't set their own behavior to wrap from the last item to the first and back, instead of stopping at the ends.
- Keyboard guard against unintended input
  - 🔴 Kept the dismiss or keep-current-choice option available while the keyboard guard ignores unintended input in the Remote Control offer, the long-session resume offer, and the usage settings prompt.
  - 🔴 Kept the deny option available while the keyboard guard ignores unintended input in the browser domain access prompt.
  - 🔴 Kept Cancel available while the keyboard guard ignores unintended input in the cloud-launch prompt and in ultraplan when `tengu_ultraplan_config` enables it; the build ships with no ultraplan config.
  - 🔴 Kept Cancel available while the keyboard guard ignores unintended input in the iTerm2 teammate pane setup and its retry prompts.
  - 🔴 Kept the decline choice available while the keyboard guard ignores unintended input on the terminal's auto mode rule review, Bash and PowerShell permissions, Claude in Chrome permissions, file permissions and web fetch prompts.
  - 🔴 Suppressed unintended keyboard shortcuts in the file permission prompt and showed a hint when it ignores one.
  - 🔴 Kept `Edit prompt` available when the keyboard guard ignores unintended input on the `Session paused` screen.
  - 🔴 Kept the default worktree-exit choice available while the keyboard guard ignores unintended input.
  - 🔴 Changed the visual Rate Limit Options picker to treat Stop as declining the offer, so it can skip the check that guards against accidental accepts.
- Typing to select in menus
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to choose an answer in the conversation-resume confirmation.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to choose an answer in the auto-mode environment setup prompt.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to jump among the choices in the model picker.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to jump through tasks in the cloud session's background task dialog.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to jump between the choices in the extended-thinking picker.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select an option when Claude Code asks whether to allow a `CLAUDE.md` import from outside the workspace.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select an option in the workspace trust prompt.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select a session in the cloud session picker.
- Esc and Ctrl+C
  - 🔴 Changed Ctrl+C at an empty prompt to open queued-message editing before stopping a detached tool call when both are present.
  - 🔴 Changed a quick repeat of Ctrl+C after it clears the draft to leave a pending usage-limit wait alone and tell you to pause before trying again.
  - 🟢 Matched the `esc to stop` hint in subagent views to when Esc can actually stop the agent, including the exception for agents with separate stop behavior under `tengu_zinc_harbor`, off by default.
  - 🟢 Changed agent view's "Esc again to clear" hint to lowercase `esc again to clear`.
  - 🟢 Changed the `esc again to clear` hint in the prompt to stay up exactly as long as a second Esc still clears, instead of for a fixed second.
  - 🟢 Changed the prompt footer to show an Escape interrupt hint only when Escape will stop the turn, rather than when a prompt field or panel will take the key.
- Prompt footer
  - 🔴 Cleared hidden footer selections so a footer item that disappears can't keep keyboard focus.
  - 🔴 Made opening a selected terminal footer item dismiss the keyboard help overlay.
- Text fields in dialogs
  - 🔴 Changed multi-field forms, such as the scheduled task, new Remote Control server and AWS access keys forms, so ← and → change a choice field and Tab no longer cycles it.
  - 🟢 Changed text entry in dialogs such as the Bedrock API key prompt and the fullscreen feedback question to a shared framed field, with an optional label above it and a hint, warning or error line below.
- Dialog layout
  - 🔴 Changed the `Session paused` screen and the Claude in Chrome setup and install screens to use the panel's padding.
  - 🟢 Changed the layout of the effort recommendation, Life Sciences use-case chooser, and plugin confirmation dialogs to use the panel's padding.
- Loading and error displays
