  - 🔴 Changed the Pro trial prompt to use the shared loading display and error styling.
  - 🔴 Aligned the dim detail on status notices below the main text.
  - 🟢 Changed inline error messages in dialogs to show an error icon with the first line in red and any further lines dimmed below it, and to show nothing for an error that's only whitespace.
  - 🟢 Updated terminal progress checklists with shared status markers and dimmed, truncated output below running or failed steps.
  - 🟢 Changed shared loading rows to keep the message and its dimmed subtitle aligned beside the spinner, including when the message wraps.
  - 🟢 Changed the managed-settings loading message to use the shared loading spinner.
  - 🟢 Added spinners while loading output styles, local session scans and remote cost, and used the same loading style on the Usage and Stats tabs.
- Tips shown during and after turns
  - 🔴 Added a server-configured "finished-work" tip that can show after a turn that edited a web page or another file, or published an artifact; it stays off unless the `tengu_humming_noodle` flag supplies its text, which it doesn't by default.
  - 🔴 Added a configurable tip after a turn finishes work via `tengu_humming_noodle` (no tip by default), with triggers for files, UI pages, and published artifacts in sessions that aren't remote-only.
  - 🟢 Removed the `/clear` tip that the progress spinner used to show after 30 minutes; the `/btw` tip can still show on long turns if spinner tips are enabled and you haven't used `/btw` yet.
  - 🟢 Removed a duplicate `/terminal-setup` tip and shortened CLI tips about the session list, Desktop, `/tui fullscreen`, and other features.
- 🔴 Added a transcript note when You Should Know automatically sends a tip along with your prompt and the prompt goes through.
- Welcome and session headers
  - 🔴 Added `(this terminal)` after the version in the welcome and agents view headers when the workspace is remote, so the version shown reads as this terminal's.
  - 🔴 Refreshed the classic welcome header when it scrolls back into view and when you switch which subagent you're viewing.
  - 🟢 Fixed the classic welcome header to show the viewed subagent or teammate's model and effort instead of the main session's.
  - 🟢 Made hidden startup notices readable in the transcript and added a shortcut hint beside their count on the compact startup banner.
- Startup suggestions and offers
  - 🔴 Dismissed the startup effort suggestion when you start typing your own prompt.
  - 🟢 Dismissed the offer to make auto mode the default when you start typing your own prompt.
  - 🟢 Kept an extra Enter after setup from accepting the offer to make auto mode the default.
  - 🟢 Kept text typed over the offer to make auto mode the default and stopped its Enter press from accepting the offer.
- Themes
  - 🔴 Changed the theme picker so the arrow keys stop at the ends of the list and its exit shortcuts only run when the picker can be closed.
  - 🔴 Changed the custom theme editor to use a labeled name field with the base theme and save path in its hint.
  - 🔴 Changed the custom theme color editor to show accepted color formats below the field and use `#af87ff` as its example.
  - 🟢 Changed syntax highlighting to show keywords, literals, class names and tag names in bright blue instead of blue on dark themes; light themes keep blue.
  - 🟢 Changed the rate-limit bar colors in four themes: the empty part is now pale in the light and light-daltonized themes, white with a blue fill in light-ansi, and gray in dark-ansi.
  - 🟢 Gave added lines in diffs their own syntax colors on dark themes in terminals without true color, and drew variables, properties and punctuation on dark themes in off-white instead of pure white.
- Transcript display
  - 🔴 Right-aligned transcript timestamps and model names without reserving extra space for the model name.
  - 🔴 Used the shared transcript indentation for tool notices and background session details.
  - 🔴 Skipped empty indented blocks in the context-suggestion display.
  - 🔴 Hid empty diagnostic notices after filtering out diagnostics with no displayed files.
  - 🔴 Changed the transcript line for a tool call you rejected with feedback from "Tool use rejected" to "Not run · rejected".
  - 🔴 Routed prompts actually submitted by a plugin to the plugin message renderer before ordinary prompt handling, keeping them visible even when their text resembles a hidden engine message.
  - 🔴 Treated plugin messages explicitly sent as the user as prompt boundaries when deciding which transcript rows to hide.
  - 🟢 Stopped older turn-duration lines from repeating the current count of background tasks still running.
  - 🟢 Changed how your own prompts are drawn in the transcript so asterisks next to words, quotes or punctuation no longer turn text italic or bold, and some lines that start with `+`, `-` or `*` stay plain text instead of becoming list items.
  - 🟢 Hid the live count of background agents still running on a completed turn once there's a later turn in the transcript.
  - 🟢 Fixed spacing while a reply streams: the gap between its finished part and the part still arriving now depends on the blocks on either side, so a list cut mid-stream doesn't gain a blank line.
  - 🟢 Showed the approximate size of hidden text when a prompt is shortened without dropping whole lines.
  - 🟢 Added a `working` badge to transcript view while the session is busy, leaving `dialog waiting` in its place when a dialog needs attention.
- Fullscreen transcript search
  - 🔴 Included plugin-submitted prompt text in transcript search and stopped treating it as an ordinary user prompt in fullscreen navigation.
  - 🔴 Preserved invisible characters in the input and cursor text of the fullscreen transcript search field.
  - 🔴 Changed the fullscreen search match counter to read `N of M` in both the search box and the transcript footer.
- Collapsed tool-call groups
  - 🔴 Listed links to documents produced by successful connector calls beneath the grouped terminal result.
  - 🟢 Capped the "$ command" preview on collapsed tool-call groups at 5 lines, ending in "…", on top of the existing 300-character limit.
  - 🟢 Listed the links of Artifacts published or updated by the calls in a collapsed tool group under that group, as "Published <link>" or "Updated <link>".
  - 🟢 Distinguished failed, rejected, never-run, and unanswered calls in grouped tool summaries instead of treating all unresolved calls alike.
  - 🟢 Kept completed fullscreen tool groups from counting search or list operations a second time as Bash calls.
  - 🟢 Fixed Bash commands that write files being summed up as searches or reads in collapsed tool lines: output redirected anywhere but `/dev/null`, `sort -o` or `--output`, and `find` with `-delete`, `-fprint`, `-fls` or an `-exec` of anything but a search command now show as ordinary commands.
  - 🟢 Kept the fullscreen tool-group headline focused on the call that's still running rather than a later call that hasn't started yet.
- Tool results
  - 🔴 Added a note to a large tool output saved to a file when the output was cut before saving, telling Claude the file holds only the first part, ends mid-structure, and that a search or count over it covers only that part.
  - 🔴 Kept the machine-readable external-Artifact mark out of compact tool errors while leaving it available in verbose output.
  - 🔴 Restyled expanded tool result summaries to line up the heading and details without dimming the detail text, including file and content searches.
  - 🔴 Restyled the Write result's notice when it replaces content without a diff.
  - 🔴 Rendered ANSI formatting in terminal tool notices instead of passing the text through the plain wrapper.
  - 🔴 Reworded the generic tool errors for failed writes, edits, reads, notebook edits and inbox reads.
  - 🔴 Shortened the status for a tool result delivered later in the conversation.
  - 🔴 Changed the short file-search error to `Couldn't search files`.
  - 🔴 Changed shortened tool output to use the shared expandable line-count hint.
  - 🟢 Fixed valid PDFs being dropped after the API refused a different PDF from the same batch of tool results: Claude Code now records which tool result held the refused PDF, so only that one is removed from later requests. (behind `tengu_mellow_quill` and `tengu_quirky_tide`, off by default)
- Shell command output
  - 🔴 Added an ellipsis to the clipped-line count in Bash output previews.
  - 🔴 Shortened the shell result's confirmation that an image was sent to Claude.
  - 🟢 Fitted the output preview in the shell details dialog to the space left on screen instead of a fixed 10 lines, keeping each line to one row.
  - 🟢 Left indented JSON in shell tool output as written instead of reformatting it line by line.
  - 🟢 Showed a clickable path and, when known, the total size in verbose Bash and PowerShell results when more output was saved to a file.
- Durations and counts in tool rows
  - 🔴 Changed WebSearch's finished line to say "Did no searches" instead of "Did 0 searches", to leave out durations under a second, and to show longer durations in minutes and seconds instead of a raw seconds count.
  - 🔴 Changed the "Monitor started" line to say "no timeout" instead of "persistent", and to show the timeout in minutes and seconds instead of a raw seconds count.
  - 🔴 Displayed AskUserQuestion timeouts with the shared duration format, so timeouts of a minute or more can show minutes.
  - 🔴 Fixed WebSearch's progress line saying "1 results" instead of "1 result".
- Diff display and `/diff`
  - 🔴 Used the same expansion hint for truncated Bash diffs and generic tool errors in the terminal.
  - 🔴 Used the selected theme for syntax highlighting of diff lines in tool-permission dialogs.
  - 🔴 Stopped the changed-file list in `/diff` from wrapping focus at the ends.
  - 🔴 Updated the loading indicator in `/diff` to match other terminal dialogs.
  - 🔴 Colored added and removed line counts in Bash diff summaries, using the same format as other file changes.
  - 🔴 Used sidebar-style diff colors inside expanded fullscreen transcript rows.
  - 🔴 Removed the dim styling from the interactive diff viewer's loading message.
  - 🟢 Fixed how diffs show `\ No newline at end of file`: it now renders as a context line with no line number, and word-level highlighting still pairs the removed and added lines around it.
- File edit and write previews
  - 🔴 Fixed the file-write permission preview using the full terminal width instead of the width inside its dialog.
  - 🔴 Made successful file-write previews and previews of declined edits use the width assigned by their surrounding transcript layout.
  - 🔴 Shortened the message shown when you reject a file write, edit or notebook change.
  - 🔴 Added a final line break to nonempty notebook cell text in the edit preview so the diff doesn't treat the cell's last line as missing a newline.
  - 🟢 Kept notebook paths visible in verbose `NotebookEdit` tool calls when optional source or cell type information is missing, and left out those fields when absent.
- Questions Claude asks you
  - 🔴 Shortened AskUserQuestion messages for answered, declined, and follow-up questions.
  - 🔴 Fell back to the most recent valid question rewrite from UI hooks in `AskUserQuestion` when a later rewrite would change more than labels.
  - 🔴 Cleared an `Other` answer when you erase its text in a single-choice terminal question and haven't attached images, instead of leaving an empty answer selected.
  - 🟢 Restored focus to the chosen option when revisiting a terminal question that you've already answered.
  - 🟢 Fit the terminal's interruption notice and follow-up question into one line, shortening it if the terminal's too narrow.
  - 🟢 Made a question's preview shrink as its notes take up more rows in the terminal.
- Screen reader mode
  - 🔴 Used the multiline rendering path for text in screen-reader mode even when the caller hasn't requested it.
  - 🔴 Reworded screen-reader mode's typed-number prompts for menus: an entry that matches no option now reads `"<entry>" isn't an option.` before the allowed range, replacing `Invalid selection "<entry>".`, and the yes/no prompt asks `Answer y or n.`
  - 🔴 Removed terminal color codes from option descriptions in screen-reader mode's typed-number menu prompt.
- Push notifications
  - 🔴 Reworded the idle reminder to turn on push notifications in `/config` when the `tengu_kairos_push_notifications` flag is on (off by default) and the account qualifies for the reminder.
  - 🔴 Reworded the notification-tool message that tells users to turn on Remote Control when a notification can't be sent.
  - 🟢 Clarified the mobile notifications hint to say to turn on notifications after getting the app.
- Terminal setup
  - 🔴 Changed `/terminal-setup`'s description in VS Code, Cursor and Windsurf to say it also sets up faster scrolling and turns off GPU acceleration to prevent garbled text, and reworded its other descriptions.
  - 🟢 Reworded setup errors for restoring iTerm2 or Terminal.app settings on macOS, finding a Git root for `--worktree`, and creating a tmux session. (behind `tengu_amber_flint`, on by default)
  - 🟢 Changed onboarding's terminal setup prompt to list the settings it changes and show setup output or an error before moving on.
  - 🟢 Changed the first-run "Use Claude Code's terminal setup?" step outside Terminal.app to say it changes the terminal's settings outside Claude Code too and to list them: Shift+Enter for newlines, plus faster scrolling and GPU acceleration off in VS Code, Cursor and Windsurf.
  - 🟢 Changed `/terminal-setup` in Windows Terminal older than 1.25 to say Shift+Enter needs 1.25 or later and to suggest Ctrl+J or a backslash before Enter, instead of saying no setup is needed.
  - 🟢 Added the `~/.tmux.conf` lines Shift+Enter needs inside tmux (`set -s extended-keys on` and `set -as terminal-features 'xterm*:extkeys'`) to `/terminal-setup`'s messages when it runs in tmux.
  - 🟢 Reworded `/terminal-setup` messages for iTerm2, Terminal.app, Alacritty, Zed and the VS Code family, such as `Couldn't back up your Alacritty config, so nothing was changed.` and `Turned on "Use Option as Meta key"`.
- Voice input
  - 🔴 Changed the voice input indicator from `Voice: processing…` to `Transcribing…`.
  - 🔴 Reworded voice startup, microphone and sign-in errors, including the messages for remote sessions without a microphone and local setups that need SoX.
- `/config` settings
  - 🔴 Added a `/config` note while a cloud session is starting to explain that changes apply to this machine and future cloud sessions.
  - 🔴 Locked the API key, language, permission mode, remote home settings and unattended serving rows in `/config` with `Locked while this cloud session is being created` while a cloud session is being set up.
  - 🟢 Changed the response and voice language editor in `/config` to put the field label, examples and English default hint around the input.
  - 🟢 Clarified why `/config` can’t change automatic updates when they’re set by an environment variable.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/config` warning when a setting change fails and kept the failure reason in the notice.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `/config` warning when model switching fails and kept the failure reason in the notice.
  - 🟢 Changed the fallback hint for invalid custom setting values to say that the value isn’t allowed.
  - 🟢 Changed settings switches to say “on” and “off,” including when a screen reader reads the switch.
  - 🟢 Changed `/config` search to accept multi-character key events so input from IMEs, dictation and fast typing can start filtering.
  - 🟢 Changed the scroll speed and auto-compact window dialogs to a slider that shows every stop on a ruler and moves with ←, →, Home and End or a click in fullscreen, with a compact `‹ value ›` line in small windows.
- Model choice and `/model`
  - 🔴 Shortened the fallback notice's pointer to model switching in `/config`.
  - 🔴 Changed the model-switch failure message in the terminal to say Claude Code couldn't switch models.
  - 🔴 Made the model-version pinning step treat Skip as its decline choice.
  - 🔴 Stopped applying a served default model whose selection source is the person's own setting, except for a claude.ai token passed in through the environment or a file descriptor whose plan can't be read.
  - 🔴 Changed the third-party model upgrade prompt to show the pinned and latest models in aligned rows above the restart confirmation.
  - 🔴 Clarified `/advisor`'s unsupported-model messages and added a select hint to its picker; the experimental override uses `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_ADVISOR_TOOL`.
  - 🟢 Showed keyboard instructions when a model or effort change needs confirmation and there's no draft underneath the dialog.
  - 🟢 Changed the inline model picker to reserve room for the prompt when the terminal isn't using an alternate screen and fullscreen is available.
  - 🟢 Kept the `/model` picker on screen while a selected model takes effect and for the first 150 milliseconds of the PreModelSwitch hook wait, so the panel doesn't go blank between screens when screen-reader mode is off.
- `/effort` and effort levels
  - 🔴 Added effort stepping by keyboard in eligible cloud sessions, behind `tengu_velvety_kay` and, for models that need in-session effort support, `tengu_mutable_flask`, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Kept single-key effort shortcuts from taking plain Up or Down away from Bash input or Vim visual selection, when effort stepping is enabled by `tengu_velvety_kay`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Showed a nondefault effort level on the first render of the terminal's effort indicator, when effort stepping is enabled by `tengu_velvety_kay`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Let the terminal keep an effort change selected while a cloud session is opening, when effort stepping is enabled by `tengu_velvety_kay`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed the model picker's effort line to a slider showing every effort level, with left and right arrows that dim at the lowest and highest level.
  - 🟢 Stopped the effort-level notice from popping up again after leaving and returning to the prompt when the effort hasn't changed.
- Fast mode and `/fast`
  - 🔴 Fixed the `/fast` dialog to hold a mode change requested during cloud startup until the new session can apply it.
  - 🔴 Reworded the `/fast` errors to distinguish a failed change from a `PreModelSwitch` check that didn't finish.
  - 🔴 Changed the `/fast` panel's overload warning to say fast mode is busy and show when the cooldown ends.
  - 🔴 Let the terminal open Fast mode controls while a cloud session is starting instead of immediately saying that cloud sessions can't switch Fast mode.
  - 🔴 Reworded the fast mode overload notice to say fast mode is busy and show when it resets.
  - 🔴 Reworded the fast mode usage limit notice to say fast mode is paused and show when it resets.
- Thinking
  - 🔴 Renamed the thinking toggle to "Thinking mode" with the description "Turn thinking on or off for this session.", replacing "Toggle thinking mode" and "Enable or disable thinking for this session.".
  - 🔴 Changed the extended-thinking picker to say `On` and `Off` and explain the cost of switching mid-conversation in plainer language.
  - 🔴 Added a request for thinking highlights on the first model call after you type a prompt when `CLAUDE_CODE_FIRST_CALL_THINKING_TITLES` is set, thinking is on and `showThinkingSummaries` isn't, on the first-party API only. (behind `tengu_mellow_quill` and `tengu_quirky_tide`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Let the terminal remember a thinking toggle made while a cloud session is opening and send it once the session connects.
  - 🟢 Reworded the notice that changing thinking mode mid-conversation can slow responses and lower their quality.
- Compaction
  - 🔴 Changed the context warning to say `auto-compact is next` when automatic compaction is on and the percentage until it runs reaches zero.
  - 🔴 Sent a prompt that was saved to a file for being too long to manual `/compact` as its first and last 2,000 characters plus the file's path, as normal turns do, instead of in full.
  - 🔴 Shortened the low-context warning's `/compact` hint when automatic compaction is off and manual compaction is available.
  - 🔴 Changed when old tool inputs are cleared as a chat resumes after a long idle, now behind the `tengu_tidy_crayon` flag (off by default) in place of `tengu_mellow_inkwell`: inputs stay when thinking is off, when no note about the cleared results goes with them, when there's no earlier thinking or a quarter or more of it would be lost, and for tools whose calls ended in an error.
  - 🔴 Let auto-compact start its background summary when a turn starts if the conversation is already past the compact threshold, not only during a turn, behind the `tengu_eager_heron` flag, which is off by default; it doesn't start then while usage credits are in use.
  - 🔴 Retried reactive compaction when its summary request comes back with no assistant message, as it already did for an empty summary, while compaction attempts remain. (behind `tengu_indexed_salamander` and `tengu_anchored_finch`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Changed the note Claude gets after compaction in sessions that have the claude.ai `read_conversation` tool: it now says to re-read earlier turns from the first one, paging forward with `next_page_token`, when it needs specific details, instead of explaining how to start from the newest messages.
  - 🔴 Reworked `/autocompact`'s guidance about automatic window sizing and its warnings when auto-compact is off or a larger window may raise token usage.
- Rewind
  - 🔴 Let eligible remote sessions undo a rewind after a usage limit even if compaction happened in the meantime, when `tengu_loyal_otter` is on (off by default) and the saved history still supports the restore.
  - 🔴 Added an error when the remote session service refuses a copied row during a rewind undo after a usage limit; the conversation stays at the rewind point instead of applying a partial restore.
  - 🔴 Raised the limits for rewinding a cloud session to a point before a compaction: the session log it reads may now hold 20,000 events and 384 MiB, up from 10,000 and 128 MiB, and it has 16 seconds from the request to read it, up from 6.
  - 🔴 Changed rewinds behind compaction to refuse when they'd lose a memory withdrawal or leave account memory visible after it was withdrawn.
  - 🔴 Stopped restoring a message a plugin sent on your behalf as a runnable slash command or shell command when it's put back in the prompt, for example after a rewind; its text comes back as a prompt, with a leading `#` when it looks like a command or an exit word.
  - 🔴 Fixed `/rewind` previews to show prompts submitted by plugins as user messages as text, even if they start like shell or slash commands.
  - 🔴 Fixed `/rewind` so selecting a prompt submitted by a plugin as a user message leaves your current permission mode alone.
  - 🔴 Changed `/rewind` to list up to three skipped paths in its partial code-restore warning when file checkpointing is on.
  - 🔴 Clarified that `/rewind`'s `Summarize up to here` keeps the selected message and everything after it unchanged.
  - 🔴 Changed `/rewind`'s summary error to say `Couldn't summarize` before the reason.
  - 🔴 Changed the `/rewind` list to stop wrapping from the last message to the first.
  - 🔴 Fixed `/rewind` to put a plugin's user message back in the draft as text, even if it starts with a slash or exclamation mark.
  - 🔴 Listed prompts that a plugin submits as you (`$.prompt.submit` with `asUser: true`) in the rewind menu, so you can rewind to them like your own prompts; 2.1.325 left them out because they didn't come from a person.
  - 🟢 Changed `/rewind` to show `Code changes unknown`, or note that other files weren't checked, for a turn with file changes it didn't track, such as an edit it couldn't back up, instead of reporting no code changes.
  - 🟢 Added a `/rewind` warning about files changed while a command ran that can't be restored, when file checkpointing and Bash edit tracking are on.
  - 🟢 Fixed fullscreen `/rewind` to fit more past messages into its list.
  - 🟢 Fixed long `/rewind` message previews to fit the terminal width so the restore warning stays visible.
- Resuming sessions
  - 🔴 Rejected `--resume-drops-turn` when its turn range contains a memory withdrawal record, so the withdrawal still applies after resume.
  - 🔴 Changed headless `--resume-session-at` to look up a rewritten message ID when the requested ID isn't in the loaded transcript.
  - 🔴 Counted a bundled skill typed as a slash command with arguments as a session's first or last prompt in `/resume`, instead of skipping it like a built-in command.
  - 🔴 Told the user when `claude --continue` finds only archived sessions, with how to open the most recent one through `--resume`, instead of saying no conversation was found, behind the `tengu_tender_wren` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed `claude --continue` to say when every session it could continue is archived, pointing to `claude --resume` and Ctrl+S to show them, or to the most recent one's `claude --resume <id>`, instead of "No conversation found to continue".
  - 🔴 Added an error for `--fork-session` with `--cloud <session_id>`, or with `--continue` or `--resume` of a cloud session: `--fork-session can't fork a cloud session, so nothing was opened or sent`, with the commands to continue it instead.
  - 🔴 Changed `claude --desktop --continue` to explain when all matching sessions are archived and show how to resume the latest one.
  - 🔴 Reworded the long-session resume prompt to say that a full resume uses more of your usage limit than resuming from a summary.
  - 🔴 Kept a plugin prompt explicitly sent as the user from being crossed when the interactive resume path searches backward for a prompt.
  - 🟢 Reworded the CLI's resume loading and failure messages to refer to sessions rather than conversations.
- `/resume` picker
  - 🔴 Changed `/resume` to track page requests separately for each list of sessions, so switching between the current project's sessions and all projects doesn't have to wait for a page from the old list.
  - 🔴 Fixed `/resume` to catch errors loading another page of sessions and stop the active search from trying to load more after a page fails.
  - 🔴 Changed `/resume` errors to distinguish when to try again from when to pick another session.
  - 🔴 Changed session renaming in the resume picker to use a single-line text field that turns pasted line breaks into spaces.
  - 🟢 Fixed `/resume` search starting with only one character at a time, so text from an input method or fast typing can start a search too.
  - 🟢 Made the session preview in the resume picker scroll in fullscreen mode with the mouse wheel and the arrow, Page Up, Page Down, Home and End keys; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_VIRTUAL_SCROLL` keeps the full-length view.
- `/context`
  - 🔴 Clarified `/context`'s near-limit and auto-compact-off notices: auto-compact summarizes older messages, and without it the session stops at the context limit.
  - 🔴 Changed `/context`'s remote failure message to say it couldn't get usage from the remote session.
  - 🟢 Reworked the `/context` chart and legend to fit narrow conversation panes, including when usage comes from a Remote Control or bridge session.
- `/copy` and `/export`
  - 🔴 Made `/export` use the command's stop signal while it renders the conversation for export.
  - 🔴 Moved `/copy` into a dialog that lists its existing copy and write-to-file keys, and stopped the selection from wrapping at the ends.
  - 🔴 Changed `/copy`'s preference confirmation to explain that future calls skip the picker and that `/config` can bring it back.
  - 🔴 Reworded `/copy`'s write-to-file error to say it couldn't write the file.
  - 🟢 Simplified `/export`'s export-method picker and filename field, with separate key hints for selecting, saving, cancelling and going back.
- Slash command descriptions and `/help`
  - 🔴 Made `help`, `-h` and `--help` after a slash command that answers them, such as `/effort`, wait like an ordinary command instead of running at once while Claude is working.
  - 🟢 Reworded the descriptions of many slash commands, among them `/add-dir`, `/btw`, `/bug`, `/copy`, `/autocompact`, `/context`, `/pause-memory`, `/powerup`, `/resume`, `/setup-bedrock`, `/setup-vertex`, `/status`, `/plan`, `/fork`, `/rewind`, `/export` and `/effort`, and gave `/help` a `[command]` argument hint.
- `/status`
  - 🔴 Added the cloud worker’s Claude Code version to `/status` when it’s available, next to the version running in this terminal.
  - 🔴 Changed `/status` while a cloud session is starting to show that the session ID hasn’t been created yet and use the cloud workspace’s directory once it’s known.
  - 🟢 Reworded the background-session launcher warnings in `/status`, for example "new background sessions won't start until it's restored", and its note on an unusable proxy URL.
  - 🟢 Removed repeated `/status` diagnostics, preferring the longer version of a message when it starts with the same wording.
- Custom status line
  - 🔴 Reported an attached cloud session's own total cost as `cost.total_cost_usd` in the status line command's input, taken from the session's result messages, instead of this terminal's cost.
  - 🟢 Re-ran the status line command when the terminal is resized or the working directory, the session name, or a remote session's URL or cost changes.
  - 🟢 Fixed a configured status line to stay visible when changing to `!` shell input or while a paste is in progress.
- `/doctor` and `claude doctor`
  - 🔴 Changed `/doctor`'s version check to run `claude --version` only at the install that `installMethod` names when it finds more than one launcher, and to report the other `claude` copies without running them.
  - 🟢 Started `claude doctor`'s warning headings and its all-clear line, and `claude setup-token`'s existing-auth warning, with ⚠ or ✔ icons in a terminal.
- `/memory`
  - 🔴 Hid the cloud `/memory` picker's Enter hint when listing files fails or there's no file to open.
  - 🔴 Kept the cursor on the first toggle when you press Up at the top of the local `/memory` settings list with screen-reader mode off.
  - 🟢 Changed the local `/memory` settings list to read toggles as `on` or `off` instead of `true` or `false` in screen-reader mode.
  - 🟢 Warned when `/memory` opens an existing file that isn't writable, distinguishing a read-only filesystem from a file with restricted permissions.
- Away recaps and `/recap`
  - 🔴 Reworded the first away recap's hint about turning recaps off in `/config`.
  - 🔴 Changed `/recap` to use the retry path for an explicit request when the API is overloaded, instead of dropping it like a background request; the direct-request check still uses `tengu_playful_hare` (on by default).
  - 🟢 Stopped generating automatic away recaps on metered accounts when the last reply was billed to usage credits; `/recap` still works.
- `/btw`
  - 🔴 Made the `/btw` panel give way to a new permission prompt while forking into an agent and show the reason if a hook declines the fork, on local non-coordinator sessions that can fork.
  - 🔴 Made Escape or Ctrl+C cancel an in-progress agent fork while the `/btw` panel is open, and showed the result in chat if a permission prompt had already dismissed the panel.
  - 🔴 Changed `/btw`'s waiting display to use an “Answering” spinner.
  - 🔴 Reworded `/btw`'s errors for an empty conversation, a failed session branch and a response the client can't display.
  - 🟢 Added a warning to `/btw` answers that include text alongside an attempted tool call, so the answer doesn't make it look as if the tool ran.
  - 🟢 Changed `/btw`'s retry display to say “retrying…” instead of showing a zero-second countdown.
- `/stats`
  - 🔴 Changed `/stats` to show a status message with the reason on its own line when stats couldn’t load, including “unknown reason” when the failure has no message.
  - 🔴 Changed the `/stats` empty state to tell you stats appear after your first conversation.
  - 🔴 Changed `/stats` to say there was no model usage in the selected period when its model view is empty.
  - 🔴 Changed the “Tokens per day” heading to sentence case in `/stats` and its exported view.
  - 🔴 Changed stats screenshot failures to say the screenshot couldn’t be copied, with the underlying error when available.
  - 🔴 Changed the Linux clipboard error for a stats screenshot to give the `xclip` installation hint in a shorter message.
- Usage limits and automatic continuation
  - 🔴 Refreshed usage data just before a waiting session tries to continue automatically after a usage limit resets.
  - 🔴 Changed the rate-limit notice to refer to the Usage page rather than the endpoint when the request gives a retry time.
  - 🔴 Changed the usage-limit checkpoint message to say progress was saved and show where to resume it, when the `tengu_vellum_anchor` checkpoint flow is enabled; it ships off by default.
  - 🟢 Added a warning when a changed or ended sign-in stops automatic continuation after a usage limit, telling the user to send a prompt to continue.
  - 🟢 Changed the usage-limit message for a team's pooled group budget to say "team's pooled group budget", show when that budget resets, and appear for usage-based billing accounts too.
  - 🟢 Added the reset time to the "requires usage credits" message when a model is blocked because the team's pooled group budget ran out (`It resets …`).
  - 🟢 Kept an upgrade or extra-usage prompt from appearing under an old usage-limit message after the reset, while allowing a prompt that's already on screen to stay put.
- `/usage`, `/cost` and the Usage tab
  - 🔴 Changed the Usage tab to frame plan-limit errors and place the server detail on its own line when the request returns one.
  - 🔴 Reworded the Usage tab’s stale-snapshot notice when it couldn’t refresh cached usage.
  - 🔴 Formatted multiline remote and local session cost as label-and-value rows on the Usage tab when the terminal is wide enough.
  - 🔴 Showed the session cost in `/cost` and `/usage` as an aligned label and value table when every line has a label and fits the window, instead of dim text.
  - 🔴 Renamed the `Total cost` line to `Estimated cost` in the session cost summary that `/cost` and the end-of-session summary show.
  - 🔴 Split the empty attribution state on the Usage tab into a status line and a hint that attribution builds up as you use Claude.
  - 🔴 Changed truncated Usage attribution tables to say how many more rows are hidden.
  - 🔴 Changed the plugin skill footprint note on the Usage tab to distinguish the listing limit from each plugin’s figure before the cap, behind `tengu_lantern_prism`, which is off by default, or `CLAUDE_CODE_LANTERN_PRISM`.
  - 🟢 Changed `/usage` in fullscreen mode to show subscribers' plan limit bars ahead of the session cost; `/cost` still opens on the cost.
- Buying usage credits
  - 🔴 Kept the option to decline buying usage credits available when the purchase prompt would otherwise ignore incoming keys, behind `tengu_ember_latch`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Labeled Enter as `confirm` in the usage credit amount and monthly limit forms and the auto-reload setup, behind `tengu_ember_latch`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Added a `When usage credit balance falls below:` label to the auto-reload threshold field, behind `tengu_ember_latch`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Added the admin request's outcome to the message shown when Claude stops at the usage-credits prompt for a model that needs them: when a team member picks asking their admin, Claude Code sends the request, waiting up to 20 seconds, and puts its result, or `Couldn't send your request to your admin.`, before `Claude stopped here because … now requires usage credits`.
  - 🔴 Prevented the usage-overage notice from using a different account's eligibility result when the `tengu_tallow_quoit_gate` flag is on (off by default).
  - 🟢 Fixed requests to your admin for usage credits reporting `Request sent to your admin…` when the server turned the request down; they now report it wasn't sent, with the server's reason or `Couldn't send your request. Try again later.`, and tell people who have billing access `No request sent, because you have billing access.` with where to manage credits.
  - 🟢 Added a confirmation before removing an existing monthly spending limit from the extra-usage spend-limit prompt; it starts on “No, keep a limit.”
- Usage and cost accounting
  - 🔴 Estimated the output tokens of a streamed reply that stops before the API reports them, from the text and tool input already received, so cost totals for interrupted, dropped, timed-out or non-streaming-retried replies no longer use only the count sent when the reply began. (behind `tengu_mellow_quill` and `tengu_quirky_tide`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Changed the Anthropic API session spending notice to call the $5 figure an estimate.
- Very long messages
  - 🔴 Saved a user message too long for the model's context window to a file Claude can read, sending only its first and last 2,000 characters with a note pointing to the file, behind the `tengu_paper_heron` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Replaced a user message that kept making requests fail as too long with its first and last 2,000 characters and a note asking Claude to tell you and suggest sending it as a file or in parts, behind the `tengu_lucky_crane` flag, which is off by default.
- @-mentioned files
  - 🔴 Told Claude when an @-mentioned file over the token limit was left out because it's too large, giving its size and advice to read it in portions, instead of only naming the mention.
- `/use-case`
  - 🔴 Added a forget option to `/use-case` for sessions with a current choice and more than one Life Sciences use case, clearing the choice so the next request can ask again unless your account default applies first.
  - 🔴 Added a `/use-case` warning when Claude can’t confirm it removed the saved choice, so you know to forget it in a session that still uses it.
  - 🔴 Shortened the warning that an approved use case is no longer available; where the session can pick another one, the warning still points to `/use-case`.
- `/import`
  - 🔴 Updated `/import` to ask what you want to import, identify items it couldn't import automatically, and use the standard dialog key hints.
  - 🔴 Made `/import`'s command-line report use the correct singular or plural for items found in project config.
- `/feedback` and surveys
  - 🔴 Changed the feedback survey's text input to use the dialog's width, with the character count shown below the free-text response.
  - 🔴 Dismissed the feedback survey's transcript-sharing request if you type something other than a survey answer or switch to a teammate's conversation.
  - 🔴 Made the fullscreen feedback prompt a labelled multiline field.
  - 🟢 Dismissed the feedback survey's transcript-sharing request when you send another message, clear the conversation, or resume a different session.
  - 🟢 Kept the report text and cursor position for `/feedback` and `/bug` when the form is restored after Ctrl+O or Ctrl+Z.
  - 🟢 Stopped `/feedback` and `/bug` from sending a report when a space is typed on the final confirmation screen.
- `/privacy-settings` and training consent
  - 🔴 Changed training-consent requests to verify the signed-in account again when OAuth refresh during a request changes the token, behind `tengu_generic_flask` (off by default).
  - 🔴 Skipped the startup training-consent notice when the service can't confirm that the token belongs to the signed-in account, behind `tengu_generic_flask` (off by default).
  - 🔴 Changed the training-consent notice to accept typing only when its choices are fully in view; arrows scroll the notice when the choices are out of view, behind `tengu_generic_flask` (off by default).
  - 🔴 Changed the new startup training-consent notice to focus opt-out after the service can't load or confirm the choice, behind `tengu_generic_flask` (off by default).
  - 🔴 Made `/privacy-settings` show the Enter hint when acknowledging a notice or a settings load error.
  - 🔴 Changed the legacy training notice's keyboard decline to choose `Not now` during the grace period and opt out afterward, while `tengu_generic_flask` is off (the default). (behind `tengu_playful_puzzle`, on by default)
  - 🔴 Changed the guard on privacy prompts to reject stray input just after they open, including when the opening keystroke arrives late.
  - 🔴 Changed the privacy dialog's single-action buttons to handle Enter directly in screen reader mode while keeping their input guard.
  - 🟢 Added a `Saving…` state to the training notice and kept an opt-out in progress from being replaced by an opt-in, on the account-settings route used when `tengu_generic_flask` is off (the default). (behind `tengu_playful_puzzle`, on by default)
  - 🟢 Added save and close feedback to the legacy `/privacy-settings` toggle, and stopped a second toggle while its first save is in flight, while `tengu_generic_flask` and the browser handoff flag `tengu_playful_puzzle` are both off (the default).
  - 🟢 Changed the local account-settings form of `/privacy-settings` to say when it couldn't verify a saved choice or when the previous setting is still in effect, with a prompt to run `/privacy-settings` again; this applies when `tengu_playful_puzzle` and `tengu_generic_flask` are both off, as they are by default.
  - 🟢 Kept focus from moving to the opposite choice in `/privacy-settings` while the selected privacy choice is being saved.
- `/powerup` lessons
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to find a lesson in the Power Ups menu.
  - 🟢 Shortened the Power Ups lessons about file mentions, rewind, tasks, MCP, subagents, Remote Control and fast mode.
- `/remote-env`
  - 🔴 Changed `/remote-env` to tell users when their chosen environment isn't available and direct them to pick another.
  - 🔴 Changed the empty `/remote-env` picker to show a fetch error, with its details, instead of saying there are no environments when the request fails.
- IDE connection and `/ide`
  - 🔴 Reworded the `/ide` project opener, its manual fallback instructions and its connection errors.
  - 🟢 Left the IDE connection out of the failed MCP servers Claude Code reports when another Claude Code session took that IDE over. (behind `tengu_surface_failed_mcp_servers`, on by default)
  - 🟢 Fixed Escape in the `/ide` auto-connect question to dismiss the prompt without completing the IDE selection; two presses of Ctrl+C no longer quit from that unanswered question.
  - 🟢 Changed the IDE onboarding shortcut hint to say that it opens Claude Code.
- VS Code extension
  - 🔴 Stopped the MCP artifact creator from auto-opening documents in the VS Code extension when the built-in Artifact tool offers the open action; open actions are enabled through `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_OPEN_ACTION` or, if unset, `tengu_cobalt_plinth_willow`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added auto-opening for documents created through the MCP artifact tool in the VS Code extension when it replaces the built-in action, the user owns the artifact, and auto-open is enabled; open actions require `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_OPEN_ACTION` or, if unset, `tengu_cobalt_plinth_willow`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made the main local VS Code integration open a newly created cloud document after the creation tool returns.
  - 🔴 Reworded the local IDE extension installation warning to point to `/status` for details.
  - 🟢 Asked the VS Code extension to open newly created Artifacts with an `open_artifact` notification, where 2.1.325 skipped opening them in VS Code; `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_AUTO_OPEN` can still turn auto-open off.
- 🔴 Reworded the Claude Desktop handoff messages for cancelling, missing installations, required updates and errors.
- `/install-github-app`
  - 🔴 Trimmed surrounding whitespace from an API key pasted into the `/install-github-app` setup wizard.
  - 🟢 Changed the error and setup warning screens of `/install-github-app`, and the GitHub CLI scope warning in `/web-setup`, to status lines with ✘ or ⚠ marks; the error screen drops its `Error:` and `Reason:` labels.
  - 🟢 Kept the `/install-github-app` sign-in form open after an incomplete paste, verification code or web address, with instructions under the field so you can correct it without restarting sign-in.
  - 🟢 Masked the pasted sign-in code in `/install-github-app`.
- Sending files to you with `SendUserFile`
  - 🔴 Changed what Claude's told when a plugin package send prompt closes without an answer: the file stays unsent, and when no choice was read Claude can take its cue from the next message when `tengu_humming_squirrel` is on (on by default).
  - 🟢 Changed the secret-key check that runs before Claude sends a zip file such as a plugin with `SendUserFile` to also scan zip-type files inside it, such as Word or Excel files, one level deep, instead of asking about them as unchecked; zips nested deeper are still listed as unchecked. (behind `tengu_humming_squirrel`, on by default)
  - 🟢 Changed the secret-key check for shared plugin packages to read archives one level inside the ZIP, including ZIP-based Office documents, when `tengu_humming_squirrel` is on (on by default).
  - 🟢 Fixed plugin package sharing sometimes refusing a ZIP after a user chose to send it: if the file's details changed during the question, Claude Code checks whether the bytes are still the same when `tengu_humming_squirrel` is on (on by default).
- Claude Design tool
  - 🔴 Changed Claude Design to treat `put_conversation` as a potentially destructive write when checking whether to ask for approval; the Design tool's `tengu_omelette_fouet` flag is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added UUID validation for Claude Design project, chat, and design-system IDs outside `finalize_plan`, when the tool's `tengu_omelette_fouet` flag is on, off by default.
  - 🔴 Added Claude Design input checks that require `synced_through_idx` to be a nonnegative safe integer and `append` to be a boolean, when `tengu_omelette_fouet` is on, off by default.
  - 🔴 Kept Claude Design's approval prompts for sensitive writes with a `chat_id` on the local display, when `tengu_omelette_fouet` is on, off by default.
- `claude purge`
  - 🔴 Made `claude purge` accept Escape or Ctrl+C just before opening a picker on stderr, so a key pressed as the prompt opens can still stop the purge.
  - 🟢 Changed the `claude purge` project picker to ask which project's data to delete.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude purge` to report that nothing was deleted when you cancel before deletion, and to report how many items were deleted if you stop partway through.
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude purge` errors for trying to combine `--all` with a project path or `--interactive`.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `claude purge` error for an unwritable `.claude.json` and its summary when some items couldn't be deleted.
- Command-line options
  - 🔴 Reworded errors for `--cloud`, `--teleport`, `--from-pr` and `--resume` without a terminal to say "run in an interactive terminal" instead of "run from a TTY", and said `--bg` "can't be combined" with `--cloud` or `--environment`.
  - 🟢 Added a startup warning when an `--allowedTools`, `--disallowedTools` or `--tools` entry doesn't look like a complete rule or a tool name, such as a rule missing its `)`, naming the first one and how many more there are.
  - 🟢 Reworded most `claude --help` option descriptions to be shorter; they now also say that `--bare` and `--safe-mode` keep the hooks of your organization's managed plugins and that `--await-initialize` also applies `mcpServers`.
  - 🟢 Reworded several command-line messages: `--session-id with --continue or --resume also needs --fork-session.`, `--agent-id, --agent-name, and --team-name must be used together`, `Couldn't start Claude in Chrome.`, `Unknown input format`, `--no-session-persistence only works with --print.`, `--plan-mode-instructions only works with --print.`, the start-with-`claude` tip, and the `Global options:` heading in `--help`.
- Sign-in screens and messages
  - 🔴 Added the organization gateway sign-in to the login screen opened from the agents view.
  - 🔴 Changed the remote sign-in error to say `Couldn't authenticate · try again`.
  - 🔴 Added the token revocation link to the success screen for `claude setup-token`.
  - 🔴 Blocked Ctrl+C and Ctrl+D for the first 30 seconds after a browser sign-in code arrives when the flow normally lets you continue without signing in.
  - 🟢 Renamed the sign-in progress panel from “Authentication” to “Sign-in.”
  - 🟢 Shortened the `/login` notice for an existing `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` without changing what it says to do.
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude auth login` and `claude auth logout` messages, for example "Couldn't log in:" and "--console and --claudeai can't be used together.", adding ✘ or ✔ icons in a terminal, and reworded the `claude agents` and deep-link terminal errors.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude auth login`'s paste-the-code prompt to show the code as you paste it, to explain what's wrong when you paste a link, a local address, a verification code or part of the code, to print "Signing in…" once a code is accepted and ignore further input, and to exit with status 130 on Ctrl+C.
  - 🟢 Changed browser sign-in to show a masked code field beside the copyable sign-in link, with explicit paste instructions when Claude Code can't receive the code from the browser automatically.
  - 🟢 Changed the browser sign-in message for a failed code exchange to ask you to try signing in again.
  - 🟢 Changed the cloud gateway connection prompt to show Enter to connect and, when the login method isn't locked, Escape to go back.
  - 🟢 Made an HTTP or HTTPS cloud gateway sign-in link use the same copy controls as the regular browser sign-in link.
  - 🟢 Added a “Finishing sign-in” spinner once Claude Code has received the browser's sign-in code.
  - 🟢 Clarified the startup sign-in message when sign-in succeeds but Claude Code can't restart, telling the user to start it again the same way.
  - 🟢 Made Escape go back one screen while first-run sign-in waits for the browser or a pasted code, clearing typed input first if needed.
- Unused `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` notices
  - 🔴 Changed the conflicting-credentials notice to point to `Use custom API key` in `/config` instead of signing out and back in when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is the API key source.
  - 🔴 Rechecked API-key verification after a credentials change makes `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` eligible or ineligible for use in the current session.
  - 🔴 Stopped `/login` from automatically retrying an authentication failure when the session still uses the same `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` after sign-in.
  - 🟢 Added hints when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set but not in use: `/status` shows an `Unused key` row, the `Not logged in` error adds `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set but not in use · /config to use it`, and when an approved key still outranks a new `/login`, the result says `Signed in, but requests still use ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and to turn off "Use custom API key" in `/config`.
  - 🟢 Added a sign-in picker notice that `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set but isn't being used, with `/config` as the place to enable it, when the picker can't continue without sign-in and there isn't a managed sign-in method or organization.
  - 🟢 Fixed the sign-in notice to refresh after enabling an eligible `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` in `/config`.
- Account switching and stored logins
  - 🔴 Made Escape choose Exit in the account-switch session dialog, which is behind `tengu_bubbly_fountain` (off by default).
  - 🔴 Changed the account-switch check to clear a pending copy prompt if the current identity is missing and there's no sign-in to use, behind `tengu_bubbly_fountain` (off by default).
- Amazon Bedrock and Google Vertex AI setup
  - 🔴 Made Escape go back while the Bedrock setup wizard is reading AWS profiles or verifying credentials.
  - 🔴 Made Escape go back while the Vertex AI setup wizard is reading gcloud projects or verifying credentials.
  - 🔴 Removed visible option numbers from the Bedrock wizard's credential-verification choices.
  - 🔴 Stopped the Vertex AI wizard's gcloud project list from wrapping from one end to the other when you navigate with the arrow keys.
  - 🔴 Removed visible option numbers from the Vertex AI wizard's credential-verification choices.
  - 🟢 Restyled the Amazon Bedrock and Google Vertex AI setup steps for the API key, region and service-account key file, and the cloud gateway's connecting and error screens: fields are labeled, a missing value shows as an inline error, and Esc hints use the shared key-hint style.
  - 🟢 Changed the Bedrock setup wizard to use the full Amazon Bedrock name in its default welcome text.
  - 🟢 Reworded the Bedrock setup wizard's API-key errors for a rejected key, an unreachable region, or another failure during the test.
  - 🟢 Reworded the Bedrock setup wizard's AWS errors for loading a named profile, credentials AWS won't accept, and a region the wizard can't reach.
  - 🟢 Changed the Bedrock wizard's manual AWS profile field to show validation errors in the field and clear them as soon as you edit the name.
  - 🟢 Clarified that you can continue with the built-in model IDs when Bedrock setup finds no Anthropic inference profiles in your region.
  - 🟢 Reworded the Vertex AI setup wizard's errors for a region it can't reach and a model test that fails even though the credentials work.
  - 🟢 Clarified the Vertex AI setup wizard's next steps when a service-account key has expired or the credentials don't identify a Google Cloud project.
  - 🟢 Changed the Vertex AI wizard's manual project ID field to show validation errors in the field and clear them as soon as you edit the ID.
- API request retries
  - 🔴 Stopped background side requests from retrying once with another dispatch header after an HTTP 529 overload error, so they give up on it the way background main requests already did. (behind `tengu_tidy_swing`, on by default)
  - 🔴 Changed API request retries that follow a server's `retry-after` header to wait up to 25% longer than the header asks, picked at random; prompt and agent hooks still wait exactly the header's time.
- Failed turns and API errors
  - 🔴 Shortened Claude's service retry message to `Wait a moment and try again.`
  - 🔴 Changed failed-turn status to use the API error only when it's the most recent conversational message.
  - 🟢 Let narrow terminals wrap context-limit and API timeout messages instead of forcing them onto one line, while preserving the `API_TIMEOUT_MS` tip when set.
- Settings files
  - 🔴 Kept using the last copy of a settings file that Claude Code read successfully when a later reread fails with a read error, instead of dropping that file's settings, unless the error is that the file grew too large to read; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_foamy_pebble` flag, which is on by default.
- Settings file errors and limits
  - 🔴 Changed the invalid settings dialog's keyboard decline to exit.
  - 🔴 Made Claude's edits to a Claude Code `settings.json` fail validation when any value is nested more than 64 levels deep, naming the keys whose nesting to reduce.
  - 🟢 Added line and column numbers to errors in the invalid settings dialog that don't name a setting.
  - 🟢 Removed the blank line after each suggestion in the invalid settings dialog.
  - 🟢 Added space between groups of errors in the invalid settings dialog.
  - 🟢 Added line and column numbers to the settings problem panel when the parser supplies them, and renamed its file badges to “Has errors” and “Has warnings.”
  - 🟢 Added a 64-level nesting limit to settings files, managed settings and SDK inline settings: anything nested deeper is ignored with a warning naming its top-level key (for a settings file, also left out if Claude Code saves a setting there), `hooks` written as nested arrays that deep is a fatal error, and a document with more than 2,000,000 objects fails with `settings document has too many objects to copy`.
  - 🟢 Shortened the error Claude gets when an edit leaves Claude Code's `settings.json` invalid: it now includes the schema for only the keys the edit changed, plus the list of valid top-level keys when the edit used an unknown one, instead of the full settings schema.
  - 🟢 Simplified the invalid-JSON settings dialog by naming the file directly and dropping the extra heading above the recovery choices.
- 🔴 Included text typed before Claude Code finished starting in the `Your message was not sent` notice shown when you decline your organization's managed settings and Claude Code exits.
- Lean rewrite prompts
  - 🔴 Removed the shorter texts that the lean rewrite (`tengu_temporal_hare`) gave some models for the ToolSearch description and the system prompt's model line; every model now gets the full texts.
  - 🔴 Removed the alternative "lean rewrite" system prompt, with its own sections on communicating, delivering work, corrections, subagents and the session's surface and its own tool descriptions; it applied on top of the lean prompt when the model had the `temporal_hare` capability (from the service, the model catalog or `CLAUDE_CODE_MODEL_CAPABILITIES`), or for Opus 5.5 with the `tengu_temporal_hare` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Removed the alternate auto-memory guidance that models on the rewritten lean prompts got ("Keep the memories free of extraneous material…"), so those models now get the same memory guidelines as others.
- 🔴 Behind the `tengu_glowing_goose` flag, which is off by default, told Claude to read as much of a PDF the person names as the request needs and to search all of its pages for a single fact, instead of starting with the first few pages.
- Bash tool
  - 🔴 Added a `view_images` input to the Bash tool for image files a command writes, which then come back with the result, behind the `tengu_goofy_sunbeam` flag, which is off by default; while it's off, a `view_images` value is dropped and the result says no pictures were shown.
  - 🔴 Limited Bash commands in Anthropic-hosted cloud sessions to a pids cgroup of their own, capped 1,024 below the session's process limit so runaway commands can't take every process slot; on by default through the `tengu_roomy_burrow` flag, and if setup fails the commands run under the session's limit alone.
  - 🟢 Made `grep` in Bash commands, which Claude Code maps to its bundled ugrep, read files as UTF-8.
- Performance and resource use
  - 🔴 Added uploading the unchanged start of the next request while you type a follow-up, then sending the rest when you press Enter if nothing else changed, behind the `tengu_playful_shannon` flag, which is off by default; it applies only to local interactive sessions on the Anthropic API with no custom base URL or proxy.
  - 🟢 Slowed token-count updates for background subagents whose transcript isn't open from every half second to every 5 seconds while more than 20 subagents are running.
  - 🟢 Stopped Claude Desktop sessions from holding the end of a turn for the session-status summary when your next message is already waiting; the summary call is cut short instead, behind the `tengu_dusky_paddock` flag, which is on by default.
- 🔴 Reworded the notice after clearing a conversation to call it a new session.
- 🔴 Refused to run a tool call that arrives with no `tool_use` id, or with an id in a form Claude Code keeps for its own calls (such as plugin, MCP-call and `confirm_` ids), unless a hook or the client issued it; the call fails with `TOOL_USE_ID_REFUSED`.
- 🔴 Changed the LSP tool's failure line in the transcript from "LSP operation failed" to "Couldn't query the language server".
- 🔴 Handled files that come back shorter than expected while Claude Code reads their first and last lines for a hover preview, returning what it read instead of reading again past the end.
- 🔴 Changed the connection message for an SSH session that ends before connecting and for a direct remote-server connection that couldn't be opened.
- 🔴 Reworded the cross-session message approval warnings when messaging gets switched off or when the held message has already been released or dropped.
- 🔴 Gave eligible cloud attachment downloads one extra try when their first HTTP 429 or 503 includes `Retry-After`, then used later headers to choose retry waits of up to eight seconds within the download's time limit; `tengu_unhurried_wren` is off by default.
- 🔴 Kept checking Bash commands against sandbox ask and deny rules when automatic approval for sandboxed commands is temporarily paused.
- 🔴 Kept the full project description and prompt when shortening them would trigger another automatic compaction, on the large-project path that requires positive `tengu_nimble_quarry` and `tengu_spare_attic` values; both default to zero and `tengu_imperative_whistle` is off by default.
- 🔴 Let `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_ADVISOR_TOOL` skip the Advisor model-rank check even when the configured and active main models are from different families.
- 🔴 Showed the active Claude account's email in interactive connector file-send approvals when the session can read an email that's safe to display.
- 🔴 Changed the usage screen's error to “Couldn't load usage data” when it can't fetch your usage.
- 🔴 Made session and peer name matching use the shared Unicode normalizer, which handles long runs of combining marks in pieces instead of sending the whole name through one normalization call.
- 🔴 Made the `ultrathink` boost honor the selected model's supplied per-turn effort capability when it's available, while `tengu_turtle_carbon` is on, as it is by default, and `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ULTRATHINK` isn't set.
- 🔴 Fixed remote file reads after a Remote Control session changes project folders: a path under the former folder can follow the session to its current folder, and the read still checks deny and ask rules on the original path.
- 🔴 Changed the Remote Control diagnostics to show “Organization found” or “Organization not found” when they check the organization.
- 🔴 Fixed Up-arrow prompt recall after clearing a draft with Escape twice so it offers the draft before a saved prompt with the same timestamp.
- 🔴 Fixed conversation merging so a malformed tool result without a string tool-call ID doesn't throw while Claude Code checks where to place the next message.
- 🔴 Used the authenticated account to name the copy destination in the account-switch dialog and the notice after copying, when it differs from the locally stored account; `tengu_bubbly_fountain` ships off.
- 🔴 Let a compaction summary that finished after Stop be reused without swallowing unanswered messages, when `tengu_mended_kite` is on, off by default, and the summary left every unanswered message available to carry forward separately.
- 🔴 Told Claude which newly listed MCP tools belong to connected claude.ai apps when the deferred-tool update lists changes rather than sending a full inventory.
- 🔴 Kept a subagent's report through compaction when it arrived beside a tool result before the cut and hadn't reached Claude in a tool reply yet.
- 🔴 Let the newer file-mention reader reserve part of a context window over 200,000 tokens when `tengu_brimming_kettle` supplies a token reserve, which defaults to zero, instead of requiring the separate `tengu_snuggly_goblet` flag.
- 🔴 Let the newer file-mention reader offer up to 20 line ranges beyond the first lines when a text file won't fit, with `tengu_luminous_truffle` on, off by default, and a `tengu_mossy_satchel` size allowance large enough for the file, which defaults to zero.
- 🔴 Kept a tool call in the foreground when it has applicable result, failure or batch hooks, or if checking for those hooks fails; moving tools to the background requires the `tengu_tool_detach` flag, which is on by default.
- 🔴 Kept a cloud session's startup question in message-typing mode after you've typed and deleted the text, so the next key doesn't start answering the question as if you hadn't typed.
- 🔴 Showed plugin-submitted prompts as plain user text in the `claude attach` preview, rather than interpreting text that looks like shell input or hiding text that looks like a generated reminder.
- 🔴 Kept a terminal component clipped entirely off the left edge from making other text unselectable, by treating its selection exclusion area as empty.
- 🔴 Counted a frame's starting row as well as its height when deciding whether it reaches the bottom of the terminal, including when switching in or out of fullscreen or redrawing after resize.
- 🔴 Skipped the new renderer's Apple Terminal check for an externally cleared fullscreen display when the window is narrower than 17 columns; the renderer uses `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or `tengu_idempotent_willow`, off by default.
- 🔴 Replaced control characters with a visible replacement character in paths shown by the missing or inaccessible working-directory error.
- 🔴 Let background workflow agents change the task list even when the `tengu_gentle_pike` flag blocks other background agents from doing so in eligible hosted sessions; the flag is off by default.
- 🔴 Made Claude Projects count the current document text shown by a refused overwrite as a read, so Claude can use that text in its next write; the text appears with `tengu_lovely_rain` and `tengu_candid_lantern` on, and `tengu_lucky_nebula` on for remote bridge calls, all off by default.
- 🔴 Made Claude Projects refresh the document at a previously read path when a different document now uses that name and the old document still exists, with `tengu_floating_bumblebee` on, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Skipped loading project document text into Claude's context from Projects with only documents and more than 50,000 tokens of knowledge, unless the request refreshes selected documents; this runs with auto-compaction enabled and `tengu_lovely_rain` and `tengu_peppy_spindle` on, both off by default.
- 🔴 Let Claude Projects shorten attached instructions on conversations with over 200,000 tokens of room when quick refills are about to stop auto-compaction or the instructions were already shortened, with `tengu_imperative_whistle` on and `tengu_nimble_quarry` above zero, both off by default.
- 🔴 Left missing text blank in terminal elements that render ANSI formatting, instead of printing `undefined` or `null` as text.
- 🔴 Rendered ANSI styling in highlighted terminal notice segments, including segmented prompt notices.
- 🔴 Rendered ANSI styling in plain-text terminal prompt notices as well as notices already split into styled segments.
- 🔴 Asked you to approve a Bash command a remote session wants to run on this computer when the host can't rule out protected credential access, even if the usual permissions would allow it; this applies outside Windows.
- 🔴 Stopped finished hook notices from holding on to their largest measured height while offscreen in the terminal transcript.
- 🔴 Changed the terminal's critical-memory warning to separate the memory reading from its restart advice with a middle dot.
- 🔴 Lowercased the shortcut in the terminal footer's view hint when the Remote Control badge is selected.
- 🔴 Shortened the Vim hint in the empty prompt-history search to `esc i / for commands`.
- 🔴 Reworded the read-only cloud `/skills` panel to say when the session hasn't reported skills or loaded none, point to local `/skills` for toggles, and use a shared Esc hint in place of the old navigation footer.
- 🔴 Shortened the message for an ambiguous `/resume` argument to show the match count and say `Run /resume to pick one.`
- 🔴 Reworded the terminal's open-in-browser screen when it has no link: outside a cloud session it gives a marked warning to start one with `claude --cloud`, and for a directly connected session it says only sessions started that way have a browser link.
- 🔴 Added a check mark to the confirmation when a provider change is ready and Claude Code asks you to restart.
- 🔴 Added Enter to view and Esc to close hints to the terminal release-notes picker.
- 🔴 Added a spinner and shorter `Loading guest passes…` message while the terminal waits for guest passes.
- 🔴 Fixed the `/permissions` user-setting location label to read `Saved in ~/.claude/settings.json`.
- 🔴 Simplified the `/permissions` Workspace list to show directory paths without numbered labels or an extra dash.
- 🔴 Changed the terminal plan panel's heading from `Current Plan` to `Current plan`.
- 🔴 Kept Skip available in `/install-github-app` while the keyboard and click guard filters unintended input, both when a workflow already exists and after the app is installed; the guard's `tengu_cheeky_newt` flag is off by default.
- 🔴 Reworded GitHub Actions setup errors to tell you to remove or update an existing Claude workflow yourself, and to check that you have repository admin access when GitHub refuses the operation.
- 🔴 Reworded cloud `/memory`'s denied-read error to explain that the session shares only files inside its workspace.
- 🔴 Reworded the cloud `/memory` note for files sent from this machine to say changes apply in the next cloud session or when this machine reconnects.
- 🔴 Changed cloud `/memory` previews to show unreadable files with an error mark and the reason on a detail line, and to show extra preview rows with a `+N` count.
- 🔴 Added spinners while feedback drafts load or send and reworded the empty queue in `/feedback` and `/bug` when `tengu_juniper_relay` is on, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Reworded `/fork`'s refusal when the current session isn't being saved: it tells you to run the task here or fork from a session that saves its transcript.
- 🔴 Changed `/hooks` to say `No hooks for this event yet` when an event is empty, keeping the hint about adding hooks.
- 🔴 Shortened the `/hooks` result when an answer applies only to the current session because it couldn't be saved.
- 🔴 Reworded the `/ide` confirmation for turning off automatic connections to ask `Stop connecting to your IDE automatically?`.
- Bash permission checks
  - 🟢 Changed the approval prompt for shell commands Claude Code can't parse reliably and that don't look like removals to say it can't be sure it read the command the way the shell will, so it can't rule out a dangerous removal, and to tell Claude not to go ahead if it can't tell what the command does.
  - 🟢 Denied shell commands longer than the permission check reads when you have deny rules with patterns for shell or file tools, saying the command couldn't be checked against your deny rules and to use shorter values or put the long text in a file.
  - 🟢 Asked before running a sandboxed Bash command that its subcommand checks had allowed when Claude Code couldn't finish parsing it, saying it couldn't finish checking the command. (behind `bot_protected_tengu_zazzy_parasol`, on by default)
  - 🟢 Stopped counting `NAME+=value` appends in Bash commands as safe variable prefixes, so a command that appends to a variable isn't auto-approved as read-only or by the sandbox, when the `bot_protected_tengu_reflective_ember` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Stopped auto-approving a sandboxed Bash command that sets `GIT_SSH_COMMAND`, `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL`, `GIT_CONFIG_COUNT`, a `GIT_CONFIG_KEY_` or `GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_` variable, or `SRT_SSH_PROXY_COMMAND`, which can make git run a program; Claude Code now asks first.
  - 🟢 Added `sandbox-exec` to the wrapper commands that Bash permission checks look through, skipping its `-f`, `-n`, `-p` and `-D` options so the command it runs is checked like any other.
  - 🟢 Gave the Bash checks that look for paths outside the working directory a budget of 32,768 path segments per command; once it's spent, the remaining paths count as outside, so the command needs approval instead of taking long to check.
  - 🟢 Counted shell commands with unquoted globs that could match Claude Code's settings files, or `set` options that change how globs expand, as possibly writing those settings in the check that guards them; the `bot_protected_tengu_fuzzy_coral` flag is on by default.
  - 🟢 Made the Bash permission checker treat more commands as ones it can't check before they run, so they need approval: a shell keyword or `{` after leading variable assignments or redirects (it caught only `[[` or `{` after a redirect), quotes or backslashes inside a `${…}` expansion that zsh can read differently, an argument, variable value or redirect target longer than 10,000 characters, and variable expansions adding up to more than 65,536 characters.
  - 🟢 Stopped Bash allow rules that end in `*` from matching when the command extends the rule's last word with `..`, a hidden-file glob, `$` or a backtick, or into a path Claude Code protects, so such commands ask first.
- Protection of Claude Code's sign-in files
  - 🟢 Added an approval prompt for `ln` and `link` commands that would put a link at, or reach, a path Claude Code's own tools may never write, explaining that a link gives the file a second name and later writes to that name change it.
  - 🟢 Added a safety check that stops tools from writing Claude Code's sign-in tokens (`.credentials.json`), the keys that identify the computer (`.device-keys.json`) and the lock taken while saving tokens (`.storage-write.lock`), including through Windows short names or hard links; auto mode's classifier can't approve these writes.
  - 🟢 Asked before running an `rm`, `mv` or `cp` command that names a folder holding Claude Code's sign-in files, such as `~/.claude/.credentials.json`, saying the command reaches a file Claude Code's tools can't write.
- Permission checks that fail with an error
  - 🟢 Started the refusal in sessions where nobody can approve a call, such as non-interactive runs, with an explanation when the tool's own permission check failed with an error.
- `claude plugin` commands
  - 🟢 Gave disabled plugins a circle and nested validation details a dot in terminal output; redirected output keeps the old cross and pointer.
  - 🟢 Changed interactive `claude plugin` output to use bullets for detail rows, a circle for disabled plugins, and shorter success lines that drop “Successfully.”
  - 🟢 Changed plugin CLI errors for unrecognized `--scope` values to call out the unknown value and list the allowed choices.
  - 🟢 Clarified that `claude plugin marketplace add --sparse` works only with GitHub or Git marketplace sources.
  - 🟢 Clarified that `claude plugin disable --all` can’t be used with a plugin name.
  - 🟢 Shortened the LSP section’s note in `claude plugin details` to say that language servers have no model context cost.
- `claude plugin eval`
  - 🟢 Clarified how to start the `claude plugin eval init` interview in a terminal or write a blank template instead when the interview can’t run there.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude plugin eval init` to say the requested case name won’t work and give the reason when it rejects that name.
  - 🟢 Changed the `claude plugin eval init` launch error to say it couldn’t start the interview and include the cause.
  - 🟢 Changed the interrupted `claude plugin eval` notice to say it stopped and is finishing up.
  - 🟢 Changed the failed case-file notice in `claude plugin eval` to say how many files it couldn’t load, with singular or plural wording on both output streams.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `claude plugin eval` warning for results that don’t match its output schema and kept the request to report the bug.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude plugin eval --json` to say it needs a file path when the argument is empty.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude plugin eval --report` to say it needs a file path when the argument is empty.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude plugin eval --runs` to ask for a whole number above zero when the count is invalid.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude plugin eval --max-cost-usd` to ask for a number of zero or more when the cap is invalid.
  - 🟢 Clarified that `claude plugin eval --no-publish` and `--publish-report` can’t be used together.
- 🟢 Ignored `Content-Length` and `Transfer-Encoding` headers set in an HTTP hook's config, with a warning, since Claude Code works out the request's length from its body.
- MCP server sign-in
  - 🟢 Fixed `c` copying a sign-in link in `/mcp` without inserting a `c` into the redirect field.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude mcp login` to ask for an interactive terminal when it needs a pasted sign-in redirect and standard input isn’t a terminal.
  - 🟢 Changed `/mcp` sign-in screens to use a shared browser-link view and, when the server needs a pasted redirect, a labeled field for it.
  - 🟢 Changed the claude.ai disconnect prompt in `/mcp` to show its browser steps and fallback link in the new sign-in layout.
  - 🟢 Added a `c` shortcut to copy the fallback sign-in link for agent-only MCP servers when the browser doesn't open.
- `claude mcp` commands
  - 🟢 Reworded the `claude mcp add-from-claude-desktop` picker to explain that servers whose names are taken get numbers added when imported.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude mcp add-from-claude-desktop` to say the Claude Desktop config is missing or has no MCP servers when there’s nothing to import.
  - 🟢 Changed MCP configuration commands to show status icons in supported terminals and use plain text when output is redirected, including for server removal and resetting project approvals.
  - 🟢 Clarified the error when an MCP server you add has an invalid configuration: it now says when the JSON isn't an object, a `url` has no `type`, the `mcpServers` wrapper was left in, a `command` or `type` is missing, or the `type` is unknown.
  - 🟢 Reworded the `claude mcp add-json` warning when the server was added but secure storage couldn’t save its client secret.
- `claude mcp serve`
  - 🟢 Clarified that `claude mcp serve --port` only works with `--transport http`.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude mcp serve` to ask for a whole number from 0 to 65535 when the port is invalid.
- MCP server questions
  - 🟢 Changed MCP to report when a server's question was declined and the tool call then failed, instead of showing an ordinary tool error.
  - 🟢 Added Tab and Shift+Tab navigation between fields in terminal MCP forms.
  - 🟢 Stopped marking an empty required field as answered in terminal MCP forms.
- 🟢 Changed plugin uninstall to remove extracted MCP bundle files that no other installed plugin uses right away, instead of leaving them for the age-based cleanup; nothing is removed when `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_USE_ZIP_CACHE` or `CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_MCPB_DIR` is set.
- `managed-mcp.json` added mid-session
  - 🟢 Added a warning when your organization's `managed-mcp.json` arrives while Claude Code is running and is applied, saying only the servers it allows will load from now on, or that your own servers won't load if the file can't be read; `-p` sessions print it to stderr when that's a terminal, or send it as a `system` message with `--output-format stream-json`.
  - 🟢 Picked up a `managed-mcp.json` that your organization adds while Claude Code is running, with a notice that only the servers it allows will load from now on, or that your own servers won't load because the file can't be read; if the account running Claude Code could have written the file, it's ignored until a restart.
- 🟢 Forwarded top-level partial responses to a Remote Control client when `tengu_bridge_partial_messages` is on (off by default) and `tengu_breezy_blanket` stays on (on by default).
- Built-in `gh` and GitHub access
  - 🟢 Reworded Claude’s GitHub login failure to point to the GitHub App sign-in.
  - 🟢 Reworded the GitHub setup notice when `gh` isn’t logged in, keeping both the command-line sign-in and web connection options.
- Background sessions routed through an LLM gateway
