  - 🟢 Made background sessions started from a shell with an LLM gateway stop before sending any model request if they come back without it, for example because a setting such as `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, `*_BASE_URL` or `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_*` took them off it or the background service lacks the gateway's variables, showing "No LLM gateway: session stopped." with steps to fix it; the `tengu_bg_gateway_mark` flag, on by default, turns this on.
  - 🟢 Stopped background sessions started from a shell that routes through an LLM gateway from going on without it: when one comes back up without the gateway, the background service marks it stopped and its row explains how to reopen it on the gateway, and `claude attach` tries such a session again from your shell. This applies while the `tengu_bg_gateway_mark` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Stopped background sessions that were routed through an LLM gateway from starting without it: when the gateway's variables are missing, startup waits for the organization's managed settings and then exits with `No LLM gateway: session stopped. No model request was sent.`
  - 🟢 Made `claude respawn` refuse to restart a background session that runs through an LLM gateway when neither your settings nor your current shell in that session's folder provide the gateway, leaving the session running and saying where to set the gateway's variables; `claude respawn --all` counts these separately. This applies while the `tengu_bg_gateway_mark` flag is on, as it is by default.
- Terminal program status (OSC 7501)
  - 🟢 Changed `claude attach` to ask the terminal whether it supports program-status reports (OSC 7501), pass the session's status through when it does and strip it when it doesn't, and clear the status when you leave.
  - 🟢 Fixed terminals that support the Program Status Protocol (OSC 7501) showing no status for a background session opened with `claude attach`: the background service now sends the session's status to attached viewers that ask for it.
  - 🟢 Let a viewer attached to a background session ask for terminal program status (state, progress and title) even when the terminal doesn't support it, unless `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_TERMINAL_TITLE` is set.
- Spinner and progress display
  - 🟢 Changed the spinner's stalled tint to use the theme's error color instead of a fixed red, and to skip the tint when that color can't be read as RGB.
  - 🟢 Changed the spinner's shimmer, and the colors it shifts to when progress stalls, to follow the current theme's inactive, warning and error colors instead of fixed grays and red.
  - 🟢 Hid the progress spinner's tip when there's already reply text and there aren't active tools, so it doesn't flash back at the end of a reply.
  - 🟢 Restored the busy indicator if a streamed reply is cut off while the turn is still running, even when part of that reply remains on screen.
- Terminal progress indicator
  - 🟢 Hid the terminal progress indicator while Claude waits for you to answer.
  - 🟢 Refreshed Ghostty's indeterminate terminal progress indicator every five seconds while work is running.
  - 🟢 Hid the indeterminate terminal progress indicator when the terminal is suspended and restored it on resume.
- API retry status display
  - 🟢 Added the API retry status (countdown, attempt count, rate-limit reset) to WebSearch's progress line while its search request is being retried, in interactive sessions.
  - 🟢 Showed API retry status, such as the retry countdown, in the view of a subagent running as a task in an interactive session.
  - 🟢 Fixed the interactive API retry banner so its countdown updates once a second while it's visible, even when the rest of the screen isn't updating; screen-reader mode skips that timer.
  - 🟢 Changed the API retry banner to say it's retrying once the countdown reaches zero, while still showing the attempt number.
  - 🟢 Made the API retry banner let the status and reason text use all the width beside the icon, so the text can wrap in narrow terminals.
- Markdown rendering
  - 🟢 Fixed backslashes disappearing from Windows paths in Claude's replies before a folder name that starts with punctuation, such as `C:\Users\me\.claude` or `..\_build`.
- 🟢 Changed notifications that last an hour or more to step aside for a newly arrived notification and come back after it, instead of keeping it from showing until they time out.
- Desktop notifications
  - 🟢 Ended kitty desktop-notification escape sequences with ST instead of BEL even when the terminal isn't detected as kitty, such as inside tmux, except inside GNU screen.
  - 🟢 Updated the terminal bell and iTerm2-with-bell labels in the notification channel chooser.
- tmux
  - 🟢 Stopped wrapping small screen updates, under 1,024 bytes, in synchronized-update markers inside tmux; larger updates and other terminals are unchanged.
  - 🟢 Removed the fullscreen tmux notice that suggested `set -g mouse on` for wheel scrolling, along with the `tmux show -Av mouse` check behind it.
- Unavailable slash commands
  - 🟢 Added a Features row to local `/status` when your provider or an environment variable such as `DISABLE_TELEMETRY` makes some commands unavailable, with a link to what’s supported.
- Output-token limits
  - 🟢 Remembered, for the rest of the session, a lower output-token limit for a model after the provider rejects a request's `max_tokens` for it, and capped later requests' default and maximum output tokens at that limit; changing provider or provider settings clears it.
- `/design-login`
  - 🟢 Kept `/design-login` on the sign-in screen after a bad paste, with separate guidance for a web address, a verification code, and an incomplete sign-in code, when organization policy allows Design sign-in on a first-party account.
  - 🟢 Moved `/design-login`'s browser fallback into a sign-in panel with a copyable URL and a masked code field, where organization policy allows Design sign-in on a first-party account.
- `claude update` and installs
  - 🟢 Reworded the update error for a WSL install that uses Windows npm to "Windows npm found in WSL", saying that installation can't be updated, and the message for a shell config file whose old `claude` alias couldn't be removed to "Couldn't clean up …".
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude update` messages, for example "Couldn't check for updates" and "No permission to install the update", and started its results with ✔, ✘ or ⚠ in a terminal instead of "Error:" or "Warning:".
  - 🟢 Shortened the installer's progress and failure messages, including the one shown while another Claude Code install is already running.
  - 🟢 Reworded the updater's status lines: `installed:`, `latest:` and `current:` labels, `Update installed · restart to apply`, `Update available · run …`, `Checking for updates…`, and `Couldn't update or restore … — a copy is at:`.
  - 🟢 Shortened the startup update summary to `Updated:` with a direct count of changes, or `Updated to latest` if there's nothing to count.
  - 🟢 Clarified the two choices in the stable update channel prompt: use stable even if it’s older, or stay on the current version until stable catches up.
- Message wording across commands
  - 🟢 Reworded messages across `/effort`, `/advisor`, `/fast`, `/branch`, `/rename`, `/mcp`, `/heapdump`, Ultracode options, skill usage notes and the provider model-upgrade prompt: `Failed to …` and `Could not …` became `Couldn't …`, `Valid options are:` became `Options:`, `Invalid argument:` became `Unknown option:`, `Branched conversation` became `Branched session`, and `MCP server(s)` now agrees in number.
  - 🟢 Reworded voice mode, custom keybindings, `/config`, `/output-style`, `/color`, `/add-dir`, `/brief` and auto-compact status messages: `… is not available` became `… isn't available`, `Failed to …` and `Could not …` became `Couldn't …`, `Voice mode enabled` and `disabled` became `Voice mode is on` and `is off`, `Invalid color "X". Available colors:` became `"X" isn't an available color. Options:`, and `Brief-only mode enabled` became `Turned on brief-only mode`.
- Anthropic Console sign-in with non-Console organizations
  - 🟢 Showed "can't use Anthropic Console sign-in — run /login or use API key" as a background session's blocked reason in agent view when the API refuses its Console sign-in.
  - 🟢 Showed a specific message when an `oauth_org_not_allowed` error comes from an Anthropic Console sign-in saved on the computer, telling the user to run `/login` and pick a Claude subscription account, set up a Console account, or use an API key.
  - 🟢 Refused an Anthropic Console sign-in from `/login` whose organization isn't a Console account, explaining that nothing changed and how to sign in with a Claude subscription or set up a Console account instead.
- Sign-in token refresh
  - 🟢 Paused before refreshing again when a just-refreshed access token is already due for renewal, logging the wait, instead of refreshing immediately in a loop.
  - 🟢 Added a quiet second try after a 401 when the Claude desktop app runs Claude Code and supplies its OAuth token: Claude Code waits 1.5 seconds and resends the request with the same token before asking the app for a new one, unless the `tengu_tidy_swing` flag is on (it's off by default).
- `/logout` and `claude auth logout`
  - 🟢 Added a warning and a link to Claude Code's account settings when `/logout` or `claude auth logout` removes your local sign-in but Anthropic can't confirm revoking it.
  - 🟢 Kept `/logout`'s success message on screen after it leaves fullscreen mode.
- Network, proxy and TLS errors
  - 🟢 Explained 403 responses that a network edge such as Cloudflare sends before a request reaches Anthropic's API: when the `forceRemoteSettingsRefresh` fetch of remote managed settings or the `forceLoginOrgUUID` organization check fails this way, Claude Code says the request was blocked before reaching Anthropic's API, so signing in again won't help, and points to the VPN, proxy, firewall or network instead of suggesting `claude auth login`.
  - 🟢 Added advice to `CERT_SIGNATURE_FAILURE` TLS errors when connecting to the API or a gateway: after a CA renewal the computer may trust two CA certificates with the same name but different keys, so the message suggests re-issuing certificates with authority key identifiers or trusting only the CA that still signs (in the system store and `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`), and warns against working around it if no CA was renewed.
  - 🟢 Explained TLS certificate signature failures (`CERT_SIGNATURE_FAILURE`) when connecting to the API, noting that two trusted CA certificates with the same name can cause them after a CA renewal, and reported them to SDK hosts as the `tls_cert_signature_failure` error kind.
- Proxies
  - 🟢 Added a 25-second connect timeout when `ALL_PROXY` is the only proxy set and Claude Code tunnels a request through it, such as plugin archive and update downloads and cloud session requests; a stalled proxy now fails with `Timed out connecting through the proxy set in HTTPS_PROXY or ALL_PROXY`, advice to check the proxy, and a suggestion to add the server to `NO_PROXY`, while the `tengu_smooth_avalanche` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Added a 25-second limit on connecting through the proxy in `HTTPS_PROXY` or `ALL_PROXY` for requests sent through Claude Code's HTTP client; a timeout fails with `ETIMEDOUT` and a message suggesting to check the proxy or add the server to `NO_PROXY`.
- 🟢 Unified success, error and warning markers in terminal output for commands such as `claude update`, `claude auth logout` and `claude mcp`, using any plain-text alternatives when redirected or in screen reader mode.
- 🟢 Changed the `/restart` refusal for a session whose transcript is elsewhere to start `Can't restart from here —` instead of `Cannot /update —`.
- 🟢 Shortened the connection trouble message in onboarding.
- 🟢 Dropped the “Note:” prefix from the `/login` notice shown when `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` was set before signing in.
- 🟢 Changed the unavailable wellbeing settings message to “Wellbeing settings aren't available in this version.”
- 🟢 Reworded the notice after saving provider settings when Claude Code can't restart for you: “Your settings are saved. Restart Claude Code to apply them.”
- 🟢 Changed the `/mcp` footer's Esc hint from “back” to “go back,” and let screens show an Enter hint alongside it when they offer an action.
- 🟢 Spelled out “tokens/turn” instead of “tok/turn” in the `/plugin` skill cost list.
- 🟢 Showed the names in `/plugin`'s “Plugins not used recently” list in the regular text color rather than the warning color.
- 🟢 Changed the remote environment picker to say “Set up environments at https://claude.ai/code.”
- 🟢 Reworded the terminal `/plugin` command guide to use shorter descriptions, including `/plugin configure` for setting plugin options and `/plugins` as another way to open the plugin menu.
- 🟢 Changed the inline warning when you select no workflows in `/install-github-app` to say `Select at least one workflow to continue` with an error mark.
- 🟢 Gave the new secret-name field in `/install-github-app` a `CLAUDE_API_KEY` placeholder and a hint that it accepts letters, numbers and underscores.
- 🟢 Updated the `/install-github-app` repository field to show accepted formats below the input and put the missing-repository error next to the field.
- 🟢 Shortened the `/install-github-app` browser instructions to name the repository and say to grant the app access to it.
- 🟢 Restyled the `/install-github-app` completion lines for app install, workflow creation and API key setup, including dropping the exclamation mark from `GitHub Actions workflow created`.
- 🟢 Gave `/install-github-app` a spinner while it checks for an existing `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` secret, and shortened its GitHub CLI, repository URL and cancellation messages.
- 🟢 Clarified the duplicate connector notice in `/mcp` to say to remove the server from `--mcp-config` if you want to use the connector instead.
- 🟢 Changed `/mcp` enable and disable summaries to use `server` or `servers` with the count instead of `server(s)`.
- 🟢 Tightened spacing in the terminal `/help` overview and command lists so less of the screen is used for blank rows.
- 🟢 Added a spinner to `/hooks` while a cloud session is checking which hooks are configured on your machine.

</details>

<details>
<summary>586 more for administrators and for authors of plugins, hooks, MCP servers and SDK hosts</summary>

- 🔴 Added the latest request's model, effort and cache lifetime, token usage and cost to the agent data available to plugin mods, including `$.agent.list`. Cost is marked when a model price is unknown.
- 🔴 Changed sandboxed Bash permission checks to apply your Bash deny and ask rules whenever the sandbox supports auto-allow on this platform, including when `autoAllowBashIfSandboxed` is set explicitly, instead of only when that setting is left unset.
- 🔴 Stopped treating `ConvertFrom-Csv` as automatically read-only in the PowerShell command check, behind `bot_protected_tengu_enumerated_brooks`, which is on by default.
- 🔴 Stopped Claude Desktop, local-agent and VS Code extension sessions from setting aside a required sandbox that can't start in a hosted Linux tool container.
- 🔴 Refused to start a Monitor command when its permission check relied on a sandbox that isn't available at launch, on the served route under `CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_SERVE_TOOL_OUTPUT`.
- 🔴 Removed the suggestion to run `/sandbox install` from sandbox-settings validation errors in the command-line validator; the documentation link is still shown.
- Device, process and special file paths
  - 🔴 Stopped cloud sessions on Linux from reusing a saved copy of an attached file when `/proc` shows the opened file isn't where its path says, for example behind a link.
  - 🔴 Rejected alternate file forks at the first path check for staged cloud file sends.
  - 🔴 URL-encoded file IDs when downloading through the Files API, and refused empty, `.` and `..` IDs for downloads and deletes.
- Git commands Claude Code runs for itself
  - 🔴 Kept the offline git settings that bring-your-own-compute sessions give some git commands, such as no lazy fetch and no remote protocols, from being overridden by the environment's own git `envOverrides`.
  - 🔴 Bundled `HEAD` alone when packaging the current branch for a remote session if git reports a branch name that starts with `-`, so the name can't be read as a `git bundle` option.
- Permission rules and managed policy
  - 🔴 Kept this computer's managed permission `deny` and `ask` rules when an organization's managed settings are in force, leaving out any of those rules that start with `!`.
  - 🔴 Checked `permissions.additionalDirectories` for calls forwarded to a linked computer as well as under `permissions.blockFileToolReadsOutsideWorkingDirectories`: entries must be full paths, `~` and `~/…` count only under that setting, and the warning now names both cases.
  - 🔴 Anchored relative deny rules at the folder Claude Code started in, as well as the working folders, when the internal `permissions.blockFileToolReadsOutsideWorkingDirectories` setting, meant for the program that starts Claude Code, is on.
- Reported permission denials
  - 🔴 Stopped the headless SDK from reporting a permission denial in its fallback prompt path when a plugin cut the call short.
  - 🔴 Named connector-file checks separately in tool permission denials.
  - 🟢 Recorded a tool call that your organization's managed PreToolUse hook refuses as a permission denial, so it appears in the result's `permission_denials` list for SDK and print-mode runs and is logged like other denials.
- Auto mode
  - 🔴 Changed permission-mode selection to use the session's `acceptEdits` starting preference when checking whether a proactivity change can switch modes.
  - 🟢 Extended the managed-policy check that removes `$defaults` from auto mode `allow` rules given with `--settings` to the auto mode `environment` list as well.
  - 🟢 Let auto mode use its server-side classifier for HIPAA organizations on the Anthropic API unless `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS` is set.
  - 🟢 Changed a session that started in the built-in auto mode default before your organization's managed settings arrived to switch, once they arrive, to the managed `permissions.defaultMode`, or to default mode when the managed settings failed to load and there's no policy verdict or the HIPAA flag applies.
  - 🟢 Removed the HIPAA hold on restoring auto mode at resume: a session saved in auto mode now resumes in auto mode when your organization's HIPAA compliance flag is set, as long as auto mode is available, instead of keeping the startup mode.
- Folder trust and added folders
  - 🔴 Stopped carrying command-line `--add-dir` folders into Claude Code's own relaunches when it runs off your machine (remote, SSH or a linked computer) outside background sessions, unless your settings already allow those folders.
  - 🔴 Changed the `register_repo_root` request to stop treating a launch-time `--add-dir` folder as an allowed anchor once it no longer resolves to where it first did, as when a symlink is pointed elsewhere.
  - 🟢 Changed sessions that ignore persisted folder trust without a terminal (`--ignore-persisted-trust`, `CLAUDE_CODE_IGNORE_PERSISTED_TRUST`, or a self-hosted runner started with `--trust-workspace false`) so a folder's `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/settings.local.json` can only make settings stricter: values that would loosen a setting are ignored with a warning naming them and pointing to user or managed settings, while the `tengu_steady_glacier` flag is on, as it is by default.
- Sessions running off your computer
  - 🔴 Widened the cloud-session refusal for `mcp_tool` hooks: besides the remote-devices and Claude in Chrome servers, hooks in a cloud session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE`) can no longer call tools of `claude-device`, Claude Preview, Claude Browser, the Claude Code iOS Simulator and Android Emulator, Framebuffer, or most servers named `client-…`.
  - 🔴 Added an internal `onPersonsMachine` declaration that a desktop host can attach to an MCP server it registers, saying the server runs on the person's own computer; in a session running elsewhere (cloud, SSH or a VM), the CLI then refuses calls to that server from `mcp_tool` hooks and mods, except, for hooks only, the tools listed in `exceptTools`.
  - 🔴 Carried `onPersonsMachine` through SDK MCP server updates so they can describe where the tool runs.
  - 🔴 Passed `CLAUDE_CODE_OFF_PERSONS_MACHINE` to terminal teammates when the parent knows it's off the user's machine, including SSH, while leaving plain cloud sessions without that signal alone.
- Tools this computer runs for a remote session
  - 🔴 Made ToolHost reject a request to add a folder on a different drive when the request must stay inside the configured workspace.
  - 🔴 Omitted the old working directory from served shell and Monitor refusal messages when the path isn't suitable to print.
- Markers for content written by others
  - 🔴 Carried Claude Code's markers for content authored by others and for remote device reports into the model calls a plugin makes with `$.model.complete` and `$.model.fork`, once the plugin's hooks have read content holding them in tool results, command output or host API results.
  - 🔴 Prefixed Artifacts permission asks and refusals with the `<artifact-content-authored-by-others/>` marker when the artifact title they quote may come from other people's content.
  - 🔴 Stopped Artifacts from treating a write as an unattended reply to a comment notification when other messages in that turn carry content marked as authored by others, when the `tengu_woolly_honey` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Stopped Artifact comment reads on marked or externally written pages from qualifying as the account holder's own words for later reply checks when `tengu_woolly_honey` is safe, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Kept the marker for artifact text written by others out of the reason shown for a declined shell call, while keeping its explanation.
  - 🔴 Marked an attached artifact's title as text written by others when the server marks an artifact you own that way, behind `tengu_fluffy_brooks`, which ships off by default.
- `plugin.register` verdicts on plugins
  - 🔴 Let a plugin's `plugin.register` hook narrow another plugin instead of only allowing or refusing it: the hook now sees the plugin's `provides`, `unread` and `requiredByOrganization`, may remove entries from `provides` (but not add them, or narrow `themes` or `bin`), and Claude Code then loads only the hooks, MCP servers and monitors left.
  - 🔴 Applied the trimmed `provides` list from a plugin hooks module's `plugin.register` handler to a plugin's LSP servers, output styles, commands, skills and agents, so the entries it removed stay out while the rest of the plugin loads.
  - 🔴 Extended `plugin.register` hooks to every plugin about to load, with or without a hooks module, so a `{ refuse }` keeps out the whole plugin, including its command hooks, MCP and LSP servers, skills, commands, agents, output styles, themes, monitors, settings and `bin/`, before any of them starts.
  - 🔴 Added `requiredByOrganization` to `initialize.plugins` entries read with `--await-initialize`, which marks those plugin folders as required by the organization.
  - 🔴 Left servers from plugins that a `plugin.register` hook refused out of the startup prompt that asks to approve a project's new MCP servers.
  - 🔴 Limited plugin workflows to what `plugin.register` hooks allow: a refused plugin's workflows don't load, and a narrowed plugin keeps only the workflows its narrowing names.
  - 🔴 Applied `plugin.register` verdicts when plugins reload, leaving out refused plugins and the hooks and monitors a narrowing excludes.
  - 🔴 Changed the `plugin.register` plugin event to cover every plugin, with a hooks module or without: a refusal now keeps out all of a plugin's parts, from its command hooks and MCP and LSP servers to its skills, commands, agents, themes and `bin/`, and a hook can pass on a narrowed `provides` list to load only some of them; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
- Safe mode
  - 🔴 Changed the note shown when a plugin's hooks module is held back so only managed and built-in plugins run to list `--safe-mode` alongside `allowManagedHooksOnly` and `disableAllHooks` as a reason.
  - 🔴 Changed safe mode (`--safe-mode` or `CLAUDE_CODE_SAFE_MODE`) to stop skipping the startup repair of managed plugins listed in `prependPlugins` or `appendPlugins`: a missing one is fetched again, though safe mode still doesn't install plugins or queue a plugin sync.
- Plugin loading and `plugin.json`
  - 🔴 Refused to read a plugin's monitors file or `settings.json` when it isn't a regular file, reporting "not a regular file, or too large".
  - 🔴 Applied plugin policy checks before using the enabled plugin list to resolve a plugin's deferred MCP server.
  - 🔴 Loaded plugin hooks before refreshing commands and agents, and waited for the plugin policy result before using the enabled list.
  - 🔴 Stopped a plugin refresh that was replaced before finishing from triggering another command-source refresh.
  - 🔴 Applied account plugin access and capability decisions to the plugin list that headless sessions initialize with.
  - 🔴 Reported plugin hook-loading failures separately from command- and agent-loading failures during interactive startup.
  - 🔴 Rejected plugin `headers` entries that spell a request-routing or identity header with punctuation other than `-` or `_`, such as `X.Forwarded-For`.
  - 🔴 Changed the startup check for plugin modules that affect tools to identify which installed plugins registered a tool hook or couldn't be loaded for inspection.
  - 🔴 Checked a plugin's permissions before connecting a plugin-declared MCP server and left the server disabled if the plugin couldn't declare it.
  - 🔴 Changed the built-in `claude-platform` plugin to reconsider whether it's offered when the sign-in changes, as well as when `tengu_peppy_crescent` changes (off by default), and updated its reload notices.
- Plugin marketplaces and installs
  - 🔴 Skipped a plugin's `engines` version check for a dependency installed at a version tag from a local file or directory marketplace, as already happened for that marketplace's relative-path plugins.
  - 🔴 Told the model, when the plugin suggestion tool renders a card for a message sent from the Claude iOS or Android app, that the user can't see the card there, in sessions started from claude.ai chat when the `tengu_soft_sundae` flag is on, as it isn't by default.
  - 🟢 Ignored `enabledPlugins` and `extraKnownMarketplaces` from a project's own settings when the project is a pull request's `pr-<number>` worktree checkout, behind the `tengu_nifty_squirrel` flag, which is on by default; user settings, `--settings` and managed settings still apply.
- `claude plugin validate`
  - 🔴 Added the `LINKED_TARGET` error code when plugin or project validation refuses a linked component directory.
  - 🔴 Added `provides` to `claude plugin validate --json`, with the component inventory when it could be read and `null` otherwise.
  - 🔴 Added `wasm` and `modules` to the per-file results from `claude plugin validate --json`, so scripts can read the mod and hook-module checks.
  - 🔴 Cleaned control characters out of paths, messages and advice in `claude plugin validate --json` output while keeping ordinary whitespace in otherwise clean strings.
  - 🔴 Changed plugin validation to recognize alternate spellings of the `.claude-plugin` directory when classifying manifests and finding the plugin root.
  - 🔴 Removed duplicate findings from plugin hook validation when the same checks report the same result.
  - 🔴 Updated the built-in tool types that `claude plugin validate` checks a hooks module against outside a session to this build's tools, listing `SetGoal`, `GetGoal` and `PauseGoal` in place of `ProposeGoal`, and the Artifact tool's new comment actions (`comments`, `reply`, `resolve`, `resume_replies`).
  - 🔴 Changed plugin validation to treat malformed display fields as errors even when the plugin loader can ignore them, and skip follow-up advice for those manifests.
  - 🔴 Changed the plugin validator to include per-module reports of hook events, calls, environment and state access, telemetry, client modules, and prompt-cache use.
  - 🔴 Changed plugin hook validation to resolve code paths the same way as the loader, including the entry used for TypeScript checking.
- Organization-required plugins
  - 🔴 Kept track of organization-required plugin paths when applying a local SDK `reload_plugins` addition, so a failed reload can restore the previous list.
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_REQUIRED_PLUGINS_HOLD` for Claude Code running as a hosted cloud worker or tool-container daemon: when it's set and only managed hooks are allowed, plugins the organization requires keep their hooks, unless hooks are restricted or `disableAllHooks` is set in a settings file.
  - 🔴 Ignored local-settings opt-outs from claude.ai plugin sync, including an unreadable local settings file, in hosted workers and tool containers started with `CLAUDE_CODE_REQUIRED_PLUGINS_HOLD`.
  - 🔴 Ignored a `--plugin-dir` copy of a plugin that the host app marks as required by the organization, reporting `--plugin-dir copy of "…" ignored: your organization requires its own copy`, and left out other copies of the same name with a warning in the debug log.
- Plugins in cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Limited the remote first-turn plugin freshness check to sessions with `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS` enabled, even if other session references are being synced, with `tengu_ccr_plugins_turn_start_check_enabled` on by default.
  - 🔴 Removed a path behind the `tengu_level_plover` flag, off by default, that made Claude Code cloud worker sessions sync plugins and session references as if `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_PLUGINS` and `CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SESSION_REFS` were set.
  - 🔴 Withheld claude.ai plugins marked as withheld from Claude Code in sessions started by a remote trigger too.
  - 🔴 Fixed remote plugin startup synchronization so a failed reload is still owed and a newer sync round isn't mistaken for an earlier one the host already handled.
- Built-in `cc-plugin-sec-default` plugin
  - 🔴 Stopped plugins a person installs from writing Claude Code's `settings.json`, `settings.local.json` or `cowork_settings.json` through their mods' file writes, processes or tool calls, where the built-in `cc-plugin-sec-default` plugin is seated (by default on machines with managed settings and for Team and Enterprise organizations), unless managed settings turn on its `allowModsToOverrideDenyRules` option.
  - 🔴 Stopped plugins a person installs from running a call Claude made before it's allowed: where `cc-plugin-sec-default` is seated, a mod's own tool call, file write or process that repeats one of Claude's calls that hasn't been allowed is refused, unless the permission check now allows that call or managed settings turn on `allowModsToOverrideDenyRules`.
  - 🔴 Changed `cc-plugin-sec-default@builtin` to stop personally installed plugins from running a model-requested tool call before permission has been granted, on managed installs and eligible Team and Enterprise accounts that load the plugin.
  - 🔴 Changed `cc-plugin-sec-default@builtin` to keep protected permission prompts in force even when a user plugin turns a plain ask into an allow.
  - 🔴 Changed the built-in security plugin to call a plain host permission request a “permission prompt” when it explains why a plugin's proposed change wasn't used.
  - 🔴 Added a `forceHotReloadPrompt` option for `cc-plugin-sec-default` in managed settings: before a mod Claude wrote in a `dev-mods` folder loads, Claude Code asks whether to turn on mods Claude writes, doesn't load it on "Not now" or no answer, and remembers "Turn them on" for that folder in later sessions.
  - 🔴 Changed the built-in security plugin to enforce managed settings on plugin registration, including asking before hot-loading a development plugin when `forceHotReloadPrompt` is set.
- `$.session.identity` and the built-in privacy plugin
  - 🔴 Changed `$.session.identity()` for plugins, which used to answer only mods installed by managed settings, to answer every caller and leave refusals to the built-in privacy and sec-default mods, which refuse mods in the `user` tier; its answer also adds `contextEmail`, the email address the model is handed, and plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Moved the `$.session.identity` check for mods into the built-in Privacy plugin, which is on by default and refuses only user-tier mods; 2.1.325 answered only built-in mods and mods that managed settings enable and load in place from a source they name.
  - 🔴 Added `contextEmail`, the email address the session hands the model, to `$.session.identity()` answers in plugin hooks modules; the built-in privacy mod leaves it out for callers outside the built-in tier.
  - 🔴 Changed the built-in privacy plugin to check the email from the active context when it differs from the signed-in account's email.
  - 🔴 Avoided unnecessary identity reads in the built-in privacy plugin for shell commands whose text can't send an email address.
  - 🔴 Restricted direct `session.identity` reads to plugins enabled by managed settings from a source those settings name; the built-in privacy and security plugins enforce the check.
- Plugin mods and permissions
  - 🔴 Reset Claude Code's internal consent fields (such as `__consentAskCanReachUser`) to their defaults in a tool input that a plugin's hook allows, so a plugin can't set them.
  - 🔴 Added a separate permission layer for tool calls a plugin's function hooks make on their own (`$.tool.call`), which builds such a call's tool list from the session's permission rules and asks the plugins' `tool.list` hooks about tools that deny rules remove before the call runs; the new `bot_protected_tengu_polished_rivest` flag, on by default, is part of how that layer applies deny rules.
  - 🔴 Let a plugin's own `$.tool.call` reach and run tools that deny rules or a `blocked` ceiling hide from the model, which now stop only the model and its agents, while a tool another plugin's `tool.list` hook removes stays refused; deny rules still bind such calls while the organization's policy verdict hasn't settled, is unattributed, or marks the session HIPAA.
  - 🔴 Kept a plugin's own tool-call permissions out of the context inherited by a newly spawned observer.
- Plugin mod hook events
  - 🔴 Added five events for plugin function hooks: `tool.judge` answers `{ decision }` (`allow`, `ask` or `deny`, with an optional reason) about a tool call it can't change, `tool.run` sees a call after it was allowed and can't rewrite it, `session.stop` receives `isTurnRunning`, `ui.pointer` receives pointer events on a surface's scene with their coordinates, and `agent.call` receives the agent id.
  - 🔴 Added a `tool.judge` event for plugins' function hooks in auto mode: a plugin can allow, deny or ask about a tool call that auto mode's safety classifier would judge, though an allow for a protected tool still goes to the classifier, and a denial reads "Permission to use <tool> denied by plugin <name>: <reason>".
  - 🔴 Added a `tool.judge` plugin event, raised when auto mode is about to ask its classifier about a call, where a hook can deny the call or put it to a person, or allow in the classifier's place a call its own plugin made with `$.agent.call` that no hook rewrote; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added a `tool.run` event for plugin hooks modules: it fires for any tool once its call is allowed (after `tool.call`, PreToolUse hooks, `tool.check` and any permission answer), on the input as allowed, so a hook can audit the call, answer it in the tool's place, or refuse it with `{ deny }`; a person's own hooks modules load while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added a `tool.run` event for plugin hooks modules that fires when an allowed call is about to run; a hook can let the tool run, answer with its own `{ result }` in the tool's place, or fail the call with `{ deny }`, but can't rewrite the call.
  - 🔴 Ran plugins' new `tool.run` hooks around each tool call, so a handler can refuse the run, which hands the model `Error: <reason>`, or replace the result.
  - 🔴 Added a `session.stop` plugin event, raised when the person presses Stop (Esc or Ctrl+C on a turn, a client's Stop, or Ctrl+C at an idle prompt that stops running scripts), telling hooks whether a turn was running; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Refused skills, messages and other input that a plugin sends from inside its hooks module's new `session.stop` handler, giving the reason that the session was stopped and nobody has sent input since, when the `tengu_plugin_input_refusal` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Changed forks that a command starts with no tool call (`/subtask`, `/btw`'s fork key, and `/fork` where it starts a subagent) to raise the `agent.spawn` plugin event, where a hook can pick the fork's model or refuse it; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Raised the plugin `agent.spawn` hook for forks a command starts without a tool call (`/subtask`, `/btw`'s fork key, and `/fork` when it starts a subagent); a hook can't change `background` or `cwd` for them.
  - 🔴 Ran plugins' `agent.spawn` hooks for `/subtask` and `/fork`, so a plugin can rewrite the new agent's prompt and description or decline it with `The <name> plugin declined the subtask: <reason>` (or `the fork`).
  - 🔴 Included plugin `tool.run` hooks when checking whether headless execution needs to preserve the tool's post-call hook handling.
- Plugin mod API
  - 🔴 Recorded each local agent's and teammate's token count every 5 seconds, keeping the last 16 readings, for the `tokenSamples` field that plugin agent rows and the deprecated subagent status line receive.
  - 🔴 Added `last` and `after` options to `$.session.messages` for plugin hooks: `last` takes a whole number from 1 to 4,096, `after` takes a row's uuid, they can't be combined, and `after` doesn't work with `as: "api"`.
  - 🔴 Added `last` and `after` options to `$.session.messages` in plugin hooks modules for reading only the newest messages or the rows after a given row, refusing an `after` row that's no longer in the conversation.
  - 🔴 Added `last` and `after` to a plugin's `$.session.messages`, so it can read only the newest rows, or the rows after a row's `uuid`, and follow a conversation without rereading it; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added `$.agent.limits()`, `$.session.info()` and `$.process.hookEnv()` to plugin hooks modules, giving how many agents may run at once, how deep they nest and whether they can run in the background; the session's transcript path, platform, hosting and similar facts; and the environment variables a plugin's command hook gets, to pass to a child process.
  - 🔴 Added `$.agent.limits()` to plugin function hooks, which reports how many agents may run at once, how deep they may nest and whether they can run in the background; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added `$.session.info()` to plugin function hooks, which returns the session's transcript path, scratchpad folder, prompt id, agent type, config root, platform and whether the engine runs on the hosted server; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added `$.process.hookEnv()` to plugin function hooks, which returns the variables a plugin's command hook gets, such as `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID`, `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` and `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA`, to pass to a child process that `$.process.run` or `$.process.spawn` starts; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added running plugin calls and spawns as a named agent, `$.agent.call` with `agentId` and `$.agent.spawn` with `parentAgentId`: a running agent lends its live conversation, tools and permissions, and a finished or stopped one is rebuilt from its saved record the way a resume rebuilds it.
  - 🔴 Added `parentAgentId` to plugins' `$.agent.spawn`, so a plugin can start an agent as another agent's child; it's refused when that parent can't take one.
  - 🔴 Added `parentAgentId` to a plugin's `$.agent.spawn`, which starts the new agent as a child of an existing running or resting agent, or with `subagentType: "fork"` as a fork of its conversation, prompt, tools and model; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Fixed plugin `tool.describe` hooks keeping an MCP tool's rewritten description after a different server (another type, origin or connector) took over the tool's name; the hook now runs again for the new server and gets it as a pinned `mcpServer`.
  - 🔴 Added `mcpServer` to the `tool.describe` plugin event, naming an MCP tool's server transport, origin and, where it's vouched for, its claude.ai connector id; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added each agent's task kind and stored status, progress label, start time, folder, model, effort, context window, token count with its last 16 readings, last request with its cache lifetime, per-model token usage and estimated cost to a plugin's `$.agent.list()` entries; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Expanded the rows a plugin's `$.agent.list()` call returns: each agent now also carries its task kind and status, progress label, start time, working folder, model and context-window size, effort, token count and recent token samples, whether the agent panel draws its row, and, for most agents, its last request, usage and cost.
  - 🔴 Stopped hooks modules in the `builtin` tier from withholding parts of `$` at `engine.create`, as `user`-tier modules already couldn't; the engine now unloads such a module with a message naming its tier.
  - 🔴 Changed a plugin's `$.session.append` user row to wait for the next model request of its loop and go out as a reminder under a line naming the plugin, stored as a `plugin_note` attachment, instead of being stored at once as a hidden user message; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Stored a plugin's own `user` row from `$.session.append` as a `plugin_note` attachment that Claude reads as "Note from the … plugin, not from the user:", and kept those notes in background sessions' transcripts.
  - 🔴 Stopped dispatching `plugin_note` attachments to mod `prompt.attachment` hooks, leaving the notes as the engine rendered them.
  - 🔴 Added `$.agent.call()` and its `agent.call` event to plugin hooks modules, which run a tool as a given agent's own call, or the main conversation's, with that agent's tools, folder, mode and rules, through hooks, permission prompts and auto mode's classifier as the model's calls go; an `AbortSignal` option cuts the call.
  - 🔴 Added `$.agent.call` to plugin hooks modules: it runs a tool as the main conversation's or a given agent's own call, with that agent's tools, rules, hooks, permission prompts and auto mode classifier, resolves `{ deny }` when refused, and takes an `AbortSignal` that cuts the call wherever it stands; it can run the `Agent` tool, except after the person stopped the session and before anyone sends new input.
  - 🔴 Added `$.agent.call` to plugin function hooks, which runs a tool as a chosen agent's own call, or the main conversation's, with that agent's tools, folder, permission mode and rules, meeting the hooks, permission dialog and auto mode's classifier as the agent's own call would, adding nothing to its conversation, and stopping when its `AbortSignal` aborts; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Refused plugin calls and spawns made as an agent that's a teammate, an observer, part of a workflow run, running without hooks, still running its first `turn.start` hooks, still stopping, or unknown to the session, with a message naming the reason.
  - 🔴 Changed plugins' `$.tool.call` and `$.mcp.call` to have Claude Code connect again to MCP servers that could supply a missing tool before failing, adding what that found to the "no tool named …" error.
  - 🔴 Changed plugin authoring guidance to account for an organization's `tool.run` hook when describing whether local package sharing is available.
  - 🔴 Changed errors for tools supplied by a `tool.list` hook to account for both `tool.run` and `tool.call` handlers.
  - 🔴 Sent bundled file writes through per-file checks when a plugin registers a matching `tool.run` hook, as well as for the existing `tool.call` hook path.
  - 🔴 Added `$.wasm.compile` for plugin hooks modules: a module can compile WebAssembly from bytes or from a `.wasm` file in its own plugin folder named by a relative string literal; links must point at a file named `.wasm`, files over 64 MiB aren't read, modules that declare or import a shared memory are refused, and it doesn't run on the hosted server or in a test file's own hooks.
  - 🔴 Added `$.wasm.compile()` to plugin function hooks, which compiles a `.wasm` file the plugin ships, or bytes made at run time, up to 64 MiB, and gives that hooks module a `WebAssembly` global that compiles nothing else; it's refused on the hosted server, `claude plugin validate` lists each file's size, SHA-256, imports and exports, and plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Changed hooks-module file watching so a module that failed to load is retried when a `.wasm` file in its folder changes, as well as its manifest or JavaScript and TypeScript files.
  - 🔴 Added `offset` and `length` to a plugin's `$.fs.read`, so a file larger than the 4 MiB one read may return can be read in byte ranges; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added `offset` and `length` options to `$.fs.read` in plugin hooks modules, which read a byte range of a regular file and refuse pipes, sockets, devices and directories.
  - 🔴 Added a separate `RunModFsRange` request for mods to read a file starting at a byte offset, with a maximum requested length of 4 MiB.
  - 🔴 Refused plugin hook answers whose `usage` carries an `ephemeral_5m_input_tokens` or `ephemeral_1h_input_tokens` count that isn't a finite number.
  - 🔴 Added the five-minute and one-hour split of cache writes (`ephemeral_5m_input_tokens`, `ephemeral_1h_input_tokens`) to the per-turn usage that plugins read, and `hasUnknownPrice` to a session's cost once a model with no known price was priced at another's rates; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Let a forwarded plugin hook call get an answer from a matching local plugin with the same name when the request explicitly allows it, and marked the result as coming from that substitute.
  - 🔴 Let `$.audio.speak` in plugin hooks take an `options.signal` to stop speech, refusing a signal that isn't an `AbortSignal`, as `$.audio.play` already did.
  - 🔴 Let an organization's plugin hook start with the environment values its own plugin set through `$.env.set`; values other plugins set are still left out.
  - 🔴 Kept prompt marks when a mod rewrites an attachment and applied the same marks to later renders of that attachment.
  - 🔴 Declared the chart plugin's MCP tool-call matcher when registering it, so hosts know which MCP calls its chart hooks can handle.
  - 🔴 Added a `signal` option to a plugin's `$.audio.speak`, which skips a queued utterance or cuts off one being spoken; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Fixed plugins' `$.tool.call` and `$.tool.check` finding no tool of that name in Code Mode for tools that only Code Mode's REPL offers, such as Bash and Read.
- Plugin mod UI rendering
  - 🔴 Added `AgentRow` and `ContextMenu` as places a plugin's `ui.render` hooks can draw for clients that ask Claude Code to render plugin UI: a background agent's row, which a plugin can restyle or hide, and a context menu's rows, where a plugin's rows that push the menu past its size limits are left out with a warning.
  - 🔴 Added an `AgentRow` render component, so a mod can relabel, redraw or hide an agent's row in the agent list under the prompt, in the desktop app's Background tasks and in VS Code's running subagents; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Showed a plugin's UI surface as having drawn nothing, with the refusal reason, when the whole tree its render returned was refused.
  - 🔴 Limited what plugin `ui.render` hooks can return to 100 levels of nesting and 1,000,000 values (16 characters of text count as one), refusing BigInt, function and symbol values; when the drawing `next(e)` handed the hook was already near one of those limits, Claude Code logs a warning and draws its own version instead of failing the hook.
  - 🔴 Limited plugin `ui.render` hooks to 30 seconds in all of `$` calls per drawing; a hook held longer is failed and skipped.
  - 🔴 Changed what Claude Code draws when a plugin `ui.render` hook's answer is invalid or fails: it now draws the props as that hook received them from the hooks above it, not the original props.
  - 🔴 Changed rendered session surfaces to use live agent labels and report whether a mod hid the agent's row.
  - 🔴 Added support for rendering the shader layer on session surfaces that ask for it.
  - 🔴 Changed remote and redacted session surfaces to omit context-menu props from their rendered response.
  - 🔴 Added a `ContextMenu` render component for the desktop app's right-click menu on the transcript, where a mod adds rows as `Button`s or `https:` `Link`s for the row, link, selection or code under the pointer, with a worked example and its test (`examples/menu.tsx`, `examples/menu.test.tsx`) in the bundled `plugin-authoring` skill; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Added `overflowX="scroll"` to a mod's `Box`, so the rows of a chart or table scroll sideways together on mobile and Desktop while the terminal and VS Code cut them at the edge, and `maxLines` and `wrap="clip"` to its `Text`; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Added `overflowX` (`visible` or `scroll`) for `Box`, and `wrap: "clip"` and `maxLines` (1 to 10,000) for `Text`, to plugin drawings; `overflowX` is dropped on the terminal and in VS Code.
- Plugin mod themes, backdrops and shaders
  - 🔴 Added a 160 MB GPU memory budget for plugin backdrop shaders; the checker lowers the render scale to fit and refuses a shader if it still won't fit at half resolution.
  - 🔴 Added GPU time checks for plugin backdrop shaders at half resolution: the checker refuses shaders it estimates will take more than 150 ms to start or more than 5 ms per frame at 60 fps or 10 ms at 30 fps on a fast GPU.
  - 🔴 Set plugin backdrop shader limits at 128 KiB of source, 24 passes including tables and 64 iterations per counted loop, with a choice of 30 or 60 fps.
  - 🔴 Added checks for `Shader` nodes that plugin hooks draw: a Shader must sit in a Backdrop, take only type, props and children, and hold exactly one `Svg` child that shows wherever the shader doesn't run; a `ui.render` hook may keep a Shader that `next(e)` handed it and change its values, but not swap which plugin or module it names, and a Shader it adds itself must be named in its module source so `claude plugin validate` lists it.
  - 🔴 Added shader reports to plugin validation for hook modules that reference `Shader`, with source lines for errors, per-pass GPU estimates on weak and fast GPUs, and the most expensive source lines.
  - 🔴 Sent hashes in place of the SVG and shader sources a desktop or mobile client says it already holds, for up to 8 parts, when sending a mod theme's scene.
  - 🔴 Added a `Shader` element that a plugin theme's `Backdrop` can draw in the desktop and mobile apps, playing a GPU scene over an `Svg` shown where it can't play; a backdrop holds one, at its root or in a `Box`.
  - 🔴 Restricted plugin theme backdrops to `Box`, `Text`, `Svg` and `Shader` elements; a backdrop drawing with any other element is now refused with a message naming it.
  - 🔴 Added `Shader` backdrops to mod themes: on desktop a theme's `Backdrop` can play a GPU shader, written in TypeScript against the new `claude-code/shader` module, over an `Svg` that shows alone on mobile, under Reduce Motion or when the shader fails; a `ui.pointer` event tells the theme's plugin about clicks on the bare backdrop, `claude plugin validate` prints each shader's cost or the line it refuses, and plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added a `ui.pointer` hook so mod themes on desktop and mobile clients receive clicks on their backdrop, with the position given as fractions of the surface rounded to 1/64.
  - 🔴 Added a `ui.pointer` event for plugin hooks modules that fires when the person clicks or taps the backdrop where nothing covers it; only the hooks of the plugin that set the theme see it, and they can only observe it.
  - 🔴 Removed picture backdrops from mod themes: `$.theme.set` now refuses `{ image }`, still backdrops are a color, gradient or vector, and a scene's `Svg` must be pure vector with no embedded pictures or fonts, or the engine refuses it while `$.theme.set` still answers `isDrawn: true`; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Refused `Svg` art in a mod's Backdrop that embeds anything, such as `data:` URLs, base64 text, images, video, audio, iframes or scripts, `url()` or `href` that isn't a local `#fragment`, event handler attributes, `@font-face` or `@import`, DOCTYPE or entity declarations, or a non-UTF-8 XML encoding, with an error saying a scene is vector art that embeds nothing.
  - 🔴 Limited mod theme shaders to 24 passes, 64 turns per loop, and 65,536 steps before loops are expanded, with a refusal that says what to simplify.
- `subagentStatusLine` setting
  - 🔴 Changed plugin validation to warn about `subagentStatusLine` both in `plugin.json` settings and in a separate `settings.json`, and to explain where to set `agent` instead.
  - 🔴 Stopped including the removed plugin subagent status-line setting's warning in headless startup results.
- Hooks
  - 🔴 Changed headless blocking Stop, SubagentStop, TaskCompleted and TeammateIdle hooks to accept a compatible reply from a paired hook event.
  - 🔴 Added `tool.judge` and `tool.run` to the plugin hook check and to the hooks omitted when settings hooks are turned off.
  - 🔴 Fixed plugin hooks and early worktree hook setup under organization policy so approved plugins can still supply hooks when settings-based hooks are turned off.
- MCP connections and reconnects
  - 🔴 Cleared the MCP connector's previous search reconnect failure once OAuth refresh returns a usable token.
  - 🔴 Stopped retrying an MCP server that answered with HTTP 429 on the same connection entry, and told the model it needs to continue without that server.
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_PREWAIT_SERVERS_MS_BY_NAME` to give named MCP servers their own shorter startup wait in headless SDK URL sessions outside the bridge.
  - 🔴 Changed the headless query path to recheck whether a refreshed MCP tool can still be used when building the next tool list.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP WebSocket connections to save their close code and reason for connection cleanup and error reporting.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP tools and slash commands to use the server connection currently listed in the session when they run, so they don't use an older copy of the row.
  - 🔴 Asked for approval and retried once when reading a resource from a claude.ai connector through a cloud session's proxy fails because the connector needs approval, instead of failing the read; this applies while the `tengu_mcp_proxy_needs_approval_retry` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Made a resumed MCP task use the current connection's configuration when it reconnects, including the current plugin and settings source; MCP tasks use `tengu_glowing_lark`, on by default for supported local CLI sessions unless `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MCP_TASKS` is set.
  - 🔴 Let the service tune MCP reconnect retries in hosted cloud sessions through the `tengu_starry_fox` flag: up to five start delays of 100 to 12,000 ms, random jitter, and whether connect timeouts are retried; without it the delays stay 0.5, 1.5 and 4 seconds.
  - 🔴 Let `tengu_starry_fox` stop eligible hosted MCP workers from retrying a connection that timed out when `afterTimeout` is false.
  - 🔴 Changed a tool call to give an eligible disconnected MCP server up to 10 seconds to reconnect and report why it couldn't reconnect if that attempt fails.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP to report when a server rejected the session credential and clear its connection for a later attempt.
  - 🔴 Distinguished rate limits from transient network failures in the SDK MCP reconnect result.
  - 🔴 Used the host's in-process HTTP handler when a Streamable HTTP MCP connection targets a server the host serves.
  - 🔴 Kept custom certificate authorities but left client certificates out of the SSE and Streamable HTTP settings for MCP connections retried without OAuth.
  - 🔴 Waited for early startup setup as well as hook registration before choosing whether to connect an MCP server on startup paths where either is still running.
  - 🔴 Changed SDK MCP reconnects to check that the server is still registered and allowed before dialing, and to keep its sign-in status when discovery hasn't recovered.
  - 🔴 Added the option to retry rate-limited MCP servers at the start of a turn after a 30-second wait, when `tengu_splendid_biscuit` is on (off by default).
  - 🔴 Added a next-turn retry of the tool list for connected non-SDK MCP servers when their last list failed and `tengu_vivid_planet` is on (off by default).
  - 🔴 Refreshed an MCP tool listing after a list-change notification when the client can now declare uploads and its earlier listing hadn't done so.
  - 🟢 Fixed HTTP and SSE MCP servers being skipped in later sessions after `headersHelper` returned no headers and no saved sign-in could cover it, behind `tengu_lazy_ladybug` (on by default).
  - 🟢 Fixed MCP server updates so a removed server isn't brought back by an in-flight reconnect and a plugin rejection is considered before reconnecting.
- MCP tool schemas and calls
  - 🔴 Limited the tools offered by newly connected MCP servers to the selected main-conversation agent's tool rules.
  - 🔴 Added support for local MCP servers to ask, through the experimental `anthropic/toolsPlacement` capability with `place: "first"`, for their always-loaded tools to be listed first, outside cloud sessions.
  - 🔴 Neutralized `<persisted-output>` and `<truncated-output>` tags in MCP tool results that a host lets through whole, so a tool can't fake those markers there.
  - 🔴 Added connector origin and ID details to MCP tool metadata, plus a marker for browser tools served by a local pane on the host.
- MCP server sign-in
  - 🔴 Stopped treating WebSocket MCP servers that set `oauth.xaa` as signed in through cross-app access when `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_XAA` is on, so they get the usual OAuth handling.
  - 🔴 Kept MCP's empty credential entry in place while an OAuth sign-in or callback is underway.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP OAuth sign-out to reject an invalid revocation address before trying to send a token to it, and log why it skipped the request.
  - 🔴 Stopped an MCP reconnect that's waiting for sign-in from starting an OAuth flow on its own; an explicit sign-in can still proceed.
- `claude mcp serve`
  - 🔴 Made MCP plugin serving distinguish failures to load callable hooks from failures in other plugin parts, so a missing skill or command file doesn't block serving unrelated hooks.
  - 🔴 Stopped advertising the option to turn sandboxing off in the tool schemas for both forms of `claude mcp serve`; HTTP mode still hides its additional restricted inputs.
  - 🔴 Disabled the per-call option to turn off the sandbox for shell tools served over standard input with `claude mcp serve`.
  - 🔴 Accepted more valid shell working-directory names, such as directories with spaces, under the launch folder in `claude mcp serve`.
  - 🔴 Limited which missing working directories `claude mcp serve` can recreate for its shell, sticking to ordinary directory names under the launch folder.
  - 🔴 Added a note to sandbox-related shell errors from the standard-input MCP server when the requested command can't use an outside-sandbox exception without approval.
  - 🔴 Made MCP-served plugin calls share the startup plugin sync that the session already uses.
  - 🟢 Made `claude mcp serve` ignore shell sandbox exclusions, since the server can't ask for approval to run outside the sandbox.
- 🔴 Flagged eligible destructive MCP calls for server-side confirmation when neither the user nor the classifier approved them and Claude Code isn't handling the prompt, including qualifying file actions.
- MCP calls moved to the background
  - 🔴 Let an MCP call move to the background to deliver an incoming message even when ordinary automatic backgrounding is off, under `tengu_velvet_panda` and `tengu_bubbly_meteor`, both on by default for eligible non-interactive sessions; calls whose result is still being awaited stay in front.
  - 🔴 Queued completion notices without starting another turn for MCP calls moved to the background to deliver a message, a route under `tengu_velvet_panda` and `tengu_bubbly_meteor`, both on by default.
  - 🔴 Warned that an MCP call moved aside for an incoming message may lose its result soon after the reply; this path uses `tengu_velvet_panda` and `tengu_bubbly_meteor`, both on by default.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP task calls to consider whether the caller still needs the result when choosing whether to move the call into the background.
- Organization connector rules for MCP servers
  - 🔴 Rechecked the organization's connector rules for MCP file uploads with the current authorization headers instead of inferring them from the tool's saved connection.
  - 🔴 Rechecked the organization's connector rules before showing an MCP task's input request or a tool's URL approval prompt, so Claude Code doesn't show a prompt for a connector it can't use.
  - 🔴 Returned `unsupported_input` for an MCP task input refused by the organization's connector rules and stopped polling that task.
  - 🔴 Stopped retrying MCP tool listings automatically when the organization's connector check has put the connection on hold.
  - 🔴 Changed the remotely controlled connector block for plugin HTTP and SSE MCP servers to wrap every such connection and read the block setting on each request, so turning it on reaches connections opened while it was off; an address it can't check now fails with `MCP request refused: connector check failed`.
  - 🔴 Added connector identity and server origin to generated MCP sign-in tools so permission checks can identify the destination behind the tool.
  - 🔴 Changed HTTP and SSE MCP connections to check the server address and outgoing requests against the connector rules before sending them, and to stop an existing transport's messages if those rules no longer permit it.
  - 🔴 Added more specific failure messages when managed policy blocks an MCP server, and closed an existing blocked connection when the connector rules can identify it.
  - 🔴 Changed blocked MCP calls to wait up to one second for cleanup of an existing connection before reporting the policy error, when the connector rules can identify that server.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP sign-in to check `headersHelper`-supplied headers against the organization's connector rules before sending them on the configured route.
  - 🔴 Sent an empty `roots/list` answer, instead of the workspace folders, to MCP servers the connector block holds back where an organization enforces `require_connector_block_on_plugin_servers`.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP `roots/list` to use the server's name and current rules when deciding which roots it can see.
  - 🔴 Changed an MCP task's roots reply to honor an empty roots response from the session's configured server as well as the live connection.
- Connector file references and uploads
  - 🔴 Let eligible connector tools accept files from their own job's temporary folder, on the file-input path controlled by `tengu_walnut_tiller`, on by default.
  - 🔴 Kept ordinary values in mixed connector arguments usable when file sends are off, as they are by default under `tengu_steady_quail`, while refusing file references in those same arguments.
  - 🔴 Let compatible connectors request a multipart POST for a file, with the filename and media type carried in the form, through the file-send feature under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Let eligible connector upload targets set a `Content-Type` header and rejected duplicate upload headers regardless of letter case, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Accepted any successful 2xx response for authenticated connector uploads, instead of requiring HTTP 200 with a particular response body, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Avoided resending non-repeatable connector uploads when the first attempt may already have reached the server, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Handled connector replies that ask Claude Code to repeat a call with updated request state, trying up to ten more times per phase of a file send, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Improved connector file-send errors to say whether files already went out, whether the connector call may have run, and what error it returned, without repeating private upload details, when `tengu_steady_quail` is on; it ships off.
  - 🔴 Stopped connector file sends when the signed-in Claude account or organization changes after the approval prompt, and asked for fresh approval, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Stopped approved connector file sends with a separate error when Claude Code can't confirm that the sign-in still matches, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Told Claude what kind of local file path to supply after it writes text or a connector file object into a file argument, including when the connector expects a list even for one file, under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Counted runs of apostrophes or backticks at their escaped width when checking that a connector's full name fits in a file approval prompt, and explained the calculation in the refusal; connector file sends run under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Kept the connector call's own error visible after Escape once it has started a file send under `tengu_steady_quail`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Let MCP servers using the files extension (`io.modelcontextprotocol/files`) ask Claude Code to upload a file by POST, including as a form with named text fields and a file field, besides PUT; an upload entry that breaks the form rules is refused with a message saying so.
  - 🔴 Kept checking for MCP file-upload forms shown earlier in the same conversation after a later tool definition drops them, so file requests don't silently go to the connector as plain text.
  - 🔴 Expanded the MCP upload schema reader for eligible local Claude connectors to handle supported mixtures of file and non-file choices, nullable arrays, and single-file or list choices, behind `tengu_steady_quail`, which is off by default, and the `allow_connector_file_uploads` policy.
- Connector file downloads
  - 🔴 Added downloads straight from HTTP MCP servers configured in local or user scope (HTTPS, or HTTP on localhost) when a tool result offers a file at the server's own address, sending the server's credentials only to the same origin and refusing unsafe request headers; it's behind the `tengu_rustling_juggling_acorn` flag and the existing downloads flag `tengu_piped_planet`, both off by default, and the `allow_connector_file_downloads` policy.
  - 🔴 Accepted file downloads offered in tool results by HTTP MCP servers from local or user settings, fetched from a link on the server's own HTTPS (or local) address, up to 500 MB, when organization policy allows connector file downloads; behind the `tengu_piped_planet` and `tengu_rustling_juggling_acorn` flags, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Added notes for the new direct connector-download route from local and user HTTP MCP servers when a link or its headers can't be used, its address is private, local, Anthropic's own or behind a proxy, or its storage answers with a redirect; the route needs the `tengu_rustling_juggling_acorn` and `tengu_piped_planet` flags, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Required HTTPS and public addresses for external connector download links, refused redirects, and kept connector credentials on the connector's own authorized route for direct downloads, behind `tengu_piped_planet` and `tengu_rustling_juggling_acorn`, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Set a 60-second request timeout for direct connector file downloads, behind `tengu_piped_planet` and `tengu_rustling_juggling_acorn`, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Distinguished expired links, missing files, refused requests, and redirects in direct connector download errors, behind `tengu_piped_planet` and `tengu_rustling_juggling_acorn`, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Raised the connector file download limit from 500 MB to 5 GB when the `tengu_piped_planet` flag is on, as it isn't by default: files over 64 MB come in parts that pick up again after a cut-off, get 5 minutes plus a second for every 2 MB, and need storage that can send parts and mark the file's version once they pass 500 MB.
  - 🔴 Added connector download notes for a file that changed while it was downloading and for storage that can't send a large file in parts or doesn't mark its version, when the `tengu_piped_planet` flag is on, as it isn't by default.
  - 🔴 Saved connector downloads under the offered file's own cleaned-up name when it's free, instead of only a generated `download-…` name, and pointed the model to each saved name by its mark in the file list at the start of the tool's result, when the `tengu_piped_planet` flag is on, as it isn't by default.
  - 🔴 Kept usable original filenames for connector downloads that appear in the tool's result list, with collision suffixes and a `connector-` prefix for names that could be mistaken for project files, behind `tengu_piped_planet`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Made background MCP tasks collect offered connector downloads when the caller isn't waiting and download policy allows it; `tengu_piped_planet` ships off, and `tengu_glowing_lark` defaults on for supported local CLI sessions.
  - 🔴 Changed MCP file download capability negotiation to consider eligible direct connectors as well as Claude-managed connectors, when connector downloads are enabled and the server advertises support.
  - 🔴 Changed cloud connector-download requests to ask for the containing folder for named files, while keeping single-file requests for generated download names.
  - 🔴 Created the configured connector download folder if it's missing after a qualifying Drive or Gmail download, and added a `.gitignore` when the folder is empty and safe to use.
  - 🔴 Saved connector downloads of Markdown and JSON files as `.md` and `.json` instead of `.txt`, and HEIC, HEIF, TIFF and QuickTime files as `.heic`, `.heif`, `.tiff` and `.mov` instead of `.bin`, when the `tengu_piped_planet` flag is on, as it isn't by default.
- Tool search and deferred tools
  - 🔴 Added a note telling Claude which apps the person has connected to the chat, for Adobe, Adobe for creativity, Firecrawl, Granola, Idiolect, Intuit QuickBooks and Pipedrive connectors whose tools wait behind tool search, so it loads them when a request could use them; it's for sessions started from claude.ai chat, behind the `tengu_mossy_gable` and `tengu_snoopy_thimble` flags, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Sent plugin-provided MCP tools with their full definitions while tool search is on, instead of holding them back for search, in claude.ai chat sessions that run without a disk; it's behind the `tengu_abundant_owl` flag, which is on by default.
- Cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Let a hosted project folder be prepared when the first tool request names one of its parent directories as well as when it names the folder itself.
  - 🔴 Recognized `no_runner_coming` as a reason a cloud request wasn't forwarded, alongside `container_limit`.
- Attachments in cloud and Remote Control sessions
  - 🔴 Skipped another cloud download when a resent attachment is already present as a qualifying read-only file, behind `tengu_lazy_pearl`, which ships off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed which attachments let a message be sent before its files arrive: files without an extension and `.ipynb` notebooks from uploads now wait for their files, and connector files no longer wait in diskless sessions; this removes the `tengu_deep_sphinx` and `tengu_misty_fiddle` flags and turns `tengu_pure_squid` on by default in diskless sessions.
  - 🔴 Stopped automatic redirects on diskless attachment downloads so they stay on the requested endpoint.
  - 🔴 Kept a remote attachment resend from deleting older spill files while it opens a new temporary download file.
  - 🔴 Let eligible silent prompt resends pick up their file list when they don't carry ordinary attachments.
- 🔴 Added cleanup of forgotten temporary upload files older than 10 minutes in Anthropic-hosted cloud sessions when `tengu_cozy_hamming` is on (off by default).
- Files Claude sends from cloud and Remote Control sessions
  - 🔴 Fell back to sending the original Office attachment alone after a refusal of the combined PDF upload that Claude Code treats as permanent for that request but not for every send.
  - 🔴 Omitted an Office attachment's PDF preview if the original and preview together would exceed the 30 MiB request budget, or a lower limit reported by the server.
  - 🔴 Stopped retrying attachment uploads that time out after 35 seconds when an Office PDF preview is included and the original exceeds 1 MiB.
  - 🔴 Stopped switching to an original-only Office upload after the service rate-limits a send that includes a PDF preview.
  - 🔴 Added PDF previews to Word and PowerPoint files Claude sends from cloud and Remote Control sessions: when `SANDBOX_OFFICE_PDF_DIR` holds a PDF named after the file's SHA-256 and `SANDBOX_OFFICE_PDF_SEND` lists `docx` or `pptx`, the upload carries it as `office_preview`, and is posted again without it if the server refuses it.
  - 🔴 Sent a matching PDF preview with Claude attachment uploads of `.docx` and `.pptx` originals on non-Windows hosts when `SANDBOX_OFFICE_PDF_DIR` is set and `SANDBOX_OFFICE_PDF_SEND` lists that file type.
  - 🔴 Stopped a `Retry-After` response header by itself from ruling out another attachment upload attempt on server errors other than HTTP 503.
  - 🔴 Continued the attachment upload when only the optional skill-file validation read fails, while still reporting a failure when the upload itself needs bytes it can't read.
- Cloud session workers
  - 🔴 Added current turn updates to remote worker status so the client can follow which turn the worker is reporting.
  - 🔴 Changed cloud tool hosting's setup failure message to point to debug output on the terminal or name the debug log when it's available.
  - 🔴 Computed disk use in the cloud-session resource watchdog the way `df` does, leaving out space reserved for root; the watchdog is behind the `tengu_ccr_disk_watchdog` flag, off by default.
  - 🔴 Let `--session-tunnel` token updates carry a `git_token`, which Claude Code gives the commands it runs as `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ACCESS_TOKEN`, and kept the tunnel dialing instead of quitting over an expired token while it can still receive new tokens.
- Cloud session API endpoints
  - 🔴 Retried creating a cloud session on the newer `/v1/code/sessions` endpoint when the older `/v1/sessions` endpoint answers that it's retired (`sessions_v1_retired`).
  - 🔴 Added a remote switch that creates cloud sessions on `/v1/code/sessions` first and repeats the request on `/v1/sessions` if the new endpoint answers 400, 404, 405 or 422, behind the `tengu_mellow_popcorn` flag (with `tengu_tidy_panda` for sources it hasn't checked), both off by default.
  - 🔴 Made cloud-session fetch, update, archive, and unarchive requests retry on `/v1/code/sessions` when `/v1/sessions` reports it's retired, using the time left from the original request.
  - 🔴 Made Remote Control session creation retry on the newer `/v1/code/sessions` API when `/v1/sessions` answers that it's retired.
  - 🔴 Retried Remote Control's peer session list and session archiving on `/v1/code/sessions` when `/v1/sessions` answers that it's retired, while the `tengu_ccr_v2_session_crud_cli` flag is off, as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Retried cloud session list and detail requests on the newer API when the old API reports that it's retired, even with `tengu_ccr_v2_session_crud_cli` off as it is by default.
  - 🔴 Added the sessions API's `connection_status` (connected or disconnected), `effort_level` and `last_served_model` to the cloud session records Claude Code builds, when the API sends them.
  - 🔴 Added a page-size option to cloud session transcript requests, accepting 1 to 1,000 events and still defaulting to 200.
  - 🔴 Added an explicit request error for a cloud transcript page size that isn't a whole number from 1 to 1,000.
- Remote Control
  - 🔴 Refused to start Remote Control's spawned sessions when the spawn folder isn't owned by you or is open to other users, where it used to check only that it was a folder.
  - 🔴 Let the remote policy recheck report when it holds a prompt and use the keepalive path for pending approvals with card input, when remote keepalives are on.
  - 🔴 Used the bridge server's configured origin when describing forwarded tools in remote tool announcements.
  - 🔴 Sent the MCP connector name and source with permission requests forwarded to a remote approval surface.
  - 🔴 Added the worker's machine tag to eligible forked cloud sessions when `tengu_twinkling_crown` is on (off by default).
  - 🔴 Stopped polling the remote permission policy after the host stops.
  - 🔴 Raised the largest event that `claude remote-control`'s watch stream accepts from about 1 MB to 3 MB before it drops the stream as oversized.
  - 🔴 Added `queued_turn_count` to the result messages Remote Control sessions send, with the number of queued turns.
  - 🔴 Marked a sandbox network approval as answerable outside the terminal only if the bridge sent the request.
  - 🔴 Changed the waiting status sent to remote viewers so local prompts to make Auto mode the default or approve a cross-session message don't announce a wait there.
  - 🔴 Included the MCP server and the reason for asking in Remote Control's tool approval request when that information is available.
  - 🔴 Sent status updates over bridge connections, except for the requesting state.
