  - 🔴 Added a `WorkspaceDiffForRemote` request that gives a remote session this machine's workspace diff under the launch permission rules, capped by size and at about 30,000 lines, with limits of 2 seconds per file and 6 seconds in total.
  - 🟢 Changed the notice Remote Control clients see while a prompt waits in the terminal to "Answer the prompt in the terminal to continue", sent as `dialog:terminal_prompt` instead of `dialog:terminal_permission`.
- Stop from Remote Control and the web
  - 🔴 Swapped the remote interrupted-turn Stop switch from `tengu_drifting_seahorse` to the default-off `tengu_settled_wren_killswitch`.
  - 🔴 Added one stop report per headless Stop, including whether a turn was running, for direct interrupts, queued interrupts, and stopped handoffs.
  - 🔴 Kept the Stop notice for a previously saved interrupted turn without needing the additional remote flag that used to control it.
- Streaming to claude.ai and the mobile app
  - 🔴 Sped up the built-in `hearthbot` server's `reply` and thread-start tool calls in sessions streamed to claude.ai: once the call's text starts arriving, the buffered stream is sent after the shorter first-flush delay instead of waiting out the regular flush interval; the `tengu_cosmic_nautilus` flag is on by default.
  - 🔴 Changed remote upload ordering to put message text first when `tengu_cosmic_nautilus` is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Streamed partial messages from spawned Remote Control sessions that someone may be watching, behind the `tengu_bridge_partial_messages` flag, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Added a network check after two Remote Control heartbeats in a row fail to reach the server: Claude Code looks up the API host, opens a connection and requests `/api/hello`, at most once every 10 minutes, and adds where it failed to its heartbeat diagnostics; it's skipped behind a proxy and stops after December 20, 2026.
- Late remote approvals
  - 🔴 Changed late remote approvals under `tengu_steady_canyon` (off by default) to check the earlier call against the saved conversation and only use an answer that still applies and hasn't already been used.
  - 🔴 Added the `late_allow_not_run` event under `tengu_steady_canyon` (off by default) to tell remote clients why a late approval didn't run, including expiration, a newer prompt running, or the earlier call being gone.
  - 🔴 Reported a late approval as not run if the headless batch carrying it can't start.
  - 🔴 Refused to run a tool call approved late, after the conversation moved on, when Claude Code can't confirm it saved the record that the approval is used up, so the call can't run twice; Claude is told to make the call again.
  - 🔴 Let a queued approval answer move ahead of a selected remote prompt batch that hasn't started when `tengu_sprightly_anchor` is on (off by default).
- Resuming interrupted turns
  - 🔴 Let a turn that a session resumes automatically give way to the person's next prompt when the interrupted turn had ended on an API error after an answer with no tool calls while earlier calls stayed open, in sessions that resume interrupted turns (`CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN`). (behind `tengu_foamy_spring` and `tengu_quizzical_globe` and `tengu_expressive_creek`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Started connecting cached MCP connectors while a resumed, interrupted turn waits for the tool behind an approval given before a restart, so that tool can join and run; this used to need the `tengu_sorted_willow` flag, which was off by default and is now gone.
  - 🔴 Changed headless sessions to report why an earlier pending approval didn't run when it's dropped during a resumed turn, and to stop treating the absence of an answer as a decline.
  - 🔴 Switched the interrupted-turn recovery check for rate limits, missing request tokens, server errors, and missing models from `tengu_cosmic_crayon` and `tengu_cozy_pinwheel` to whether the headless session is remote.
  - 🔴 Changed interrupted-turn recovery so it only yields to newer input after an API error past an open tool call when `tengu_mutable_goblet` is on (off by default).
- Claude Projects sessions
  - 🔴 Added the coordinator's post to the user's reply in Claude Projects sessions: when the user answers a coordinator message in the project's main chat, Claude sees that message (up to 8,000 characters) marked as the coordinator's words that ask nothing by themselves, or a note that it couldn't be read, so the answer approves nothing.
  - 🔴 Always pointed Claude to the Projects tool for reading a Project's synced sources in claude.ai chat and remote Cowork sessions, instead of to matching connector tools (the `tengu_radiant_badger` flag that gated this, off by default, is gone), and explained upload names that carry a bracketed ID.
  - 🔴 Allowed a Remote Control project reply to set or clear a status reminder, with at least 60 seconds between reminders while the server asks for one.
  - 🔴 Reported an error when the Claude Projects list endpoint replies without an array of projects.
  - 🔴 Redacted API keys and tokens (`sk-ant-…` and `eyJ…` strings) from Projects API error messages.
  - 🔴 Expanded the Project list response Claude Code reads to include membership account IDs, topics, icons, colors, pause time, last activity, and unread and working-session indicators when present.
  - 🔴 Read the caller's membership account ID from single-Project responses as well as from the Project list.
  - 🔴 Read the Project message response's private-message and sender-type indicators when the service supplies them.
  - 🔴 Broadened the event-stream parser for Project RPC replies to handle UTF-8 byte-order marks and streams that use carriage returns as line breaks.
  - 🔴 Let blocking tool hooks review the files a hosted project read would bring into the session before the worker writes the files into its project folder.
  - 🔴 Changed hosted project reads to try restoring upload links once when the runner has mounted a project folder that lists those uploads.
  - 🔴 Removed an early 255-byte filename filter from hosted project reads.
- Remote Cowork sessions
  - 🔴 Changed the `tengu_inked_lantern` flag, which gates docs-connector behavior in diskless sessions, to default on in remote Cowork sessions when the service sends no value, so there hooks from the user's own Claude Code files can no longer replace a docs-connector tool's output; elsewhere it still defaults off.
  - 🔴 Turned on the automatic safety check for saving and deleting project docs by default in remote Cowork sessions started from a claude.ai chat, and refused the save or delete, saying the check wasn't available, when it can't run.
- Cowork memory
  - 🔴 Added short waits for the memory service's file pick in remote Cowork sessions: a turn waits up to the service's requested time, at most 350 ms, for a pick still being fetched for the user's message, and the first model request waits at most 100 ms more; `CLAUDE_CODE_MEMORY_CONTEXT_NO_WAIT` skips the new waits too.
  - 🔴 Added a second request to the memory service when its file pick for a message comes back marked as not final in remote Cowork sessions, at most twice per message and 200 times per session.
  - 🔴 Kept prefetched Cowork memory files away from models they don't list, removing their text from requests to those models, such as an advisor model.
  - 🔴 Added memory files that the memory service prefetches for the user's latest message to the memory context of remote Cowork sessions, leaving out files the conversation already holds and sections past a chat byte budget the service sets; it's behind the `tengu_misty_anchor` flag, which defaults on only in diskless launches that have no fetched or cached flags.
  - 🔴 Withdrew prefetched memory files from remote Cowork conversations when the memory service's list of models that may keep them stops covering the session's model or says `none`, telling Claude not to rely on anything they said.
  - 🔴 Let SDK sessions that set `excludeDynamicSections` reuse the last memory section while it's rebuilt, when the memories it named haven't changed, so the request doesn't wait on the rebuild; it's on by default through `tengu_melodic_mitten` where memory sync is allowed.
  - 🔴 Re-attached the memory snapshot after compaction in remote Cowork sessions unless memory was written since it was fetched, behind the `tengu_fizzy_adleman` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Returned a resumed session from the Teleport relay to standard request handling when the route withdraws memory files, just as it already did when account memory was withdrawn.
  - 🔴 Changed Cowork memory context to let a session's first turn go ahead with the memory it already holds, instead of waiting for a fresh read, when the last memory check is under 30 seconds old; this window used to come from the `tengu_mighty_raccoon` flag, which defaulted to 0, so the first turn waited.
  - 🔴 Changed how diskless hosted sessions hold, prefetch and attach memory context from the person's computer, with an optional wait for a memory send at the start of a turn behind the `tengu_toasty_floyd` flag, off by default.
- Artifacts
  - 🔴 Included `folder_audience` in owner-only Artifact publish results when the folder may be visible to others.
  - 🔴 Kept the external-content mark on Artifact listing results when any returned Artifact is marked, including listings by type when the type catalog is enabled.
  - 🔴 Skipped exposing the built-in artifact link when the artifact's MCP access is hidden or its connector is still being held back.
  - 🔴 Made the commands that create Design, Slides and Motion artifacts available in cloud sessions that are served the `claude-artifacts` connector, not only where artifacts are turned on.
  - 🔴 Kept automatic Artifact write refusals in scheduled sessions from taking priority over a matching mod `tool.judge` hook, just as they already deferred to `tool.check` hooks; database writes depend on `tengu_cobalt_plinth_sedge`, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Kept the external-content mark on Artifact database-read prompts out of the explanation passed to the permission classifier, when `tengu_cobalt_plinth_sedge` enables the database actions; it's off by default.
  - 🔴 Shortened the instructions Claude receives when an artifact is attached, keeping the reminder to reread it before editing and that publishes from elsewhere don't start a turn.
  - 🔴 Expanded the Artifact tool's permission checks on script and endpoint calls to cover rules for individual field edits and version conditions.
  - 🔴 Set the `artifact_publish` mark on SDK tool-call display entries only for messages marked as the model's own reply, instead of on every assistant message and never on converted messages.
  - 🔴 Allowed scheduled Artifact updates to include CSS directly under `project/` in the existing permission check for updating a type instance.
  - 🟢 Made `disallowedTools` rules and agent tool lists that name the Artifact tool or one of its sibling tools also cover MCP tools standing in for them.
- Artifact actions approved by hooks or mods
  - 🔴 Stopped Artifacts calls approved by a hook, or made by a plugin mod for itself, from recording publish consent, remembering an ask's answer, or marking comments and notices as seen.
  - 🔴 Stopped hook approvals and mod-owned Artifact watch calls from counting as user approval to start automatic replies.
- Claude in Chrome
  - 🔴 Answered a plugin's request to connect a Claude in Chrome MCP server that the organization's policy blocks with a policy refusal, "Claude in Chrome is disabled by your organization's policy.", instead of a connection failure.
  - 🔴 Added a `chrome_hint_superseded` field to remote sessions' metadata, set when a browser-device hint is superseded, behind the `tengu_vivid_key` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added the Claude in Chrome availability explanation to the reasons an MCP server can give for being unavailable.
  - 🔴 Added an own-tab approval marker to Claude in Chrome tool requests when the server supports it and the existing Accept Edits checks let Claude create a tab without asking.
  - 🟢 Sent the named site with a Claude in Chrome permission request forwarded over Remote Control when the browser action supplies it.
- Self-hosted runners
  - 🔴 Changed self-hosted runner sessions whose tools host has its own git token to end only when the server says the session itself is gone, not on any not-found answer, when re-presenting a refreshed session token on `/remote` fails.
  - 🔴 Passed a self-hosted runner operator's `skipWebFetchPreflight` setting from the runner's host config to tools-only sessions, which now get it in their own `settings.json`.
  - 🔴 Changed `claude self-hosted-runner --agent-proxy-url` to clone http(s) repositories through that proxy without the machine's git login, so the git host's credential has to be saved at the proxy; `--no-clone-through-agent-proxy` turns this off, and `--use-anthropic-git-proxy` skips it.
  - 🔴 Refused to start `claude self-hosted-runner` with `--agent-proxy-url` when git is older than 2.31, unless `--use-anthropic-git-proxy` or `--no-clone-through-agent-proxy` is set.
  - 🔴 Passed the runner's Git credential to child processes started by the HTTP MCP server when the runner supplies it.
  - 🔴 Made self-hosted runners refuse a session that comes with an uploaded copy of the project (`CCR_SEED_BUNDLE_PATH` in its environment), failing it with "The cloud machine cannot unpack the uploaded copy of this project."; the runner also keeps `CCR_SEED_BUNDLE_PATH` and `CCR_SEED_BUNDLE_NO_SAVED_WORK` out of setup scripts and sessions.
  - 🔴 Made `claude self-hosted-runner` refuse a session that starts from an uploaded copy of a local project, with "The cloud machine cannot unpack the uploaded copy of this project."
  - 🔴 Changed self-hosted runners that host a session's tools to run the session's restore scripts, which put back its uncommitted work, before starting it, for up to 9.5 minutes; if one fails, can't start or runs out of time, the runner logs that the session may lack unpushed work and starts it anyway.
  - 🔴 Changed the self-hosted runner's work-available stream to be read as server-sent events, acting on an event when it ends and skipping any event over 64 KiB, instead of reacting to each `event: work_available` line as it arrived.
  - 🔴 Expanded which self-hosted runner sessions can use UI-surface features, with a validated writer statement still required for the remote transport.
- Built-in `gh` and GitHub access
  - 🔴 Added self-hosted runner log lines for the built-in `gh`: one at start-up saying it's on, or naming why no session will get it when `--use-anthropic-gh` is set explicitly, and per-session lines saying whether Claude Code set it up, why not, or that it's still trying.
  - 🔴 Required a private per-relay credential for the cloud agent proxy's built-in `gh`-only relay, and stopped setup when Claude Code couldn't save that credential for the current account alone.
  - 🔴 Made the built-in `gh` authenticate to the session's GitHub proxy with the secret saved next to its CA bundle.
  - 🔴 Added an error for the built-in `gh` when the session's GitHub proxy rejects its secret (HTTP 407), asking for one retry and saying `gh` can't be used until the proxy restarts if it fails again.
  - 🔴 Limited the built-in `gh`'s local proxy to `api.github.com` on port 443 and to callers that present the session's secret, answering others with a 403 or 407, and made it refuse new connections with a 403 once the session's model provider, nonessential-traffic setting or GitHub tool rules stop allowing it.
  - 🔴 Made the built-in `gh` relay cap waiting unauthenticated connections at 256 and close each one after 10 seconds if it hasn't identified its session.
  - 🟢 Added a gh-only mode to the session proxy, in which only `gh` reaches github.com through the proxy with the GitHub sign-in Anthropic holds for the session; Claude is told that git and other tools use the machine's own network and sign-in, and that a 403 from the proxy is a refusal not to retry.
  - 🟢 Offered Claude Code's built-in `gh api` to `claude self-hosted-runner` sessions with no `gh` installed, on by default, sending github.com REST requests through Anthropic, which adds the GitHub credential it holds for the session; `--use-anthropic-gh false` or `SELF_HOSTED_RUNNER_USE_ANTHROPIC_GH=0` turns it off, and it's withheld on Windows, at `--capacity` above 1, with `--agent-proxy-url`, and in other setups the runner's log names.
  - 🟢 Added a built-in `gh api` for sessions on self-hosted runners without the agent proxy: where the session's PATH has no `gh`, Claude Code writes a stand-in that sends requests for github.com through Anthropic, and skips it when nonessential traffic is disabled, the model provider isn't Anthropic's API, or, when it's only offered by default rather than turned on with `--use-anthropic-gh`, the session's settings deny Claude's GitHub tools.
  - 🟢 Added Anthropic's built-in `gh api` for eligible self-hosted cloud runners without `CCR_AGENT_PROXY_ENABLED` or an installed `gh`, unless the runner turns it off with `--use-anthropic-gh false`.
- Cloud agent proxy
  - 🔴 Cut everything up to an `@` from each word of the failures the agent proxy records for its status page, so user names and passwords written into URLs no longer show there.
  - 🔴 Removed the agent proxy's local relay in Anthropic cloud sessions, which could hand a tool's port-443 connection to a relay holding renewable tickets after reading its TLS hello; those connections now always go through the agent proxy's tunnel.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud agent proxy's retry wait to add up to 25% jitter to a server's `Retry-After` value, so the longest server-directed wait can reach 6 minutes and 15 seconds instead of 5 minutes.
  - 🔴 Made the cloud agent proxy enforce its 8 KiB request-header limit on complete headers as well as partial ones.
  - 🔴 Made the cloud agent proxy reject an invalid port in a selective-relay request before it opens a direct connection.
  - 🔴 Added another recognized `ca-certificates-java` package build so the cloud agent proxy can update its Java trust store.
- Product events in cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_WITHHOLD_PRODUCT_EVENTS` for cloud sessions: when `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` is set and this variable is anything but empty or `0`, Claude Code sends no first-party product events, Datadog logs or error reports, skips the feedback survey, refuses plugin requests that post event batches with `this session sends no product events`, and passes the variable on to the Claude Code processes it starts.
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_WITHHOLD_PRODUCT_EVENTS`: in a remote session (`CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE`), setting it turns off error reporting and Datadog logging, and a background job's launch request keeps it when its value is `1`.
  - 🔴 Passed `CLAUDE_CODE_WITHHOLD_PRODUCT_EVENTS` to terminal teammates alongside the parent's other supported environment variables.
  - 🔴 Passed `CLAUDE_CODE_LAUNCHED_BY_SESSION_IDS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_NOTES_DIR` and `CLAUDE_CODE_WITHHOLD_PRODUCT_EVENTS` from a client to the background session it starts, and stopped background session processes from inheriting the first two, or `CLAUDE_BG_GATEWAY_ROUTED`, from the background service's own environment.
- Data-flow records and reports
  - 🔴 Added the Claude Code sessions that launched a session (up to 32, passed to child sessions in the new `CLAUDE_CODE_LAUNCHED_BY_SESSION_IDS` variable) to the `data_flow` field sent with API requests, as edges saying those sessions wrote to this one; the field is behind the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag, which is off by default, and the `allow_data_flow` policy.
  - 🔴 Retried side queries without the `data_flow` field after an HTTP 413 that isn't a known prompt-too-long error, as they already were after a 400 naming the field; behind the same off-by-default `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag.
  - 🔴 Added `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_NOTES_DIR` to the environment of the commands, hooks and plugin processes Claude Code starts outside diskless sessions, when the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag is on (off by default) and organization policy allows data flow.
  - 🔴 Set `CLAUDE_CODE_LAUNCHED_BY_SESSION_IDS` to the session's ID for commands plugins run with `$.process.run`, and `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_NOTES_DIR` as well when the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag, which is off by default, and the `allow_data_flow` policy allow it.
  - 🔴 Passed the launching session IDs to local stdio MCP subprocesses in `CLAUDE_CODE_LAUNCHED_BY_SESSION_IDS`.
- 🔴 Registered extra working directories supplied when a parked session is claimed as launch directories and made them available to the `CLAUDE.md` loader.
- Cross-computer tool calls
  - 🔴 Changed the receiving side of cross-computer tool calls to check them without carrying over session settings that grant permission.
  - 🔴 Carried the connector origin into stand-in tool descriptions when the other computer isn't connected.
- 🔴 Changed device MCP consent and re-consent prompts to label the count of additional entries as servers.
- Subagents and forks
  - 🔴 Kept restricted background agents, such as background comment agents, under their limits when they're resumed: the limit is saved with the agent and with agents it starts and is restored on resume, and a resume that can't restore it fails saying the agent "ran under limits this process can't apply".
  - 🔴 Let the service raise the concurrent subagent limit above 20, up to 64, through the `tengu_valiant_reef` flag (20 by default), except in remote and Cowork sessions; `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS` still takes precedence. (behind `tengu_amber_kestrel`, on by default)
  - 🔴 Added delivery of the reports a foreground subagent sends back to the main agent beside that subagent's result in remote non-interactive sessions, behind the `tengu_glittery_robin` flag, which is off by default.
- Agent teams
  - 🔴 Kept pending teammate notes across auto-compaction for in-process Agent Teams runs so they can still reach the teammate on a later turn.
  - 🔴 Counted an in-process teammate's compaction work against its resumable agent loop.
  - 🔴 Kept a mod's permission exceptions for its own tool call from carrying over to a new Agent Teams teammate in a terminal pane.
  - 🔴 Carried the spawning hook's origin into in-process Agent Teams compaction when automatic compaction is on.
- Terminal program status (OSC 7501)
  - 🔴 Added a background-session message that reports whether all, some, or none of its attached viewers support Program Status Protocol updates.
  - 🔴 Changed remote turn status to clear when a turn finishes or a queued batch is drained without starting a turn.
  - 🟢 Made OSC 7501 status report a turn as working while background commands, eligible monitors or MCP tasks are still running, and include those tasks as child entries when the terminal keeps them.
  - 🟢 Kept idle compaction from looking like a new turn in terminals that read OSC 7501 status.
- 🔴 Added an `opts.sharedHeadLength` option to `agent()` in dynamic workflow scripts that sends that many leading prompt characters as a separate first message and labels the agent from the rest; a negative or fractional value refuses the spawn.
- Goals
  - 🔴 Kept `ProposeGoal` as an alias of the goal tool's new name, `SetGoal`, so permission rules, hook matchers and resumed transcripts that name `ProposeGoal` apply to `SetGoal`.
  - 🔴 Stopped a paused goal's evaluator from blocking the `Stop` hook at the end of a forked slash command.
- 🔴 Added `plan_entered_by` to the permission-mode metadata that cloud session workers report when entering plan mode, and clear it when the mode stays the same but who entered plan mode is gone.
- Code Mode
  - 🔴 Let Code Mode continue its eligibility check when the final plugin check succeeds with no blockers, while still refusing when that check finds issues or can't run.
  - 🔴 Checked the active startup model override for Code Mode eligibility when the terminal starts with `--model default`.
- 🔴 Updated Claude's PR Steward instructions to describe fixing CI and working through review comments rather than merging, when `tengu_federated_flask` is on; it ships off by default.
- 🔴 Fixed width calculations in the chart plugin for several wide symbols and for Indic spacing marks.
- 🔴 Turned the `tengu_virtual_pancake` flag on by default in diskless sessions, so text pasted into their prompts is wrapped in paste tags.
- Typing to select in menus
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select an option in the unattended serving consent prompt.
  - 🔴 Enabled typing to select options in the device re-enrollment, home settings, and device MCP consent prompts.
- 🔴 Used the newer output-preview format for saved Bash results when `tengu_zippy_sparrow` is on; the flag ships off by default.
- 🔴 Cleaned up AskUserQuestion activity summaries by removing detected secrets and invisible or unsafe text before display.
- 🔴 Included the active model in an SDK model-switch error when a server refusal makes Claude Code drop the unconfirmed launch model.
- 🔴 Sent the partial thinking of an interrupted turn back to the API as an unsigned thinking block in SDK sessions without the `tengu_velvety_galaxy` flag, which was removed.
- 🔴 Ran reactive compaction in cloud sessions despite a `PreCompact` hook's block when the request can't be sent uncompacted, behind the `tengu_typed_snail` flag, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Added stopping of background shell commands when a remote session's conversation is rewound past the calls that started them, removing their pending results too; it's behind the `tengu_pruned_cedar` flag, off by default.
- 🔴 Changed headless `--resume-session-at` to put discarded plugin tool calls on the record when it trims the transcript.
- 🔴 Passed current extra-usage status alongside successful admin request checks in terminal Team account dialogs.
- 🔴 Counted auto mode's permission classifier calls, where they're billed, and model calls stopped or cut off mid-response, with output tokens estimated from what had streamed, in the SDK's `total_cost_usd` and per-model usage, which `--max-budget-usd` checks.
- 🔴 Added a refusal in `-p`, SDK and cloud sessions for a prompt whose estimated tokens exceed the model's largest context window, replying "This message is too long to send, so Claude didn't get it. Send a shorter one." instead of sending it; it's behind the `tengu_warm_crescent` flag, off by default, and applies only with Anthropic's own API.
- `/use-case`
  - 🔴 Added telemetry for `/use-case` that distinguishes clearing the saved choice from being unable to confirm the deletion.
  - 🔴 Stopped scheduled (cron) runs from sending a stored `program_use_case_id` when several program use cases are offered and the person hasn't acted as the current account in that process.
- 🔴 Blocked sending feedback transcripts to Anthropic when the session or its transcript doesn't allow that transfer under data residency rules.
- 🔴 Added the `tengu_peppy_muffin` and `tengu_woolly_gadget` switches to the VS Code handshake; both default to on.
- 🔴 Changed the sign-in screen to distinguish gateways offered by the organization from ones selected by the user when deciding which sign-in methods to show.
- 🔴 Changed the headless OAuth refresh request to identify the rejected token by hash when outgoing frames aren't persisted.
- 🔴 Skipped listing Bedrock inference profiles when the active provider isn't Bedrock, returning the default list instead.
- `claude gateway`
  - 🔴 Made `claude gateway` answer Vertex's request-size 400 errors with "Input is too long.", so Claude Code drops older images and compacts as it already did for Bedrock's.
  - 🔴 Made `claude gateway` report managed settings keys nested more than 64 levels deep, which Claude Code ignores, in place of the usual schema errors when it checks the settings it serves.
- Retry delays and jitter
  - 🔴 Randomized the 30-second retry delay by up to 25% for cloud event streams that keep retrying after the initial reconnect attempts.
  - 🔴 Varied MCP reconnect hold-off delays by up to 25% after the transport closes soon after connecting, so clients don't all retry at once.
- Settings files
  - 🔴 Ignored `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EARLY_KEY_GUARD`, `CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_PREWAIT_SERVERS_MS_BY_NAME`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_POWERSHELL_ALIAS_SHADOW_CHECK` and `CLAUDE_CODE_ARTIFACT_LIVE_WATCH` in a repository's `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/settings.local.json`, with a logged warning to set them in `~/.claude/settings.json` or managed settings.
  - 🔴 Ignored 15 more variables when a settings file's `env` block sets them, warning that only the environment Claude Code starts with can set them, among them `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_POWERSHELL_ALIAS_SHADOW_CHECK`, `CLAUDE_CODE_REQUIRED_PLUGINS_HOLD` and `CLAUDE_CODE_LAUNCHED_BY_SESSION_IDS`.
  - 🔴 Stopped loading project and local settings from `connector-downloads` folders inside Claude Code's per-project data folders, as it already did for the main connector downloads folder.
- 🔴 Rejected managed settings and policy helper payloads whose `hooks` is written as nested arrays holding values more than 64 levels deep, because such a value could hide a PreToolUse or PermissionRequest hook that can't be loaded; the error asks for `hooks` as an object of event names.
- Managed settings
  - 🔴 Refused remote managed settings nested more than 5,000 levels deep as a whole, reporting `The settings nest too deeply to be read` with the top-level keys involved and keeping any settings received earlier.
  - 🔴 Stopped retrying the remote managed settings fetch when a workload identity federation profile's token exchange fails with a reset, refused, aborted or closed connection, `ETIMEDOUT` or `EPIPE`; DNS failures, unreachable hosts, connection timeouts and HTTP 5xx, 429 or 408 still count as temporary.
  - 🔴 Changed policy-limit and managed-settings fetches in diskless sessions to retry up to 11 times with delays from 1 to 8 seconds, and to give up on a policy-limits connection handshake after 10 seconds.
  - 🟢 Contained unexpected errors while loading a single managed settings file, `managed-settings.json` or a file in `managed-settings.d`: Claude Code now rolls back what that file had merged, reports an error naming it, and keeps loading the others.
  - 🟢 Let managed settings set `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL_FORCE` and `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_SUBAGENT_MODEL` for first-party sessions Claude Desktop starts (`CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT=claude-desktop` without host-managed routing), in place of the value the session started with, unless the same settings also choose a provider, gateway or non-Anthropic base URL, in which case they're ignored with a logged warning.
  - 🟢 Added a managed-settings error for documents Claude Code fails to apply because of an internal bug: that document's settings are rolled back and the error says it `could not be applied because of a bug in Claude Code` and asks for a report to Anthropic support, as a startup error, or a status warning when the user can write the document.
- SDK and headless control protocol
  - 🔴 Added `message_uuid` to the SDK's reply to a message cancel, naming the dropped message when `cancelled` is true and no turn read it, because a client can reuse a request id.
  - 🔴 Added the cancelled message's `message_uuid` to a successful `cancel_async_message` reply in Remote Control sessions.
  - 🔴 Allowed `set_message_waiting` and `prompt_draft_activity` on the headless control-message channel.
  - 🔴 Allowed the headless worker waiting for a session claim to process `set_message_waiting` and `prompt_draft_activity`.
  - 🔴 Added `set_message_waiting` to the headless control protocol so a host can tell Claude Code it's holding a message; the worker clears the mark when it takes a prompt from the queue.
  - 🔴 Added `prompt_draft_activity` to the headless control protocol so a host can report typing while no turn is running; on a local first-party connection, `tengu_playful_shannon` (off by default) can use that time to start warming the prompt cache.
  - 🔴 Cleaned question text and tool summaries before showing them on SDK permission prompts.
  - 🔴 Added `whenUnanswered` to SDK PreToolUse callback hook matchers sent by the process that owns Claude Code's stdin: when the host's callbacks leave a file tool call unanswered for `afterSeconds`, the call goes ahead if its path lies in the folders the host listed (`within`, `readsAlsoWithin` for reads, minus `noWritesWithin` for writes) instead of being refused at the timeout; a malformed value is ignored with a warning.
  - 🔴 Let a tool call go ahead when Claude Desktop or another host app doesn't answer its `PreToolUse` hook in time, if the call's path lies in the folders the host left with the hook, telling the user that file tools work only in those folders until the host responds.
  - 🔴 Added `hooks_when_unanswered_kept` to headless SDK initialization replies so the host can tell whether its registered hooks include a `whenUnanswered` policy.
  - 🔴 Added `mcpServers` to the SDK's initialize request: under `--await-initialize`, Claude Code reads the Agent SDK's serializable MCP server configs from it at launch and handles them like one `--mcp-config` item, so stdio `env` and HTTP or SSE `headers` stay off the command line; the response's `mcp_servers_applied` says whether they were taken.
  - 🔴 Added `mcpServers` to the `initialize` request read with `--await-initialize`: the servers, a JSON object keyed by server name, load as the first `--mcp-config` entry, and a value that isn't an object or can't be read stops startup with an `initialize.mcpServers` error.
  - 🔴 Sent a `hook_progress` message with `backgrounded: true` to SDK and `stream-json` clients when a hook moves to the background, where it used to send nothing.
  - 🔴 Added `oldestDesktopSdk` to the `get_binary_version` reply, naming the oldest Agent SDK version (0.3.280 in this build) that a Claude Desktop may bundle and still run this CLI.
  - 🔴 Added `server_tool_use` to the usage that the SDK message schema carries for assistant messages.
  - 🔴 Added `asked_of_plugin` to SDK permission prompt metadata when the approval comes from an account plugin's MCP tool.
  - 🔴 Let SDK MCP manifest inputs use session writer statements up to 15 minutes old instead of 5 with `tengu_sdk_mcp_manifests` on by default, removing the `tengu_snug_glacier` switch.
  - 🔴 Added an `Invalid set_message_waiting: waiting must be true or false` reply when a headless host sends a non-boolean waiting value.
  - 🔴 Changed the headless `set_model` error path to settle a model change that already took effect before sending back the failure and the current model, if it can still read it.
  - 🔴 Added `refused_bearer_sha256` to requests for a replacement cloud session credential when a compatible SDK host handles refresh directly.
  - 🟢 Made `--await-initialize` exit with an error, as `--managed-settings-in-initialize` already did, when combined with `--bg`, `--background`, `--routine`, `--worktree --tmux` or a background session command such as `claude logs`; the reworded error says those paths never read the initialize request and points to `--managed-settings`, `--plugin-dir` or `--mcp-config`.
- Headless and SDK sessions
  - 🔴 Kept the full MCP startup wait for headless turns in Auto mode and in Plan mode when Auto is active underneath.
  - 🔴 Added a handoff validation error when an assistant message is missing `usage.server_tool_use`; a `null` value is accepted.
  - 🔴 Kept tool refusals attached to a resent headless prompt so they still apply when the prompt actually runs.
  - 🔴 Let a headless MCP update skip waiting for plugin setup when its caller already handled that wait.
  - 🔴 Changed remote headless sessions to handle MCP control requests alongside prompt processing by default, replacing the `tengu_limber_finch` switch.
  - 🔴 Removed the optional two-second delay that let redelivered prompts go ahead of MCP task notifications under `tengu_fizzy_galaxy`.
  - 🔴 Changed headless first-turn MCP waits to include extra servers only when their connection was marked for a named wait.
  - 🔴 Passed additional SDK host warnings through to the headless transcript once the session was ready to show them.
- 🔴 Saved a fresh transcript ID when a headless host asks to store the same prompt again, and restored the prompt's original ID on load if no other row uses it.
- 🔴 Let a cloud session's first-turn skill rescan still recognize the placeholder for a claude.ai skill that finished downloading, when `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_FOLD_FIRST_TURN_RESCAN` is set.
- 🔴 Put pinned plugins first, then organization-required plugins, when Claude Code looks through synced plugin folders on Anthropic-hosted workers and tool-container daemons.
- 🔴 Kept the size-limit error for an oversized `$.audio.play` download even if canceling the download fails; plugin hooks run while `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Checked the OAuth request URL a plugin's MCP server uses to sign in against the organization's connector block, rather than checking only the configured server URL, when `require_connector_block_on_plugin_servers` enforces the block.
- 🔴 Added the uploaded-project staging directory to the paths the self-hosted runner cleans up at session end with `--remove-session-state`.
- 🔴 Ran plugin `tool.call` hooks as soon as a subagent finishes producing a tool call's input, including when a `turn.step` hook wraps the reply, while `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Skipped the invalid-proxy warning when choosing a WebSocket connection to a loopback address, since Claude Code now rules out the proxy before reading the HTTP or HTTPS proxy setting.
- 🔴 Raised the minimum accepted catalog version from 4802 to 5078 when published model catalogs are in use and come from Anthropic or are checked with the built-in signing keys.
- 🔴 Left WebAssembly bytes out of plugin hook traces for `$.wasm.compile`, while keeping the file path when present and any denial reason; plugin hooks run while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Changed `next()` in a chain of hooks from the same plugin to start the next hook on the next microtask and wait for it to start before the chain returns, while the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Counted the spawning plugin when checking who is behind a plugin's `$.env.set` call in organization-managed sessions, under `tengu_steady_bittern`, which is on by default.
- 🔴 Raised the tool protocol version Claude Code advertises to attached computers from 30 to 31 for shell working-directory handling.
- 🔴 Let a plugin dependency resolve to the organization's required copy when it takes the place of the dependency's usual source, so Claude Code doesn't disable the dependent plugin just because the original source was replaced.
- 🔴 Carried source markers from content a mod read into later tool calls in the main conversation, so those calls carry the content's origin even when the read happened through the mod.
- 🔴 Changed web and connector isolation in remote sessions so the built-in memory and artifact tools can count as neither side when the service lists their genuine MCP servers in `tengu_groovy_dongarra`, which starts empty.
- 🔴 Left the default `Accept-Encoding` value out of MCP server comparisons when deciding whether to reuse a connection or match a server configuration.
- 🔴 Tightened the exception that lets file tools read a session's own stored files without asking: outside `--restricted`, it now requires a session ID that's a valid folder name.
- 🔴 Kept earlier Git-folder sandbox protections during a sandbox rebuild on Linux or WSL when the folder can't be inspected and the other checks can't rule out the earlier Git folder.
- 🔴 Added advice to the Bash sandbox warning for a suspected Git file it moved aside: if you inspect the file and it's empty, you can delete it.
- 🔴 Made the Bash sandbox's after-command Git link search skip the `worktrees` subtree when the session has explicitly allowed writes back inside the denied folder there, under `bot_protected_tengu_robust_island`, on by default.
- 🔴 Kept the link's own name on the Bash sandbox's write-deny list when its destination can't be checked safely, including links that point to another host, under `tengu_cozy_willow`, on by default.
- 🔴 Used the full plugin tool-permission check instead of the built-in security plugin's shortcut whenever a user-tier module is loaded, when `tengu_vast_wave` is on; it ships off.
- 🔴 Changed the reason shown when a cloud session can't bind to this machine through the new session-create endpoint: it now mentions session environment variables as well as the endpoint being off.
- 🔴 Refused outbound peer file staging when a file opens from a different path than the one the send check approved, when the caller supplies the checked path. (behind `tengu_send_file`, off by default)
- 🔴 Used the session's shell folder for strict forwarded approvals even when its name is hidden, so withholding the path from tool messages doesn't by itself stop the directory check from matching.
- 🔴 Carried the calling hook's origin into plugin `agent.offer` events for eligible local agent calls, so the offer goes through plugin dispatch as a call from that origin.
- 🔴 Used the authenticated account for the session's saved owner when it differs from the locally stored account, so a stale saved profile doesn't decide who owns later session records.
- 🔴 Left `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_NOTES_DIR` out of the environment saved in Bash shell snapshots, so a command gets its own session's notes folder when the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag, off by default, and the organization's data-flow policy allow one.
- 🔴 Changed unattended permission asks from a mod's own tool call to follow the plugin's own-call policy, still refusing when `bot_protected_tengu_polished_rivest`, on by default, requires the stricter check.
- 🔴 Used the rewritten tool input from a plugin's permission check if an approval prompt still had to be shown. (behind `bot_protected_tengu_polished_rivest`, on by default)
- 🔴 Kept sessions using API credentials out of automatic remote subagent execution when `tengu_neapolitan` is on, off by default, even if the session has used a remote environment before.
- 🔴 Limited agent hooks to the built-in `Read` tool and their result tool in sessions where tools may run on the host.
- 🔴 Used the organization's `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSIONEND_HOOKS_TIMEOUT_MS` value for SessionEnd hooks from plugins it requires when `CLAUDE_CODE_REQUIRED_PLUGINS_HOLD` is set on a hosted worker or tool-container daemon.
- 🔴 Kept the regular HTTP hook URL and environment-variable rules in force when a hook's source can't be verified, including hooks from required plugins let through by `CLAUDE_CODE_REQUIRED_PLUGINS_HOLD` on hosted workers and tool-container daemons.
- 🔴 Stopped matching goal-generated hooks and ordinary hooks as duplicates just because they share a command and source.
- 🔴 Kept the `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_NOTES_DIR` data-flow variable out of custom status-line and file-suggestion commands, even when `tengu_pewter_grebe` and organization policy let other hooks get it; the flag is off by default.
- 🔴 Made the merged-branch check for worktree cleanup require a local branch and refuse a base ref that could be read as a Git option.
- 🔴 Passed session ancestry and the child-notes directory into eligible `--worktree --tmux` launches when the values fit the launcher's character check and the notes directory is available under the organization's data-flow policy and `tengu_pewter_grebe`, off by default.
- 🔴 Cleared a plugin's saved reason for its hooks module being out of service when the module goes live, so a recovered module no longer shows an old failure; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Stopped carrying `tool.list` forward as the root event of later host calls a plugin makes, while keeping it as the plugin's own event; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Changed `$.env.set` in sessions that take settings from their organization to check whether a hook that rewrites a value is in the outermost plugin group rather than whether it's built in, when `tengu_steady_bittern` is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Refused plugin `$.env.set` changes to credential, provider, endpoint and proxy variables in sessions off the user's machine when a plugin that can change what the host sees makes or rewrites the call; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Included context supplied by a plugin hook that answered a tool call, merging it with the call's other context without repeating the same entries; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Tried to mark the project to keep later conversations out of training when saving a Gmail connector attachment and the project's config folder is known; this is skipped when managed web-search isolation applies and `tengu_doorbell_agave` is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Changed the saved full copy of a shortened MCP tool result sent through a separate tools host: the copy now includes the URL and title only when the tool manages its own output.
- 🔴 Applied file size and regular-file checks when collecting a plugin's MCP configuration for `plugin.register`, and reported read failures to the plugin inventory collector.
- 🔴 Made the organization connector block match IPv4-mapped and standard NAT64 IPv6 addresses against their underlying IPv4 addresses, and refuse address forms it can't judge.
- 🔴 Changed organization connector checks to recognize routing fields in `Forwarded` headers, distinguishing headers that name no host from values that couldn't be read.
- 🔴 Matched connector routing header names with punctuation treated as hyphens when applying an organization's connector rules, where earlier builds only treated underscores that way.
- 🔴 Varied the retry delays for claude.ai connector-list fetches so repeated attempts don't all land at the same time, and classified temporary network, DNS and proxy failures through the shared checks.
- 🔴 Used the alternate Claude in Chrome approval path for CLI-owned browser server configurations in remotely hosted Desktop sessions, after Claude Code's startup acceptance check.
- 🔴 Applied tool restrictions for plugin-written calls off the user's machine before running permission hooks and again if a hook allows the call with updated input; refused calls don't run.
- 🔴 Let a plugin hook's own host call use a `tool.check` allow in place of a permission prompt when the hook isn't serving a model call and the tool doesn't require its own approval; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Told plugin authors when `$.agent.call` can't run because the chosen agent hasn't loaded the tool: load it through that agent's tool search, then run the script again from the agent's next reply or later; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Checked whether a plugin call is still running under `tool.list` before invoking tool-list hooks again, so the hook can't run itself again even though child host calls no longer carry that event as their root; `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` is on by default.
- 🔴 Carried tool restrictions into subagents whose spawn was shaped by a plugin, so their later calls are checked off the user's machine too.
- 🔴 Checked for missing organization-managed hooks modules in `--safe-mode` too, keeping the organization's hook load marked as pending while any of its required modules haven't loaded.
- 🔴 Compared the script too when deciding whether a Workflow permission answer changed the input shown for approval, instead of setting it aside when the answer omitted the script, name and script path.
- 🔴 Applied tool restrictions to calls written or rewritten by plugin `turn.step` hooks in sessions off the user's machine, including remote sessions and non-WSL SSH; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Marked a background subagent's completion summary as its final report for desktop hosts when it contains actual agent text and no handback review is needed.
- 🔴 Made cloud-hosted sessions answer `set_message_waiting` and `prompt_draft_activity` with a named cloud-session rejection instead of an unknown-request error.
- 🔴 Cleared `CLAUDE_CODE_FIRST_CALL_THINKING_TITLES` and `CLAUDE_CODE_REQUIRED_PLUGINS_HOLD` from the inherited environment when preparing fresh background sessions, alongside the session IDs and notes folder used for data-flow records.
- 🔴 Used the new shared Unicode normalizer in Artifact permission checks so an unusually long run of combining marks in a field name won't stall the check.
- 🔴 Changed the remote-tool sandbox settings check to look for Git without repository-provided environment overrides when it checks whether local settings are untracked, with `tengu_synchronous_bunny` on by default.
- 🔴 Gave the remote environment picker a separate warning for a self-hosted default it won't use, naming the source and explaining that it reads this default only from user settings, managed settings or `--settings`.
- 🔴 Updated the file-input rejection message in debug logs to name macOS named forks alongside credentials and Claude Code's own files.
- 🔴 Made the address check for direct HTTP hook requests recognize all spellings of the IPv6 unspecified address as reserved.
- 🔴 Made `$.audio.play` report that a downloaded clip is over the byte limit even if canceling the response stream fails, when plugin hooks are enabled by `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, which is on by default.
- 🔴 Made `$.http.fetch` stop reading a plugin's response body if the request is canceled while waiting for another chunk; plugin hooks work while `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Distinguished missing object fields from fields set to `undefined` when matching a plugin's tool result to its input across multiple recorded runs, with the `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` flag on by default.
- Debug logs
  - 🟡 (2.1.327) Stopped writing MCP elicitation requests and hook answers in full to the MCP debug logs; they now record only the form or URL mode, the action, and up to 100 field names.
  - 🟡 (2.1.327) Changed print-mode MCP elicitation debug messages to log the mode, action and field names instead of the full request or hook answer.
- Saving permission rules
  - 🟢 Warned in the terminal when a tool approval's permission rule can't be saved to its settings file.
  - 🟢 Changed how permission rules from remote approval are filtered against the tool prompt's choices and current permission rules before saving.
- `claude plugin` commands
  - 🟢 Changed `claude plugin` status messages to use the same success, warning, and error formatting as other CLI commands, with plain text in redirected output where the command supplies a text fallback.
  - 🟢 Updated `claude plugin` help to describe redirected input or output in its confirmation instructions and to explain that `--keep-temp` keeps scaffold directories for debugging.
- HTTP hooks
  - 🟢 Exempted hooks that managed settings define, or that come from a plugin they enable from a local folder, from `allowedHttpHookUrls` and `httpHookAllowedEnvVars` lists in other settings files, unless managed settings set that list themselves.
  - 🟢 Fixed hooks whose owner Claude Code couldn't determine losing that uncertain mark when their timeout or command was adjusted, which made them count as the organization's own hooks, for example when choosing which settings an HTTP hook's URL allowlist comes from.
- `claude mcp` commands
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude mcp add` errors for a missing server name or command, missing XAA options, an invalid callback port, or using `--xaa` without `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_XAA`.
  - 🟢 Reworded `claude mcp add` warnings when a client secret couldn't be saved, HTTP or SSE options are passed to a stdio server, or a URL will be treated as a stdio command because `--transport` wasn't set.
  - 🟢 Updated `claude mcp xaa` error text for an invalid callback port, a missing `MCP_XAA_IDP_CLIENT_SECRET`, and failures saving settings, storing the secret or token, or signing in to the identity provider.
  - 🟢 Changed `claude mcp add` results on a terminal to use the shared markers for success, warnings, and errors.
  - 🟢 Clarified `claude mcp` help for `--verbose`, `--scope`, and `--no-browser`, and for `--port` on the remote HTTP server path.
- 🟢 Added an MCP tool error for a server that replies after its question about the call was declined: the reply is shown with a note that the decline still stands and that calling the action again repeats the request the question was about.
- OpenTelemetry exports
  - 🟢 Held back an organization's OpenTelemetry exports in non-interactive Anthropic cloud sessions started with `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_OWNED_SPAWN_ENV` until the first prompt is committed, keeping up to 20 batches and sending none if the session gets no prompt; the `tengu_sleepy_wicket` flag, off by default, ends the hold.
  - 🟢 Added `OTEL_RESOURCE_INCLUDE_HOST`, which adds the machine's `host.name` and `host.id` to OpenTelemetry resource attributes when telemetry is on, except in Anthropic-hosted cloud sessions and when an exporter is `console`.
  - 🟢 Added `OTEL_RESOURCE_INCLUDE_HOST`, off by default, to attach the machine name and ID to eligible OpenTelemetry exports; when `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES` is set, it supplies those attributes instead.
- 🟢 Blocked more Git operations that can change shared refs or worktree registration when a worktree-isolated subagent runs Bash, even if the command targets another repository, behind `tengu_level_meadow`, which is on by default.
- 🟢 Changed the startup error for organizations that require remote managed settings: when the fetch got a 403 from a network block in front of Anthropic's API, such as a Cloudflare block page, it now says the request was blocked before reaching Anthropic, so signing in again won't help, instead of suggesting `claude auth login`. First-party API only, outside `CLAUDE_CODE_REMOTE` sessions.
- 🟢 Added line and column numbers to the invalid-JSON error details for an MCP configuration file when its parser can find the error.
- 🟢 Fixed terminal text in mods with `wrap="truncate-end"` and the related `end`, `truncate` and `clip` styles: each line is shortened on its own, so a line break no longer hides later lines; plugin mods use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🟢 Applied the settings copy checks before restoring substitute values from remotely managed settings, so that fallback uses the same cleaned values as a normal settings load.
- 🟢 Fixed terminal mod `Text` with `wrap="truncate-end"` losing later lines when the text contains a newline; the terminal now clips each line separately, with `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules` on by default.

</details>

<details>
<summary>319 internal changes</summary>

- 🔴 Updated the settings fallback copy after an approved write, so a file that later becomes unreadable uses the last settings Claude Code saved rather than an older copy. If the saved file can't be matched to the settings source or parsed, the old copy is dropped.
- 🔴 Treated `sandbox-exec` like `env` or `nohup` in the Bash permission checker, as a command that runs another command.
- 🔴 Stopped the sandbox from turning `Edit` allow rules that start with `!` into write grants, and the Linux sandbox from creating a placeholder for a missing path allowed as an exception inside a denied folder.
- 🔴 Identified permission rules sent by `PermissionRequest` hooks as hook changes rather than user changes when passing them to the settings writer.
- 🔴 Stopped reporting a plugin-cancelled tool call as a permission denial or an agent abort.
- Auto mode
  - 🔴 Added handling for MCP tools in plan mode that the model entered from auto mode, treating such a tool as auto mode would, behind the new `tengu_mellow_gizmo` and `tengu_eventual_beacon` flags, both off by default.
  - 🔴 Showed auto mode's classifier the new plugin notes as prompts from their plugin, and the post a person answers in a claude.ai project as an assistant message before their reply.
  - 🔴 Turned on, for every session, the auto-mode arbiter check for the `mcp__workspace__bash` tool that the `tengu_parsed_creek` flag (off by default) used to gate, and removed the flag.
  - 🔴 Sent a PreToolUse hook's allow through auto mode's own checks by default in diskless sessions; elsewhere the `tengu_virtual_knuth` flag still decides, and it's off by default.
  - 🟡 (2.1.329) Changed auto mode's git status read for the server-side classifier to skip `git status` unless one of the last three replies was blocked by auto mode or followed a compaction, and then to wait up to 2 seconds instead of 300 ms; this needs the `tengu_rippling_waffle` flag (off by default) and the auto mode git status option, which is off by default.
- Auto mode when nobody is present
  - 🔴 Behind the `tengu_toasty_turtle` flag, off by default, made remote sessions in auto mode deny a tool call that would need a permission prompt when nobody's present, instead of prompting.
  - 🔴 Changed the explanation Claude gets when auto mode denies a permission ask because nobody is present in a cloud session: it now says the user hasn't written since something else opened the turn, that Claude can go on with work that needs no approval, and that the call can be made again after the user's next message; behind the `tengu_toasty_turtle` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Gave Claude a different denial in remote sessions in auto mode when a permission prompt can't be answered because the user isn't there, such as after something other than their message started the turn: Claude may make the call again after the user's next message, instead of every such call being denied for the rest of the session; behind the `tengu_toasty_turtle` flag, off by default.
  - 🔴 Ended the turn after a run of refused calls under `permissions.denyInsteadOfPrompting` in auto mode when Claude Code knows nobody is at the keyboard, and stopped auto mode's denial counts from resetting there, behind the `tengu_toasty_turtle` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed approval-required cross-computer calls in unattended auto mode to tell Claude why nobody can answer, and to stop the turn after repeated denials when `tengu_toasty_turtle` is on (off by default).
  - 🔴 Changed unattended auto mode to stop repeating the classifier for cross-computer calls once the session's denial history calls for stopping, under `tengu_toasty_turtle` (off by default).
- Typed-text marks for the auto mode classifier
  - 🔴 Marked messages queued while a turn runs, and text stamped as typed by the session's owner, as typed by the person, when the `typedMarks` setting in the `tengu_auto_mode_config` flag is on; it's off by default.
  - 🔴 Added support for marking verified user-typed Remote Control messages for the auto mode classifier when `tengu_auto_mode_config` enables both typed marks and remote typed marks, off by default.
- Tools this computer runs for a remote session
  - 🔴 Used the shared Unicode normalizer when checking whether remote shell commands mention protected settings, including the served path controlled by `tengu_violin_purfling`, which is on by default.
  - 🔴 Counted plugin `tool.run` hooks, as well as `tool.call` hooks, when tools this computer runs for a remote session check whether plugin hooks apply.
- 🔴 Kept the marker for content authored by others at the start of the message Claude gets when an action needs a permission prompt the session can't show.
- Plugin loading and `plugin.json`
  - 🔴 Refreshed the interactive session's plugin commands and agents after the organization's plugin load decisions arrive, when those lists loaded before the decisions were ready.
  - 🔴 Made prompt submission wait up to 10 seconds for a pending decision on which plugins can load before starting a model request.
- 🔴 Removed the code-rail fallback from claude.ai plugin sync in cloud sessions, which kept plugins listed through the rail installed when the account plugin list failed (reported as `rail_rows_kept`) and, behind the `tengu_level_plover` flag (off by default), synced every account plugin into sessions only the owner's account can write to.
- 🔴 Asked plugin modules' `tool.list` hooks about a tool a module reaches through its own `$.tool.call` when that tool isn't in the session's tool list, refusing the call as "no tool named …" when the hooks remove it; if the hooks don't answer, nothing is removed.
- Plugin mod API
  - 🔴 Made `tool.list` hooks track which tools were in their latest request, whether the hook is running, and when a previous deny rule was removed.
  - 🔴 Made tool handling recognize when the latest `tool.list` pass has finished even if it ended without an answer after the caller stopped waiting.
  - 🔴 Taught the static scan of a mod's source to read hook matcher values that come from a `for…of` loop over a literal array, and to keep regular expressions, plain values, lists and objects apart instead of flattening them to text.
- Plugin mod UI rendering
  - 🔴 Added an `AgentRow` UI render hook that lets plugin modules redraw or hide rows in the agent panel, given each row's details and any `subagentStatusLine` text.
  - 🔴 Extended the internal plugin `ui.render` engine with a `ContextMenu` component, an `AgentRow` component that falls back through earlier drawings, a one-time static draw, a shaded tree for `Backdrop`, and over-long props drawn as they were handed down.
  - 🔴 Let UI render hooks that rewrite a tool call's row inside a collapsed group also change whether the call is drawn as running.
  - 🔴 Stopped marking a plugin-drawn interface element as failed for its live copy when it only fails while drawing into already-printed output.
- Hooks
  - 🔴 Marked cross-computer tool results passed to hooks as ordinary output, separate from published files or output the tool manages itself.
  - 🔴 Marked a project hook listing for a remote session as `unreadable` also when a settings file was read but part of it couldn't be, not only when the file was missing with errors.
- 🔴 Changed how claude.ai skills sync (`CLAUDE_CODE_SYNC_SKILLS`) treats a stale skill folder that's still in use and belongs to a skill it's downloading: the folder is checked again after the downloads finish instead of being settled right away.
- 🔴 Changed the bundled claude-api skill's `ant` sign-in guidance, used only behind the `tengu_ancient_axolotl` flag (off by default), to read the `Credentials` block of `ant auth status`, split raw HTTP advice by credential row, and warn that Claude Code can switch to that sign-in without asking, sending the conversation to the Console organization and billing it.
- MCP connections and reconnects
  - 🔴 Changed MCP prompt slash commands to connect through the server's current entry, with its current scope and plugin source, when its connection settings haven't changed, instead of the entry captured when the prompt was listed.
  - 🔴 Marked MCP servers in claude.ai plugin listings as remote when the service gives them an origin.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud MCP proxy to track 403 replies and refusals without a recognized code as well as expired sign-ins during its handshake and tool listing.
  - 🔴 Stopped emitting the standard MCP connection event for Claude's own HTTP proxy addresses in diskless sessions.
- MCP server sign-in
  - 🔴 Skipped writing a saved MCP sign-in failure after the CLI starts shutting down.
  - 🔴 Kept the cached credential store contents when a reread fails while checking whether MCP servers have stored OAuth tokens, as writes to the store already did.
- 🔴 Changed MCPB bundle extraction for plugins to check the extracted files a second time whenever the first pass wrote any, rewriting files whose size doesn't match the bundle.
- Connector file references and uploads
  - 🔴 Added the rejected path's category and the reason for refusing it to file-reference upload diagnostics.
  - 🔴 Changed the Google Drive and Gmail connector file-reference hints to say the app fills in a file's content when the call runs instead of once the user approves it, and added the reason to file-reference refusals.
  - 🔴 Carried the file service's caller-uploaded text classification through fetched and prefetched attachments so the receiving session can tell those files from connector data.
  - 🔴 Limited the safety scan for mixed MCP upload arguments to 200,000 nested values and treated larger arguments as possible file requests.
  - 🔴 Logged the first MCP upload check in a process that gets a call without a message list, with different guidance depending on whether uploads are on.
- 🔴 Added a shortened deferred-tools list that folds an MCP server's tools into one row such as `mcp__claude_ai_Slack__* (42)` and explains `+server` searches, used only when the `tengu_slim_lantern` flag sets a size limit, which is 0 (off) by default.
- Cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Updated cloud session inventory to track changes in worker version even when its skills, plugins, tools and commands haven't changed.
  - 🔴 Added the last reported session cost to the data returned by cloud history prefetch.
- Taking back messages sent to cloud sessions
  - 🔴 Kept withdrawn, unread messages out of prefetched cloud history and sent that withdrawal state to the history reader.
  - 🔴 Moved taking back a message sent to a cloud session from the `tengu_lazy_creek` flag to `tengu_stateless_treasure` (both off by default); with it on, messages taken back before Claude read them are also left out when the session's events replay or its history loads.
- Attachments in cloud and Remote Control sessions
  - 🔴 Counted files from a resent prompt among the attachments that a cloud SDK client reports as omitted when a different client sent them.
  - 🔴 Handled @-mentioned files in prompts that arrive from a connector the way Claude Code does when `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_FILE_MENTIONS` is set, by default in diskless sessions; elsewhere the `tengu_pure_squid` flag still decides, and it's off by default.
  - 🔴 Changed attachment download reporting to distinguish transient download failures the service can still handle from other failures.
  - 🔴 Added early-JPEG counts and reasons for waiting on the original to cloud attachment reporting.
  - 🔴 Removed the old `model_copy_beside_flag_on` field from attachment reporting along with the flag it described.
- Cloud session uploads
  - 🔴 Lowered the size cap on event batches uploaded to cloud sessions from 10 MiB to 4,128,768 bytes, both when Claude Code sends a session's history and in the cloud worker's event uploader.
  - 🔴 Fixed size checks in cloud-session uploads that counted MCP tool results kept as compact JSON as nearly empty, so size limits on live updates now apply to them.
  - 🔴 Added a server-set `results_go_beside_running_calls` option to the cloud-session upload hold policy, which releases held tool results while other tool calls are still waiting for answers.
- 🔴 Stopped model requests made with a workload identity federation token while Claude Code is asking who owns the session, with the same session-owner error that requests on a claude.ai login already got.
- Cloud session workers
  - 🔴 Added keep-alives during quiet model calls in hosted remote sessions after a transcript upload succeeds, with up to eight in a row when `tengu_valiant_heron` sets the wait to 120–260 seconds; the default of 0 leaves this off.
  - 🔴 Sent empty turn metadata for idle remote workers at startup and when reporting subsequent turns.
  - 🔴 Added the current turn's start time to the metadata that cloud and Remote Control sessions report to claude.ai, leaving it out while the session is idle.
- Mounted file store cache limit
  - 🔴 Added a limit on the cache that staged files fill on a session's mounted file store in hosted sessions: a file that would push the cache in `/dev/shm` over the limit waits up to 90 seconds for room, then fails with `over_mount_cache_limit`; the `tengu_brimful_cistern` flag sets the limit, and it's 0 (no limit) by default.
  - 🔴 Held back non-forced cloud output downloads that would put a mounted cache over `tengu_brimful_cistern`'s size limit, checking every 3 seconds for up to 90 seconds before giving up; the limit ships off.
  - 🔴 Reported a mount-cache limit error if a cloud output file still won't fit after the wait, and left out the output path for an already-present file that would exceed the limit, set by `tengu_brimful_cistern` and off by default.
  - 🔴 Used a 500 MiB estimate for a cloud output file whose size couldn't be read when the `tengu_brimful_cistern` mount-cache limit is enabled; the limit ships off.
- Remote Control
  - 🔴 Applied the remote worker's UI-surface eligibility checks to host approval frames as well as other UI frames.
  - 🔴 Sent up to 128 characters of the host name when starting Remote Control with `tengu_foamy_treehouse` enabled, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added a machine ID tag to Remote Control session creation when `tengu_twinkling_crown` is enabled, off by default, and there's room within the 16-tag limit.
  - 🔴 Changed remote prompt delivery reporting to distinguish a prompt that was declined from a delivery attempt that failed.
  - 🔴 Added short reason labels for remote approval failures when files aren't attached or an approval changed the input or files.
- Stop from Remote Control and the web
  - 🔴 Kept a queued-message take-back from being recorded as a press of Stop when it interrupts the active turn.
  - 🔴 Changed headless Stop handling to recognize when clearing the queue also took out a saved interrupted turn.
  - 🔴 Added `stop_left_for_next_worker` to the headless worker's exit telemetry so it reports whether a stop still has rows on their way to the next worker.
- Remote session heartbeats
  - 🔴 Added diagnostics for remote heartbeats that recover after at least three unanswered requests, recording whether the event stream stayed alive or other requests got answers.
  - 🔴 Added DNS, TCP and HTTP diagnostics after two unanswered remote heartbeats, with five seconds per step and ten minutes between probes; the check requires `tengu_partitioned_platypus` to be off as shipped and stops at the December 20, 2026 cutoff.
  - 🔴 Changed the cloud-session and Remote Control event stream to count SSE comment lines as signs of life, and to ignore frames that carry no data, when deciding whether the stream has gone quiet.
  - 🔴 Switched the cloud event stream to the shared server-sent events reader, which ignores IDs containing a null byte and reads comments separately from data.
- Late remote approvals
  - 🔴 Added `same_tool_called_since` to approval reporting so it can tell whether Claude called the same tool between the newer message and the approval's place in the transcript.
  - 🔴 Dropped the `tengu_steady_wilkes` flag, off by default, from the 5-second wait for a cloud session's SDK MCP servers before a late permission answer is applied; the `tengu_splendid_lynx` flag, also off by default, now turns that wait on by itself.
- Resuming interrupted turns
  - 🔴 Saved the opening message of an interrupted headless turn alongside the turn being resumed.
  - 🔴 Added a signal to resumed-turn reporting when an API error happened after open tool calls.
- 🔴 Replaced the Teleport relay's error-body reader with the shared stream reader, keeping the 4 KiB and five-second limits and canceling the reader on exit.
- Claude Projects sessions
  - 🔴 Kept project chat messages with answered posts separate when batching headless prompts.
  - 🔴 Changed the automatic backgrounding check for foreground subagents to stop scanning queued prompts at a project post with an answer when `CLAUDE_AUTO_BACKGROUND_TASKS` is enabled.
  - 🔴 Checked attached project docs against PreToolUse hooks 20 at a time instead of all at once, treating a cancel partway through as a block.
  - 🔴 Changed projects relay turns that include an empty, cut-off row from a member so the person's words aren't forwarded to agents, matching how an empty row from the owner was handled.
  - 🔴 Carried the answered-post metadata on eligible project chat messages so the remote turn can use it instead of treating the message as plain input.
- Cowork memory
  - 🔴 Recorded prefetched Cowork memory as a `memory_read` call in the session's list of tools used.
  - 🔴 Added `memory_check_detail` to headless results for turns that perform the memory check, including any wait to deliver the memory.
- Artifacts
  - 🔴 Reworded three Artifact tool messages for Claude: the network error now says HTTPS access is needed "to read artifacts", the note for a session with live watching off no longer mentions a cached feature flag, and the nothing-to-retry note says "read it instead".
  - 🔴 Dropped the Artifact tool's name from the DesignSync tool's note that designs, decks and prototypes are made as Slides or Design Artifacts.
- Claude in Chrome
  - 🔴 Added Claude in Chrome diagnostics for whether a tab lookup was skipped or completed and whether the page had moved before the tool could act.
  - 🔴 Added a `chrome_built_in_browser_answer` value to `tengu_feature_ok` telemetry for Claude in Chrome's built-in browser answer, which only happens when the `tengu_cheerful_orbit` flag is on, as it isn't by default.
- Built-in `gh` and GitHub access
  - 🔴 Passed the kind of GitHub block (IP allowlist, suspended GitHub App, required SSO or identity-provider conditional access) along with its message from GitHub App checks.
  - 🟢 Told Claude, in the `gh` command description, when the session's GitHub proxy adds the session's GitHub sign-in only if it has one.
- Data-flow records and reports
  - 🔴 Added a notes folder that a session passes to the Claude Code sessions it starts, as `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_NOTES_DIR`, where each child leaves a note so the parent's data-flow record lists it, behind the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag, which is off by default, and only where the organization's policy allows data flow.
  - 🔴 Recorded an unknown data source when bridge session setup can't read or recognize the session's tags; the extra tag lookup on other bridged launches uses `tengu_pewter_grebe`, off by default.
  - 🔴 Extended the data flow records that sessions write into their transcripts to list the API responses the session received, by message id, and to keep records of tool calls whose messages were built but never sent; this applies only when the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag, off by default, is on and the organization's policy allows `allow_data_flow`.
  - 🔴 Retried a request without its data-flow report when it's refused as too large (HTTP 413), and left the report off while that request is retried; data-flow reporting sits behind the `tengu_pewter_grebe` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Recorded the data source as unknown for Remote Control messages from certain hosted launch paths.
  - 🔴 Added `previous_message_ids` and the responses each request lists to the data-flow record Claude Code attaches to API requests.
- 🔴 Added permission mode and time spent parked to the report sent when a parked session is claimed.
- 🔴 Changed cross-computer tool calls to carry the calling agent's usage-accounting ID.
- Subagents and forks
  - 🔴 Reworked how forked agents' copies of the parent conversation are cut and cached, marking a copy that lacks the conversation's start.
  - 🔴 Reworded the task-not-found errors to list background and named agents that are "running or waiting" instead of "running".
  - 🔴 Marked Claude Code as shutting down when its interface exits from inside the app, as graceful shutdowns already did, so background agents aren't resumed and the turn engine treats a cut-off turn as still owed while it closes.
- 🔴 Added a flag to permission requests forwarded by a teammate so the permission handler can tell those apart from direct requests.
- Agent view and background sessions
  - 🔴 Flushed pending data-flow records into the saved conversation before moving it to a background session.
  - 🔴 Added a `retire-if-empty` operation to the background service's control socket, checked against its control key.
- 🔴 Let check-ins about a silent background shell go out while the agent that owns it is waiting on its background work with the wait tool, instead of holding them while the main loop or other tasks are busy; these check-ins only run when the `tengu_silent_heron` flag sets an interval, and it's 0 (off) by default.
- 🔴 Made model-proposed goal tools available only in interactive local sessions that aren't background workers, with slash commands enabled, `modelProposedGoals` set to a value other than `disabled`, and `tengu_propose_goal` on; the flag's off by default.
- 🔴 Changed remote permission-mode switches to clear the note about how plan mode was entered.
- 🔴 Changed the REPL's debug log for a tool schema it can't print to log a cleaned tool name and the length of the error, rather than dumping the error text.
- 🔴 Counted Ultrareview launches refused because Ultrareview is off for the organization under their own reason instead of as failed cloud-session starts.
- Task tools
  - 🔴 Made `description` optional in the TaskCreate tool's instructions when the `tengu_composed_pillow` flag is set to `no_description` or `subject_only` (it's off by default); `subject_only` also tells Claude to leave out `activeForm`.
  - 🔴 Changed the hints Claude gets after calling `TaskCreate` with the wrong parameters to mention only `subject` when the `tengu_composed_pillow` flag, off by default, makes task descriptions optional.
  - 🔴 Added TaskCreate variants behind the `tengu_composed_pillow` flag, off by default, that make `description`, or both `description` and `activeForm`, optional, show "Adding task: <subject>" while adding, and refuse a blank subject.
- 🔴 Added `-q` to the Monitor tool's `inotifywait` example so its startup messages aren't delivered as events.
- 🔴 Remembered the renderer explicitly requested by `/tui` across the restart with `CLAUDE_CODE_TUI_ASKED`.
- Terminal rendering
  - 🔴 Removed the old scrolling-region renderer's special layout rules for terminal progress and dialogs; WezTerm still skips the progress bar.
  - 🔴 Made the renderer capture its final state when the terminal view closes after a confirmed exit, even before shutdown starts.
- Hidden and invisible characters
  - 🔴 Changed which combining marks and lookalike characters Claude Code escapes when it quotes a name in a message.
  - 🔴 Added an option to show hidden characters as visible markers in inverse video, control pictures such as ␍ for control characters and `<U+XXXX>` for invisible ones like a soft hyphen, switched on by `CLAUDE_CODE_SHOW_HIDDEN_CHARACTERS` or the `tengu_bubbly_gadget` flag, which is off by default.
- Queued messages
  - 🔴 Changed queued follow-ups to recognize a matching `tool.judge` plugin hook when deciding whether a pending tool approval can be held.
  - 🔴 Changed how queued input holds approvals under `tengu_wild_forest` (off by default) so MCP tools can be held too, and messages that already have an answer stop the search for a queued follow-up.
  - 🔴 Enabled send-now actions on the merged view of queued prompts, using the source that holds the selected message.
- 🔴 Used list navigation in slash-command lists that don't need approval or another input guard.
- 🔴 Kept the previous turn's file-edit results available when deciding whether to show a terminal tip.
- 🔴 Shared plugin-theme loading between the theme picker and plugin inventory so both apply the same file checks.
