- 🔴 Removed the once-per-second redraw from the subagent retry status row.
- 🔴 Cached syntax-highlighted diff lines per language, theme and plugin grammar version.
- Model choice and `/model`
  - 🔴 Stopped counting a model that came from a pick saved on the server as the person's explicit model choice when no model is set by flag or settings.
  - 🔴 Replaced the built-in price at the end of a server-listed model's description in the model picker with Claude Code's current price for that model, for first-party users.
  - 🔴 Added a second model check request when a model name check hasn't answered after 5 seconds; the first request's answer is used whenever it arrives first, and the second's only if it accepts the model, behind the `tengu_eager_thimble` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Removed the `tengu_peppy_sphinx` flag, off by default, that let any SDK session skip the server check on a model chosen before its first turn; only a pre-started spare session that hasn't run a turn skips that check now.
  - 🔴 Queued account default-model writes after the local choice saves, so rapid changes don't overlap, when `tengu_delegated_quail` puts the served catalog in primary mode; the build defaults it to off.
  - 🔴 Made successful account default-model writes update the live model selection even without a cached catalog, when `tengu_delegated_quail` puts the served catalog in primary mode; the build defaults it to off.
- 🔴 Fixed checks meant for the first turn after a compaction counting user messages that the compaction kept as new input.
- Rewind
  - 🔴 Changed Cowork rewind to mark a memory-file withdrawal for delivery again if the rewind would remove its notice.
  - 🔴 Changed rewind-undo reporting to identify why restoration wasn't safe, such as a new reply, more than one input message, a delivered poll event, or withdrawn memory.
  - 🔴 Changed `/rewind` to count tool calls from turns it removes from the conversation.
  - 🔴 Kept transcript rows from being added to the old checkpoint while a rewind needs to write a new one.
  - 🔴 Fixed rewind so removing a compaction marker still protects the kept prompt's tool catalog from a channel-tool layout change.
  - 🔴 Changed rewind checks to treat guarded rows as user folds and account for newer messages from project and resumed-turn inputs.
- Resuming sessions
  - 🔴 Recognized prompt-file and memory withdrawal records when reading saved turns for resume and cleanup.
  - 🔴 Added a checkpoint to the session file when a resumed session goes on past a message that was dropped while loading, so the next load follows the continued conversation; the same happens past a side branch behind the new `tengu_expressive_creek` flag, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Started the cost shown for a remote session you open in this terminal from its last recorded cost.
- Usage and cost accounting
  - 🔴 Priced each iteration of a multi-iteration API response separately when the model has long-prompt pricing and the iteration totals add up, so cost estimates apply the long-prompt rate only where an iteration reaches it.
  - 🔴 Cleared the model prices fetched from the server when you sign out or switch accounts, so the next account doesn't reuse them.
  - 🔴 Counted auto mode's classifier requests in the spending total of the agent or loop that made them, for accounts not on a Pro, Max or Team plan.
- Uploaded file paths in notes to Claude
  - 🔴 Added shortened paths for @-mentioned uploaded files, with a note that the user's message has the whole path, behind the new `tengu_trim_dunnock` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Shortened uploaded-file paths in notices that a message's files went missing or were moved, keeping the name's id prefix and extension around "…" and saying the paths are cut short; behind the new `tengu_trim_nuthatch` flag, which is on by default.
- CLAUDE.md files
  - 🔴 Added the full text of partly shown CLAUDE.md and rules files as a `claude_md` field in first-party API requests for the server's permission check, behind the `tengu_vivid_treehouse` byte limit, which is 0 (off) by default; if the server rejects that field or the request as too large, Claude Code resends without it, and until the next turn or compaction the check sees only the files' starts.
  - 🔴 Added the whole text of CLAUDE.md files that a headless session cut to their starts, up to 4,000,000 bytes, to first-party API requests checked by the server-side auto mode classifier, behind the `tengu_vivid_treehouse` flag (0, off by default) and `tengu_temporal_haven` (off by default); a 400 or 413 about it sends the request again without it.
  - 🔴 Added two more cases where headless sessions cut project CLAUDE.md files to their starts: after 2 quick context refills in a row, before auto-compaction's thrashing breaker would trip, and again later for files already cut that way; both need the `tengu_brisk_acorn` flag (0, off by default) and the `tengu_temporal_haven` flag (off by default).
- `/privacy-settings` and training consent
  - 🔴 Made privacy consent treat a changed session access token as unnamed until Claude Code identifies the account, with `tengu_generic_flask` off by default.
  - 🔴 Added diagnostics for a changed or unconfirmed sign-in at open, save, and close in the account-settings form of `/privacy-settings`; this applies when `tengu_playful_puzzle` and `tengu_generic_flask` are both off, as they are by default.
- 🔴 Changed the plugin package sharing check to log why it couldn't list a ZIP instead of reporting only that the listing failed, when `tengu_humming_squirrel` is on (on by default).
- 🔴 Reported native installer failures to download the app bundle as checksum or size failures instead of a generic download failure.
- 🔴 Updated the release notes bundled for the what's-new summary after an update: added 2.1.325 and dropped 2.1.318 and 2.1.319.
- 🔴 Reported `not_console_organization` separately from other Console sign-in refusals.
- Sign-in token refresh
  - 🔴 Asked the session's token source once more when the access token a refresh returned had been refused while that refresh was in flight, instead of using the refused token.
  - 🔴 Saved the identity details a new sign-in returns together with its tokens.
  - 🔴 Made the credentials lock for workload identity federation sign-ins fall back to the plain file lock when Claude Code's own lock handling (behind the `tengu_zany_book` flag, on by default) fails with an unexpected error, such as on a shared filesystem, instead of failing.
- Account switching and stored logins
  - 🔴 Saved the account and organization ids onto a stored claude.ai login that lacks them after the next profile fetch, behind the `tengu_bubbly_fountain` flag, which is off by default; a failed save is logged and tried again at the next start.
  - 🔴 Saved the account and organization IDs that come with a claude.ai sign-in in the stored login, and kept them across token refreshes.
  - 🔴 Added the `tengu_session_owner_ask_shown` event when the account-switch copy dialog opens, behind `tengu_bubbly_fountain` (off by default).
  - 🔴 Added a sign-in token observation callback on Claude's own MCP proxy and first-party API connector requests so the login check can tell when a request used that token.
  - 🔴 Cleared the cached organization name when the signed-in account's organization changes, so the previous organization's name isn't kept with the new account.
  - 🔴 Cleared remembered uncoded cloud connector refusals when the account changes and MCP connection caching is active.
- 🔴 Reset cached provider state, including context-window refusals and learned output-token caps, when `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS` or `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_LEGACY_MODEL_REMAP` changes during a session, as already happened for the other provider variables.
- Proxies
  - 🔴 Stopped installing a process-wide undici dispatcher for the proxy in `HTTPS_PROXY` or `ALL_PROXY`; when a proxy is first configured, Claude Code now resets its own cached connection agents instead.
  - 🔴 Stopped setting a global `undici` proxy dispatcher, built from the proxy URL, `NO_PROXY` and any client certificate or custom CA, when an HTTP or HTTPS proxy is configured.
- API request retries
  - 🔴 Counted `UND_ERR_SOCKET` as a closed connection when deciding to retry an HTTP request once after its reused keep-alive connection fails.
  - 🔴 Changed `Retry-After` handling to accept only plain seconds or a standard HTTP date of up to 100 characters, so other values, such as hex numbers or loosely formatted dates, no longer set a retry delay.
  - 🔴 Made uploads of a local session's history to a cloud session honor a `Retry-After` HTTP date on a 429, not only a number of seconds.
  - 🔴 Honored date-form `Retry-After` headers when the file-input upload service says it's busy.
- Retry delays and jitter
  - 🔴 Changed when a remote connection is reported as having started to reconnect, giving the randomized delay another quarter second before counting the attempt as late.
  - 🔴 Changed retry delays that don't pick their own jitter to vary randomly by up to 25% either way instead of following a fixed doubling schedule; this covers MCP server reconnection attempts, retries after a transient error on an MCP server's first connect, remote session log persistence retries and the SSE transport's event POST retries.
  - 🔴 Added up to 10% random jitter to the polling intervals for policy limits and managed settings.
- 🔴 Changed the resend of an empty or thinking-only reply to also follow turns started by auto-continuation, host-made or peer messages, behind the `tengu_calm_waffle` flag, which is off by default.
- Settings files
  - 🔴 Updated local settings' last-read record after cloud home sync writes settings, so later settings edits use the copy just written.
  - 🔴 Ran the check for whether `.claude/settings.local.json` is tracked by git with the person's own environment variables restored in place of the values Claude Code had set for itself.
- 🔴 Reloaded policy settings after a remote managed settings check that changed nothing whenever the previous settings load had failed.
- 🔴 Extended the internal desktop-host protocol in the SDK schema with a plugin's `requiredByOrganization` mark, `keeps_request_id` for a permission card kept open behind a message, memory-check timing and details, hook origin and display names, `summary_is_report`, `refused_bearer_sha256`, a PreToolUse `whenUnanswered` fallback, typing and held-message notices, a page `limit` for cloud session transcripts, shader backdrops with `ui.pointer` clicks, and the `surface` and `client_id` of posted messages.
- Headless and SDK sessions
  - 🔴 Fixed headless control-response reporting to record a reply as unclaimed if the attempt to apply an earlier permission answer says it couldn't use it.
  - 🔴 Routed deferred session-stop requests through the session service once the SDK runtime has started.
  - 🔴 Changed headless cache warming to use the intended model's effort and memory layout when checking a resumed prompt.
  - 🔴 Fixed a headless turn using a custom prompt built for a different model so it can keep the system prompt already in the conversation.
  - 🔴 Stopped the output-stall timer when a headless remote runner disconnects, so closing the connection doesn't produce a stall report later.
  - 🔴 Counted remote prompts that end a wait as pending even when they bring no attachments.
- Session history and transcripts
  - 🔴 Recognized more unfinished tool responses as synthetic when examining session history, including tools skipped before running and approvals that were still waiting when a new message arrived.
  - 🔴 Skipped replaying the synthetic result that says a tool approval is still waiting when a remote worker catches up on earlier turns.
  - 🔴 Changed resuming or branching a session to keep tool usage that hasn't been reported yet.
  - 🔴 Kept a file that a resumed session had edited in its list of read files, as a partial view with its modification time, when the file is now too large to reload or briefly unreadable, instead of dropping it.
  - 🔴 Added transcript notes telling the model when a plugin cancelled the tool call it made and when the system running the conversation stopped before the turn finished, instead of reporting those as ordinary interruptions.
  - 🔴 Stopped counting assistant messages that hold only placeholder text, or the earlier parts of a split assistant message, as Claude's last reply when Claude Code collects what was sent since then.
  - 🔴 Changed internal conversation bookkeeping: `/clear` starts the new conversation's record of tools used without a link back to the cleared one, keeping only tool responses not yet reported, and forgets which plugin mods' markers were read; auto-mode setup records its model call as an event from an unknown source.
  - 🔴 Changed the interruption marker Claude sees after the system running a conversation stopped mid-turn: instead of "[Request interrupted by user]" it says nobody asked to stop the turn, and an unfinished tool call says its outcome is unknown; behind the `tengu_precious_brook` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Recorded the tool calls in messages dropped by a rewind or compaction in the conversation's tools-used record, and marked sessions started from an unknown inbound origin there, with a telemetry event for the latter.
  - 🔴 Bridged gaps in a cloud session's stored transcript when loading it, linking messages past missing ones; behind the `tengu_vectorized_aho` flag, off by default.
  - 🔴 Removed the `tengu_plumb_marten` flag check, so diskless sessions always leave out the source-message link on the error results Claude Code adds for interrupted tool calls.
  - 🔴 Continued the API thread after an aborted reply with no answer, replacing its unanswered tail, instead of starting a new thread when the next request picks up from the same message, behind the `tengu_tidy_crab` flag, which is on by default.
  - 🔴 Let a remote prompt replay mark its prompt row as newly saved when the earlier copy is on the other side of compaction, under `tengu_wobbly_journal` (off by default).
- 🔴 Added a system prompt note telling Claude that values and files from websites, APIs and downloads are untrusted and must be checked in code against the expected format before they reach a command or tool, behind the `tengu_reactive_heron` flag, which is off by default.
- Lean rewrite prompts
  - 🔴 Removed the longer re-read note from the Read tool's description for models that used the lean prompt rewrite; they now get the same short "Do NOT re-read a file you just edited" line as other models.
  - 🔴 Removed the lean rewrite variant of the system prompt and its turn-update texts, which a model's capabilities or the `tengu_temporal_hare` flag could turn on, and the system-prompt line telling Claude the user pays per token, which sat behind the `tengu_wobbly_dahl` flag.
- 🔴 Opened the Grep tool's short description, used for models that get lean prompts, with one line telling Claude to prefer it over `grep` or `rg` in Bash, replacing a version with no opening line and a longer alternate wording.
- 🔴 Added Bash guidance telling Claude to read a picture a foreground command writes right after the command in the same message, behind the new `tengu_smooth_giraffe` flag, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Added a Bash tool parameter in diskless sessions for absolute paths of images a command will write, returning those pictures with the result, behind the new `tengu_goofy_sunbeam` flag, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Stopped the comparison of two edits to the same path from throwing when the path isn't a regular file.
- Performance and resource use
  - 🔴 Cached the color functions the terminal renderer builds for theme colors, up to 1,024 each for text and background, clearing them when the terminal's color level changes.
  - 🔴 Added an option for diskless sessions to put off prompt-cache break tracking and message hashing until after a model request is sent, behind the `tengu_mellow_quill` and `tengu_quirky_tide` flags, both off by default.
  - 🟢 Slowed the local event-loop stall check from every 200 milliseconds to once a second after 30 quiet seconds, and restored the faster check on terminal input or work, with `tengu_rusty_orchard` on by default.
- Telemetry
  - 🔴 Added a 500 MiB size bucket to attachment-upload telemetry so uploads above 400 MiB can be split at 500 MiB.
  - 🔴 Split the telemetry for a plugin manifest that fails its schema check by the failing field, and added an event for ignored display fields.
  - 🔴 Added `tengu_added_folder_trust_answered` telemetry recording whether an added folder was trusted and whether the prompt came at startup.
  - 🔴 Batched the user part of the active-time metric into whole seconds, sent once a second builds up, after 5 seconds without input, or when Claude starts working.
  - 🔴 Reported in telemetry which kind of plugin load error caused the MCP server to refuse a plugin call.
  - 🔴 Reported changed and unconfirmed sign-ins separately in connector file-send telemetry, including whether they happened during transfer or while finishing a connector call.
  - 🔴 Added counters for MCP tool relists and recovery after rate limits to the next-turn reconnect report. (behind `tengu_splendid_biscuit`, off by default)
  - 🔴 Added `tengu_upload_ahead`, `tengu_typing_spell` and `tengu_slow_prompt_hook` to the events sent to Datadog.
  - 🔴 Added a sampled `tengu_slow_prompt_hook` event for slow UserPromptSubmit hooks, with the hook's duration, type and shell and whether it was aborted or left its output open.
  - 🔴 Stripped the `bash_places` field from event metadata where Claude Code already strips other detailed fields.
  - 🔴 Added `bash_places` and `bash_argv0` to Bash tool-use telemetry, and stopped logging tool result sizes for the tools of the built-in `claude_ai` MCP server.
  - 🔴 Added a `searches_whole_disk` kind to Bash command telemetry for commands such as `find /`, `grep -r … /` or `rg … /` that search from the root without a depth limit.
  - 🔴 Added `clears_tool_inputs`, `clear_tool_inputs_count` and `reason` fields to the telemetry for clearing tool results after an idle.
  - 🔴 Added `headersMs`, `thinking_titles_asked` and `first_title_ms` fields to API success telemetry.
  - 🔴 Added a `permission_check_crash` diagnostic event, sent once per tool call, naming the kind of failure, the permission mode and the tool when a tool's permission check throws.
  - 🔴 Added `plugin_host_required_mark` telemetry recording whether the host's marks for required plugins matched a loaded plugin.
  - 🔴 Dropped the always-false `prev_was_steered` field from Workflow relaunch telemetry.
- Debug logs
  - 🔴 Cleaned and shortened the prompt preview in in-process Agent Teams debug logs before writing it.
  - 🔴 Changed remote debug logging to skip stream-event frames.
  - 🔴 Redacted the response body from MCP "Received a response for an unknown message ID" errors in the MCP server log.
- Diagnostics file
  - 🔴 Changed remote stream gap diagnostics to use the event parser's partial-frame byte counts when `CLAUDE_CODE_DIAGNOSTICS_FILE` is set.
  - 🔴 Added a diagnostic for Anthropic-hosted cloud sessions with `CLAUDE_CODE_DIAGNOSTICS_FILE` set that logs up to 16 `mcp_inbound_index_keys` warnings when large MCP HTTP messages carry many numeric-looking JSON keys; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MCP_INDEX_ROOM_COUNT` turns it off.
- 🔴 Added old `~/.claude/child-logs` files and `claude-paste-` temporary files to the cleanup that follows `cleanupPeriodDays`.
- 🔴 Tightened how Android project detection reads shell commands, so a Gradle build variant has to appear in the same command; detection still requires an edit to a Kotlin or Java file.
- 🔴 Narrowed the `x-claude-1p-request-class` header on first-party API requests: subagent requests are marked `background-task` only for workflow subagents and listed sources, and are otherwise `blocking-subagent`, while auxiliary requests outside a listed set no longer claim `summary-extraction`.
- 🔴 Updated the feature-flag client's internal tables of flag names, such as the flags never served from the saved flag cache, adding this release's new flags and dropping retired ones.
- 🔴 Cleared cached feature-gate decisions after first-run onboarding fetches fresh feature flags, so later checks use the new values.
- 🔴 Carried the source post that a verified Slack message answered into the turn's metadata.
- 🔴 Added a fetch count, capped at nine, to per-turn memory context diagnostics, resetting it after each report.
- 🔴 Labeled canceled policy-limit requests as `ABORTED` in fetch diagnostics when the HTTP client reports a cancellation or abort.
- 🔴 Made sampled SDK output validation reports name the most specific field the validator can find when a message doesn't match any allowed shape.
- 🔴 Added tracking for file-use markers seen by plugin hooks that transform conversation or tool data, so the main session's tool calls can report files a hook read.
- 🔴 Distinguished escape decoding for deferred tools defined only in an API message from escape decoding for tools already in the session's list, in hosted tool-input diagnostics.
- 🔴 Added more detail to the diagnostics sent when tool-result clearing after an idle period stops, including whether the last reply carried context-management data and how many tools were left out.
- 🔴 Skipped the tool-result-clearing check when there hasn't been a long enough idle gap since the latest reply, on the reminder path behind `tengu_humble_tide`, which is off by default.
- 🔴 Replaced raw tool names in ToolSearch debug messages with names safe to log, such as a generic label for MCP tools, when it reports already-loaded tools or partial selections.
- 🔴 Reused serialized input-schema sizes across requests when measuring how many bytes the tool list adds to an API request, so the diagnostics don't have to serialize those schemas again.
- 🔴 Set aside 64 extra rows when the terminal renderer grows an existing screen buffer, so it can reuse the allocation as more lines arrive.
- 🔴 Reused already painted cells for recurring unclipped text in the terminal renderer, so it can skip laying out the same segment again.
- 🔴 Classified more socket failures, including a down network or a closed socket, as connection errors rather than generic network errors when checking an OAuth token.
- 🔴 Explained in debug logs why file edits ask for approval when Claude Code can't read a folder under `dev-mods` or follow one of its links, including when it leads to a network location.
- 🔴 Added the spawning plugin and depth to hook origin text in diagnostics for a plugin's own tool call when `tengu_plain_kestrel` is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Kept synced skill placeholders available for the first cloud turn's command rescan after the skills finish downloading, when `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_FOLD_FIRST_TURN_RESCAN` is set.
- 🔴 Added another way for cloud sessions to ask the service for a message sender's address: early lookups use it under `tengu_swift_plover`, and replacement lookups under `tengu_woven_ladle`, both off by default; lookups can also carry a session ticket.
- 🔴 Skipped the short wait after starting an early cloud sender-address lookup when it uses the new request route under `tengu_swift_plover`, which ships off.
- 🔴 Gave the built-in Claude Code Remote MCP server a fixed identity in data-flow reports when `tengu_pewter_grebe` is on and organization policy allows reporting; the flag ships off.
- 🔴 Changed which source an MCP tool can report in data-flow records, including accepting the Claude Code Remote identity from the VS Code entry point and keeping a client-configured identity when the memory server supplies one; reporting needs `tengu_pewter_grebe` and organization permission, and the flag ships off.
- 🔴 Marked data-flow reports as incomplete when a model response has no usable message ID, when `tengu_pewter_grebe` is on and organization policy allows reporting; the flag ships off.
- 🔴 Counted connector files that arrive in a silent prompt resend in the session's data-flow reports, when `tengu_pewter_grebe` is on and organization policy allows reporting; the flag ships off.
- 🔴 Added `hooks_init_without_silent_ms` to startup timing reports in remote sessions or with `CLAUDE_CODE_EMIT_STARTUP_TIMING` on, where hook-origin reporting is allowed; it separates startup hook time from work that can run silently.
- 🔴 Reworded the sandbox warning for a suspected planted Git file: it says an existing file can't be distinguished from a planted one and that after-command cleanup will move it aside.
- 🔴 Dropped the flag-name field from the warning logged when a Cowork memory window flag read fails, on the Cowork memory path that uses `tengu_misty_anchor`.
- 🔴 Grouped more TLS failures under `CERT_ERROR` in API network diagnostics, including revoked certificates, overlong chains and violations of certificate name restrictions.
- 🔴 Suppressed long MCP tool-name warnings and their `tengu_mcp_degraded` events during lookup-only checks used for plugin tool calls; tools with names the API can't accept are still left out.
- 🔴 Passed along why an API request wasn't saved for transcript replay, such as a request shape that's too large or a stale hash, when replay recording is active.
- 🔴 Kept track of which data-flow entries were already written when `/cd` successfully moves an on-disk transcript to a new project folder, when `tengu_pewter_grebe` is on and the organization allows data-flow reporting; the flag ships off.
- 🔴 Kept temporary entries for synced skills that finish downloading during a cloud session's first-turn rescan, so the refreshed command list can recognize the completed downloads without listing the skill twice; this needs `CLAUDE_CODE_CCR_FOLD_FIRST_TURN_RESCAN`.
- 🔴 Added more reason labels to permission telemetry, distinguishing an unenforceable rule in a permission answer, a reserved input key, a problem with personal hooks, and a tool removed by a plugin from other refusals when permission telemetry is collected.
- 🔴 Counted exits Claude Code had already committed to as graceful in the last-session statistics, including in the next session's shutdown telemetry when it's sent.
- 🔴 Checked which other on-disk sessions wrote to the workspace before the current session records its own data flow, when `tengu_pewter_grebe` is on, off by default, and organization policy allows data-flow tracking.
- 🔴 Skipped separate reads for missing `CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md` and `.claude/rules` paths when scanning nested project instructions on disk, if the `tengu_modular_reddy` flag is on, as it is by default.
- 🔴 Made local startup's `tengu_context_size` telemetry count tools with the session's own permission settings when they're available, falling back to the earlier default context otherwise.
- 🔴 Counted Bash searches of the whole disk as waiting work in the turn classifier, alongside file reads and narrower searches.
- 🔴 Logged six more named reasons when cloud session creation fails, covering a disconnected GitHub App, a disabled GitHub connector or managed environment, a stale sign-in, organization-disabled Ultra Review, and disallowed session environment variables.
- 🔴 Made the git-bundle upload path check the current working directory even when the caller supplied a directory.
- 🔴 Cut down the skill list sent to Claude after a rapid-refill compaction or when the previous list was shortened, if the `tengu_slim_lantern` flag sets a positive character budget; it defaults to zero, meaning this extra limit is off.
- 🔴 Carried markers for content a plugin read into the agent's own later model requests, so Claude still gets warnings about where that content came from.
- 🔴 Discarded a saved compaction summary before trying to reuse it when its coverage no longer matches the current messages.
- 🔴 Added context to the logged error when Claude Code can't save the ID of an API reply, saying the reply's ID wasn't noted.
- 🔴 Stopped treating an HTTP 400 message that mentions only `previous_message_ids` as an API thread error, so that text alone no longer triggers thread recovery.
- 🔴 Stopped recording a tool permission answer when the request was cancelled before the answer could be recorded.
- 🔴 Counted an MCP resource mention as use of its named server as soon as Claude Code reads the prompt, including prompts that were queued.
- 🔴 Told Claude which MCP servers could retry after authentication and when ToolSearch might not be available while deferred servers reconnect.
- 🔴 Stopped counting an unavailable MCP tool as reconnecting in cloud telemetry when reconnecting wasn't enabled for it.
- 🔴 Left the inline-tools API beta off prompt-hook model requests when tools can run on the host.
- 🔴 Made shared network address checks recognize more spellings of reserved IPv6 addresses by classifying the parsed address rather than its text.
- 🔴 Skipped dispatching some tool, file, process and prompt hooks to the built-in `cc-plugin-sec-default` mod when it can do the same checks in the host and there's no user mod or reload to account for; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_LONE_SEC_DEFAULT_SHORTCUT` turns off the shortcut.
- 🔴 Recorded a recognized permission reason's label in tool permission telemetry where those decisions were all reported as `other`; unrecognized reasons still count as `other`.
- 🔴 Counted permission waits for plugin-intercepted tool calls while the hooks path holds an approval open, so clients listening for tools that are waiting get the same start and end signals as for an ordinary dialog; plugin hooks use `tengu_plugin_hooks_modules`, on by default.
- 🔴 Included one more host name and known connection hops in diagnostics for MCP connector requests checked against organization rules.
- 🔴 Used the shared proxy-refusal check when fetching the claude.ai connector list, so related proxy errors are handled consistently when deciding whether an attempt can be retried.
- 🔴 Kept known organization-blocked connectors ahead of older rows when refreshing a partial claude.ai connector list, while keeping inferred blocks separate from the rows used to judge endpoints.
- 🔴 Used the current tool search definitions and the tools being held back when deciding whether Claude called a tool before loading it; this affects the explanation given for a tool call Claude can't make yet.
- 🔴 Left the built-in `cc-plugin-sec-default` mod's `prompt.submit` event out of the hooks worker's published module summaries.
- 🔴 Counted whole-disk searches alongside ordinary reads and searches when grouping shell activity for background-task reports.
- 🔴 Rendered PDF references under recognized project or skill folders in the user attachment group when building Claude's conversation, like other files in those folders.
- 🔴 Used the IDs of refused tool calls to find their earlier results first when removing rejected content from a later model request, rather than looking only for the nearest matching message.
- 🔴 Measured how long it takes to paint the first terminal frame after a keypress or paste, starting from the input event.
- 🔴 Recognized Claude Code's own Artifact page wrapper with up to three inline scripts in its head, up from two.
- 🔴 Changed the subagent progress-summary debug log to show the generated summary's character count instead of its text when progress summaries are enabled.
- 🔴 Counted file-upload screening refusals under the existing read-block and restricted-mode telemetry reasons, and counted rejected Chrome uploads using macOS named forks under the unusual-path reason.
- 🔴 Let Claude Projects check attached document hashes after a transcript marker that didn't actually compact the conversation, with `tengu_keen_dunlin` and `tengu_brisk_pebble` on, both off by default.
- 🔴 Changed startup MCP telemetry to count single-label hosts and more reserved addresses as private, using the shared address checks for loopback and unspecified addresses.
- 🔴 Kept counting organization-approved plugin hooks in `hook_registered` telemetry when ordinary hooks are turned off, including organization-required plugins on eligible hosts.
- 🔴 Counted a background session that couldn't reopen because its gateway was lost as `gateway_lost` in `fleet_view_open` telemetry rather than as a general restart failure.
- 🔴 Gave offscreen terminal renders their own input stream, so drawing text for uses such as plan approval doesn't read from the session's terminal input.
- 🟢 Skipped measuring flex children a second time when the terminal layout engine only needs the row's total size and already has the cross-axis size.
- 🟢 Kept the terminal's new paint cache from reusing a drawing after its node moves under a different parent or is hidden; the new renderer uses `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or `tengu_idempotent_willow`, off by default.
- 🟢 Reused parts of the terminal's saved drawing when only a child changes, while rebuilding a node's own drawing when its properties change; the new renderer uses `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or `tengu_idempotent_willow`, off by default.
- 🟢 Reused terminal rows that were already laid out across redraws and capped the memory used for the saved rows.
- 🟢 Cleared saved text layout when a terminal node is laid out again, so the new renderer doesn't reuse a row laid out at its old size; it uses `CLAUDE_CODE_NEW_RENDERER` or `tengu_idempotent_willow`, off by default.

</details>

<details>
<summary>49 changes with no effect in this build</summary>

- 🔴 Added a filter that ignores custom commands and skills named after a bundled skill marked as one that can't be shadowed, logging which were ignored; no bundled skill in this build carries that mark.
- 🔴 Added a view-skills callback to the local MCP server menu, but the menu never calls it in this build.
- 🔴 Added a cloud-session ping request with a 30-second timeout and a retry after an eligible sign-in refresh; this build has no caller for it.
- 🔴 Added a refusal for late permission answers when a tool's MCP server can't be confirmed as the one the request was made for ("The call was not run: it could not be confirmed that … still reaches the server …"); nothing in this build supplies that check, so it never fires.
- 🔴 Added handling for a `memory_ask_started` event in cloud-session event streams; nothing in this build passes a handler for it, so it only logs that the event arrived.
- 🔴 Added per-session counters of Claude in Chrome tab reads, skipped reads and site changes; nothing in this build reads or sends them.
- Self-hosted runners
  - 🔴 Added tool-server token renewal and its log lines to `claude self-hosted-runner`, in code only staff builds reach; this build still refuses a session that needs a tool server.
  - 🔴 Changed the git config that `claude self-hosted-runner` prepares to say `filemode = false` on Windows; this Linux build keeps `true`.
- Hybrid organizations
  - 🔴 Added a way for the tool runner to skip queued tool calls with `[Tool call skipped: the turn paused before this call ran…]` when a session move asks it to give way; nothing in this build makes that request.
  - 🔴 Added a "hybrid organization" stop for Remote Control sessions that skips archiving the session; nothing in this build turns it on.
  - 🔴 Stopped terminal sessions using a hybrid organization from moving to the background.
  - 🔴 Disconnected Remote Control when a change to hybrid organization policy prevents the session from continuing there.
  - 🔴 Refused cloud session forks for hybrid organizations, using the same fork check that handles disabled conversation history.
  - 🔴 Changed Remote Control startup to stop the bridge and show the organization policy reason when it detects a hybrid organization before startup finishes.
- Workflows
  - 🔴 Removed the dormant workflow v2 engine from the Workflow tool: a shared fact store that scripts could use through `put`, `read`, `on`, `retract`, `agent`, `v` and `atleast`, and child workflows built on it; the tool's `runId` path that led to it was already switched off.
  - 🔴 Removed the Workflow tool's unused "v2" engine: fact rows with rules, a per-run row cap that killed runaway runs, and a `world.jsonl` journal replayed on resume; in 2.1.325 its only entry point was a stub that always returned nothing, so no session could reach it.
- 🔴 Added ancestry checks for rewinding behind compaction to an earlier session's prompt; the only caller leaves that path disabled in this build.
- 🔴 Reworded stats screenshot clipboard errors on macOS and Windows, and clarified the message for other platforms that don’t support copying a screenshot.
- 🔴 Added organization checks that would keep some past transcripts out of `/feedback` reports and session-analysis features; the checks always pass in this build.
- Sign-in screens and messages
  - 🔴 Added a `hybrid_gateway` reason for refusing a stored sign-in, with the message that your organization's gateway doesn't accept your own Anthropic sign-in; the gateway check always says no in this build, so neither appears.
  - 🔴 Added a refusal for Anthropic Console profile sign-in when the session's requests go to the organization's gateway, but this build's gateway check always answers no, so the refusal can't appear.
- Bundled Agent SDK
  - 🔴 Added a once-per-process warning, `CLAUDE_SDK_MCP_SERVERS_ARGV_DEPRECATED`, for apps that launch their own local Claude Code with MCP servers configured but leave `mcpServersDelivery` unset, because command-line delivery can expose server headers and environment variables to other users of the machine.
  - 🔴 Added `mcpServersDelivery` to local SDK launches so apps can choose `initialize` to send MCP server settings over stdin or `argv` to pass them on the command line.
  - 🔴 Rejected SDK calls that set `mcpServersDelivery` to anything other than `argv` or `initialize`, with an error naming the two accepted values.
  - 🔴 Added an `mcpServersDelivery` option to the Agent SDK bundled for scheduled tasks: with `'initialize'` it sends MCP servers in the initialize request instead of on the command line, ends the session if that Claude Code is too old to read them, and warns with `CLAUDE_SDK_MCP_SERVERS_NOT_APPLIED` if they weren't loaded; scheduled tasks pass no MCP servers, so nothing uses it.
  - 🔴 Prefixed the request IDs the bundled Agent SDK forwards to in-process MCP servers, mapping replies and cancellations back; scheduled tasks, its only user here, register no such servers.
- Bash tool instructions
  - 🔴 Added a note to the Bash tool's instructions, when the host sets a wait for background commands after a reply, telling Claude how many minutes the session waits for a background command to end and to tell the user what's still running.
  - 🔴 Changed the Monitor tool's guidance in diskless sessions that wait for background work after a reply: it says a monitor goes on after the reply, that only a few can start between the user's messages, and that a server restart ends one without notice, instead of warning that a watch may stop within a minute of the reply.
- Windows sandbox MXC backend
  - 🔴 Added an experimental MXC backend for the Windows sandbox, picked with `sandbox.windows.backend: "mxc"` in user, managed or `--settings` settings: sandboxed commands run in a Microsoft eXecution Container through the MXC SDK in Claude Code's runtime, with nothing to install, and Bash commands are refused so only the PowerShell tool runs them.
  - 🔴 Kept a Windows session on the sandbox backend it started with when `sandbox.windows.backend` changes or `"mxc"` gets set aside mid-session, with a notice that the change applies at the next start of Claude Code.
  - 🔴 Added explanations when a command doesn't start on the Windows MXC sandbox backend, such as a path in the request that doesn't exist (usually a deny entry under a missing folder such as `.claude`), telling whoever manages the sandbox settings to set `sandbox.windows.backend` to `"sandboxuser"`, or remove it, to use the sandbox account backend from the next start.
  - 🔴 Changed the Windows sandbox's MXC backend (`sandbox.windows.backend`) to run commands through MXC's own process handle, killing and releasing them through it, and gave its refusal of Bash commands a message pointing to the PowerShell tool or the `sandboxuser` backend; Windows only.
  - 🔴 Made Windows sandbox installation report that there's nothing to set up when `sandbox.windows.backend` is `mxc`.
  - 🔴 Added the MXC container backend to the Windows `/sandbox` status when `sandbox.windows.backend` picks it, with the result of its last start, and a warning when that setting asks for MXC but the sandbox account backend runs instead; the Windows sandbox is behind the `tengu_nankeen_kestrel` flag, which is off by default.
  - 🔴 Added an internal, Windows-only `sandbox.windows.backend` setting that can run sandboxed commands in an experimental Microsoft eXecution Container (`mxc`) instead of the dedicated sandbox account (`sandboxuser`, the default); with `mxc` only the PowerShell tool is enabled and Bash commands are refused, and project settings can't choose it; on Linux it has no effect.
  - 🔴 Added the start path for the Windows sandbox's MXC backend, chosen with `sandbox.windows.backend` set to `mxc`: it skips the sandbox-account dependency check, ignores `tlsTerminate` with a logged note, and refuses to start with a `backend_unsupported` error when the build doesn't include the MXC SDK.
  - 🔴 Added an experimental Windows sandbox backend, chosen with `sandbox.windows.backend` set to `mxc`, that runs sandboxed PowerShell commands in a Microsoft eXecution Container; Bash commands and skills that need bash are refused on it, and the sandbox instructions tell Claude what the container can read, write and reach.
  - 🔴 Added a Windows startup error for when the sandbox's MXC backend (`sandbox.windows.backend`) withholds the Bash tool and `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_POWERSHELL_TOOL=0` also turns off the PowerShell tool, telling you to remove that variable or switch the backend to `sandboxuser`.
  - 🔴 Added the Windows sandbox backend to `claude sandbox status` and let `claude sandbox install` report when the Windows backend in use can't be installed that way; both paths are Windows-only and never run in this Linux build.
  - 🔴 Made `/sandbox install` on Windows explain that the MXC backend needs no setup instead of launching the installer, on the install path enabled by `tengu_nankeen_kestrel`, which defaults off, or `CLAUDE_CODE_NANKEEN_KESTREL`.
- macOS sandbox path checks
  - 🔴 Added a macOS check that refuses a path whose real location is behind a link at or under a protected folder (`tengu_polished_hammock`, on by default), and resolved such paths before the sandbox check (the new `tengu_flickering_alpaca` flag, on by default); macOS only.
  - 🔴 Replaced the base64-encoded command text in the macOS sandbox profile's log tag for sandboxed Bash commands with a random per-command ID, when the `tengu_glittery_frost` flag is on, as it is by default.
  - 🟢 Extended the sandbox's planted-Git-repository checks on macOS to the `/private` spelling of the working folder and added folders, such as `/private/tmp` for `/tmp`; behind the `bot_protected_tengu_curious_rose` flag, on by default. (behind `tengu_tender_rivest` and `tengu_russet_hawthorn` and `bot_protected_tengu_curious_rose` and `bot_protected_tengu_steady_lantern` and `bot_protected_tengu_concurrent_creek` and `tengu_stalwart_quince`, on by default)
- 🔴 Added groundwork in `/insights` for leaving out sessions from other organizations (an `organization_mark` on cached session facets, a left-out count, and hiding the facets folder when anything was left out), but this build's organization check accepts every session, so nothing is left out.
- 🔴 Allowed multiline prompts and stop conditions in the `/loops` creation dialog; in the recurring form Up and Down can return to the interval from the prompt field.
- 🔴 Changed `/loops` to explain that an interval it couldn't read should look like `10m`.
- 🔴 Counted the final app-bundle file checks toward the macOS installer's 30-minute check, where the installer used to check elapsed time before reading those files.
- 🔴 Added a spinner to the Windows `/sandbox` dependency check for the older Windows backend while it reports `Checking Windows sandbox status…`; Linux can't take this path.
- 🟢 Changed Windows shell snapshots to run the login-shell PATH probe alongside snapshot creation and append its PATH to the snapshot afterward, instead of waiting for the probe first, unless the new `tengu_velvet_tollgate` flag is off (it's on by default).
